# Line-level findings for chapters 03-46

Appendix to n0024. Raw per-chapter findings from the t0028 review: seven editorial
reviewers (one per chapter range) and one rendered-surface reviewer. Line numbers refer
to the locale files at commit `35faa73`. Findings are reviewer output; only the items
quoted in n0024 were spot-checked against the omp source. Verify each one before you
apply it, and re-locate lines after any edit.

Key: **P** prose (quote → problem → rewrite), **B** bluff (quote → reason → action),
**I** interactive block (editorial review), **R** rendered block (✓ adds understanding,
✗ does not; size at 1280 px / 390 px).

## 03 코딩 에이전트와 OMP

- **Verdict:** keep. The catalogue's overview chapter. Its trace is the anchor example for 04–08.
- **Overall:** The four-request read→edit→run trace (L64-85) and the paragraph after it (L88) meet the bar set by ch01–02. The weak spots: the task setup is split across two sections, and the user request is worded four different ways (L22, L35, L67; ch06 adds more). One `read` call fetches two files, which contradicts the single-`path` schema shown in ch07. Section 3 covers ground that ch04 owns. The two package-map sections list packages for later chapters instead of mapping them onto the trace. Every fact checked out at the snapshot; at HEAD only one README line changed.
- **Organization:** Section 1 mixes three things: the definition, the task setup, and an OMP class-wiring paragraph (L44) that names `createAgentSession`, `Agent` and `AgentSession` before the reader needs them. Section 2 then adds more setup (the check script, L62). Proposed layout. Section 1: definition plus the complete setup (code, the check script with expected `50`/`90`, one request). Section 2: the trace, L88, and one paragraph merged from L44 and L92 that names who acts at each step. Section 3: cut to one paragraph that points to ch04. Sections 4 and 5: merge into one section on packages (see crossChapter).
- **Ordering:** L44 introduces `createAgentSession`, `Agent`, `AgentSession` and "코어 루프" before the trace, but nothing uses them until section 3. Move it after the trace. The check-script setup (L62) arrives after the request has already said "확인해 줘" (L22, L35), so readers don't know what "확인" means until section 2. L148 promises "비교합니다", but no comparison follows; the reader has to diff the Pi and OMP lists themselves.
- **Missing/stale:** Verified at 3b3a6dc9: createAgentSession→Agent→AgentSession wiring; ReadTool/EditTool (native `EditSession`)/BashTool resolve paths against the session cwd; persistence at `message_end` (session-manager.ts JSDoc says so verbatim); AgentSession's internal subscription; EventController as a separate subscriber; print-mode JSON events; all 13 packages exist at both the snapshot and HEAD.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: README.md:22 now reads "Built by Stencil Labs · Fork of Pi by @mariozechner". The claim still holds. Cited line anchors have drifted (createAgentSession is at sdk.ts ~L1640).
- **Missing/stale:** Missing continuity: the same `filter(Boolean)` bug was ch01's response A. One clause would make the link explicit.

- P `coding-agents-and-omp.ts:92` 「모델이 생성한 경로나 명령 문자열 등의 텍스트는 이 도구들을 통해 실제 작업으로 이루어집니다.」 — Uses the exact collocation the user already rejected ("실제 작업으로 이루어집니다"). "실제 OMP의" earlier in the paragraph is filler. ⇒ 모델은 경로와 명령 문자열을 만들 뿐이고, 파일을 읽고 고치고 명령을 실행하는 일은 이 도구들이 합니다.
- P `coding-agents-and-omp.ts:62` 「`check-average.ts`는 `average.ts`가 제대로 동작하는지 두 테스트 케이스 … 검사하는 프로젝트의 기존 확인 스크립트입니다.」 — Noun stack, and "제대로 동작하는지" is vague. The expected results only appear inside the diagram (lesson: introduce an example by its expected results). ⇒ 프로젝트에는 확인 스크립트 `check-average.ts`가 이미 있습니다. 이 스크립트는 `average([0, 100])`이 `50`, `average([80, 100])`이 `90`인지 검사하고, 둘 다 맞으면 종료 코드 `0`으로 끝납니다.
- P `coding-agents-and-omp.ts:22` 「`[0, 100]`의 요구 결과는 `50`이지만」 — "요구 결과" is unnatural; ch01 says the same thing more naturally. ⇒ `[0, 100]`의 평균은 `50`이어야 하지만, 지금 함수는 `filter(Boolean)`이 `0`을 걸러 내서 `100`을 반환합니다.
- P `coding-agents-and-omp.ts:18` 「하네스는 이를 작업 공간에 적용한 뒤」 — Blurs who executes: the harness dispatches, and the tool changes the files. ⇒ 하네스는 그 호출을 해당 도구에 넘겨 실행하고, 결과를 다음 모델 요청에 넣습니다.
- P `coding-agents-and-omp.ts:111` 「…이어 쓸 수 있어야 합니다. 이를 위해 실행 상태, 영속 기록, 화면 표시를 나누고」 — Framed as a requirement, and the abstract noun list hides the actors. ⇒ OMP는 응답이 생성되는 동안 화면을 갱신하고, 끝난 메시지는 파일에 저장해 나중에 대화를 이어 갈 수 있게 합니다. 두 일은 서로 다른 객체가 맡고, 모두 같은 이벤트를 받아 움직입니다.

- P `coding-agents-and-omp.ts:178` 「OMP에서 확장된 실행 환경」 — The section is about packages, not an execution environment. ⇒ Pi와 OMP의 패키지 구성 (두 절을 합칠 때의 제목)
- P `coding-agents-and-omp.ts:200` 「질문이 속한 경계부터 찾는 편이 빠릅니다」 — "경계" is used abstractly. ⇒ 질문에 따라 먼저 열어 볼 파일이 다릅니다.
- B `coding-agents-and-omp.ts:161` 「Pi README의 전체 패키지 목록에는 `packages/chord`와 `packages/telemetry`도 있습니다.」 — Name-dropping: true but never used, and irrelevant to the chapter's question. ⇒ delete

- B `coding-agents-and-omp.ts:182` 「여기에 모델 목록, 네이티브 연산, 메모리, 컨텍스트 압축 등을 담당하는 지원 패키지가 연결됩니다.」 — Restates the diagram right below it. ⇒ delete

## 04 세션 런타임

- **Verdict:** keep. No other chapter owns this API surface: the createAgentSession result, the prompt contract, subscriptions, and abort/dispose. After the overlaps are removed it still has enough distinct mechanism for a chapter.
- **Overall:** Accurate at both the snapshot and HEAD; every API claim and the SDK sample were verified. The chapter's own value is the `prompt()` return contract, `isTerminal`, `waitForIdle`, abort vs dispose, and the SDK sample. About a third of the text duplicates other chapters (tool lists → ch07, transform chain → ch06, persistence tree → ch24, event fan-out → ch03). Several terms appear before they are defined (`steer`/`followUp`/`aside`, `xd://`). The vague nouns "후속 처리" and "컨텍스트 정리" hide real mechanisms: auto-compaction, retry, advisor events and post-prompt recovery.
- **Organization:** Section 2 (L51) mixes three questions. (1) Live vs persisted state (L55-66). (2) The active model and tools (subheading L69, L73-77). (3) The persistence tree (L81). Move L77 to ch07, leaving one sentence. Move L81 to ch24. Fold model restoration into section 1, since it happens during construction. In section 3, L127 belongs in ch06; keep only what the session supplies (the image→text fallback in `convertToLlm`). In section 4, the EventController batching in L169 belongs in ch38. In section 5, abort overlaps ch06 L107-108. Add ch01/02-style group markers: 세션 만들기 (sections 1–2) / 실행 중 다루기 (sections 3–5).
- **Ordering:** L131 uses `steer`/`followUp`/`aside` before ch06 L105 defines steer/followUp. `aside` is never defined anywhere in 03–08. L77 uses `xd://`, which is first explained in ch07 L119. L105 mentions extension commands, custom commands and prompt templates (ch30/31) with no gloss. L165 uses "자동 컨텍스트 정리" without the term compaction (ch25). Moving ch06 ahead of ch04 removes most of these forward references.
- **Missing/stale:** Verified, no errors: the CreateAgentSessionResult fields, model restore order and `modelFallbackMessage`, getActive/Enabled/MountedXdevToolNames, prompt() returning false/true, AgentBusyError, `isTerminal`, waitForIdle, everything abort cancels, the shared promise from repeated dispose calls, and the SDK sample.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: line drift only (createAgentSession → sdk.ts ~L1640; abort → agent-session.ts ~L9211; dispose ~L5253).
- **Missing/stale:** The extra session events are reduced to "자동 컨텍스트 정리, 재시도, 모델 변경 등". Name them (auto_compaction_start/end, auto_retry_start/end, retry_fallback_*, model_changed in session/agent-session-events.ts), because subscribers have to handle them.
- **Missing/stale:** Changing the model or thinking level mid-session is in this chapter's "active model" scope (AgentState has `thinkingLevel`) but is not covered. [INFERENCE on the setter API names]

- P `session-runtime.ts:9` 「한 작업의 실행 수명을 관리하는 객체.」 — "실행 수명" is a calque of "lifecycle". ⇒ 모델·도구·대화 기록을 묶고, 대화를 시작부터 종료까지 관리하는 객체.
- P `session-runtime.ts:17` 「세션은 한 작업 대화의 모델·도구·환경·기록을 연결하는 실행 단위입니다.」 — Genitive/noun stack, and "실행 단위" is abstract. ⇒ 세션은 한 대화에서 쓰는 모델, 도구, 작업 디렉터리, 대화 기록을 하나로 묶은 객체입니다.
- P `session-runtime.ts:55,121,165,204` 「후속 처리」 — Used 4× and never defined. In source, `waitForIdle` waits for `agent.waitForIdle()`, then advisor card events, then post-prompt recovery; `abort` also cancels retry and compaction. ⇒ L165: `waitForIdle()`은 코어 실행이 끝난 뒤 세션이 이어서 하는 작업(자동 압축, 재시도 등)까지 모두 끝날 때까지 기다립니다.
- P `session-runtime.ts:109,165,223` 「컨텍스트 정리」 — Inconsistent with ch03 L182 "컨텍스트 압축" and with ch25's title. ⇒ 컨텍스트 압축(compaction)
- P `session-runtime.ts:148` 「응답을 기다리면서 진행을 받기」 — "진행을 받기" is an unnatural collocation. ⇒ 이벤트로 진행 상황 받기
- P `session-runtime.ts:187` 「작업을 멈추는 것과 세션을 닫는 것」 — Paired "~하는 것" construction. ⇒ 작업 중단과 세션 종료
- P `session-runtime.ts:131` 「이때의 `true`는 입력을 큐에 넣었다는 뜻이지, 그 입력에 대한 답변이 완성됐다는 뜻이 아닙니다.」 — Negative framing. ⇒ 이때 `true`는 입력이 큐에 들어갔다는 뜻입니다. 그 입력의 답변은 이벤트로 받습니다.
- P `session-runtime.ts:13` 「세션을 조립하는 팩터리」 — Leads with jargon. ⇒ `createAgentSession`으로 세션 만들기
- B `session-runtime.ts:191` 「정상 완료, 작업 중단, 세션 폐기는 서로 다른 수명 경계입니다.」 — Abstract; restates the two sentences before it. ⇒ delete
- B `session-runtime.ts:34` 「반환값에는 … 도구 UI 연결 함수인 `setToolUIContext`, 공유 `eventBus` 등이 들어 있습니다.」 — Restates the exchange and drops names that lead to no decision. ⇒ Keep only: 반환값의 `session`을 쓰고, 저장된 모델을 복원하지 못하면 `modelFallbackMessage`를 확인합니다.
- B `session-runtime.ts:73` 「인증 정보는 모델 레지스트리가 요청할 때마다 확인해 전달합니다.」 — Tangent; belongs to ch23. ⇒ delete
- B `session-runtime.ts:169` 「터미널 UI의 `EventController`는 잦은 `message_update`를 모아 …」 — A TUI detail (ch38), also already cited in ch03. ⇒ Keep sentence 1 and delete the rest.
- B `session-runtime.ts:81` 「기본 파일 저장에서 기록 항목은 `id`와 `parentId`로 트리를 만들고 …」 — ch24's scope (session storage and resume). ⇒ delete or reduce to one clause
- B `session-runtime.ts:77` 「현재 모델이 직접 볼 수 있는 도구를 표시하려면 … 활성 목록을 사용해야 합니다.」 — Duplicates ch07 L106-123. ⇒ Move to ch07 and leave a one-sentence pointer.
- I exchange L24-31 (createAgentSession → AgentSession/Agent/SessionManager) — A factory producing three objects is a fair fan-out. The output texts are overloaded, and L34 repeats them. ⇒ Reduce each output to its fields plus one verb, and delete L34.
- I exchange L58-66 (snapshot taken during the edit) — Duplicates ch03 L113-121. ⇒ Keep one fan-out diagram, here, and have ch03 link to it.
- I tool-sequence L112-123 — It does show a useful relationship: events reach the subscriber before `prompt()` resolves, and the return value `true` carries no answer. But the prompt's `@average.ts` mention has no visible effect, because no step reads the file. L121's detail "필요한 후속 처리를 이어서 정리" is vague. ⇒ Add a self-event on AgentSession (the renderer supports from===to): 파일 멘션 → `average.ts` 내용을 메시지로 추가. Or drop the mention from the prompt. Merge L121 into L122.

The boundary that matters, "abort does not undo applied changes" (L223), is never shown. ⇒ Replace tab 1 with "편집 적용 후 중단" (file stays changed, edit result kept), so the two tabs contrast stopping before vs after the edit. ## 05 프로젝트 지침과 프롬프트 구성

- **Verdict:** keep. A distinct pipeline: discovery providers, dedupe, rules/TTSR, system-prompt assembly, overrides, and caching.
- **Overall:** Technically precise: every discovery, dedupe, @import, rule, TTSR, deadline and cache claim was verified at the snapshot. It reads like a code walkthrough. The enumerations are dense (walk-up rules L16, rule pipeline L50), no concrete rule file is ever shown, and TTSR is used before it is defined. The catalogue's key ask, tracing one instruction into the outgoing request, is a 4-step diagram that packs the actual transformations into one arrow. Negative disclaimers about truncation and conflicts repeat without stating the actual guidance, even though the prompt template has one ("deeper rules override higher ones").
- **Organization:** The trace (section 4) comes after the rules section, so the AGENTS.md sentence from L15 disappears for two sections. Reorder: discovery → selection/dedupe → assembly trace → rules & TTSR → dynamic additions & caching. L36 (`<dir-context>` lists subdirectory files) answers "which files become instructions"; move it to section 1. Put SYSTEM.md (L78) with APPEND_SYSTEM.md under a subheading on overrides. TTSR's interrupt modes (L57) depend on streaming; keep the timing distinction here and move the interrupt detail to ch06 or a subheading.
- **Ordering:** "TTSR" appears at L50-54, is only explained at L57, and is never spelled out. `rule://` is used at L53 before internal URLs are introduced (ch27). The exchange at L51 uses metadata keys without ever showing a rule file; add a frontmatter example first. "공급자" at L16 is never glossed as a discovery provider.

- **Missing/stale:** Missing (snapshot): APPEND_SYSTEM.md, which appends instead of replacing (project beats user; `composeAppendPrompt` in packages/coding-agent/src/system-prompt.ts). It is the natural sibling of SYSTEM.md.

- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD: rules with a `question` field also go to TTSR (packages/coding-agent/src/capability/rule-buckets.ts), so L50's "`condition` 또는 `astCondition`" is incomplete.

## 06 에이전트 루프

- **Verdict:** keep. packages/agent is the core mechanism, and this chapter has more distinct mechanism than any other in 03–08.
- **Overall:** The densest of the core chapters, and mostly well chosen. Its strongest parts: the execution-path separating validation errors from implementation errors (L39), the scheduling barrier diagram (L73) and its consequence paragraph (L75), and the steer-vs-abort consequence (L107). The opening is weak: its tool-sequence re-traces half of ch03 and stops at the edit call, and L24 duplicates ch04. The continuation and interrupt rules are long prose lists that would read better as interactive comparisons. Accurate at the snapshot; the line anchors have drifted. It omits `afterToolCall`, the soft-tool-requirement escalation, and IRC interrupts.
- **Organization:** Each section answers its own question: one round trip / how a call is assembled and validated / scheduling / continuation / interruption. L90 (toolResult shape; streamed updates vs the final result) belongs with results in stream-and-dispatch, not in continuation. L76's provider limiter is a tangent. Consider moving continuation to second place: the opener ends mid-loop, so the reader's next question is when the loop stops.
- **Ordering:** Tool properties `openArgStream`, `lenientArgValidation`, `concurrency` and `interruptible` are used here, but ch07 doesn't list them among the tool fields. "호출 전 게이트" (L92) is never defined. steer/followUp are defined here after ch04 has already used them.
- **Missing/stale:** Verified at the snapshot: toolcall streaming and openArgStream, retainCompletedToolCalls, transformAssistantMessage, validate→beforeToolCall→revalidate, lenientArgValidation, scheduler semantics with `shared` as default, `edit` exclusive, `bash` exclusive only with pty, a throwing concurrency function → exclusive, the provider limiter, stopReason handling, synthetic results and re-call on `length`, the pause_turn cap of 8 (MAX_PAUSED_TURN_CONTINUATIONS), queue defaults one-at-a-time, `interruptMode` defaulting to immediate, interruptible/steeringSignal, and queues left untouched on external abort.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: anchors have drifted (scheduler → packages/agent/src/agent-loop.ts ~L3780-3796; error/aborted handling ~L1520-1544).
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD, new: tool-call IDs are now repaired to be unique for the whole run (`ensureUniqueToolCallIds`), which bears on L76's claim that results are matched by toolCallId.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing (snapshot): the `afterToolCall` hook, the counterpart to `beforeToolCall` (packages/agent/src/agent-loop.ts).
- **Missing/stale:** Missing (snapshot): when a soft-required tool isn't called, the loop forces `toolChoice` to it on the next turn and gives up after `MAX_SOFT_TOOL_ESCALATIONS = 3`. This is a continuation branch the chapter doesn't cover.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing (snapshot): IRC peer messages are a third interrupt source (`ircAbortController`). At least point to ch37.
- **Missing/stale:** Not needed: Harmony/DSML leak recovery (provider-specific).

- P `agent-loop.ts:15-16` 「“평균 계산에서 0점도 포함하도록 고치고 테스트해 줘” / “`average`가 `[0, 100]`에서 `100`을 반환하는 버그를 고쳐 줘.”」 — Two different requests in one section, and ch03 uses check-average.ts, not "테스트". ⇒ Reuse ch03's request word for word: `average.ts`에서 0점도 평균에 포함되게 고치고 `check-average.ts`로 확인해 줘.
- P `agent-loop.ts:39` 「같은 편집 의도, 다른 실행 경계」 — "경계" is used abstractly. ⇒ 같은 편집 호출, 세 가지 결과
- P `agent-loop.ts:91` 「실행할 도구 호출이 남아 있으면 … 도구가 실패하면 대개 오류 결과를 모델에 보여 주고」 — "남아 있으면" is the wrong verb; "대개" hedges without naming the exception. ⇒ 응답에 도구 호출이 있으면 … 도구가 실패해도 루프는 멈추지 않고 오류 결과를 다음 요청에 넣습니다.
- P `agent-loop.ts:92` 「모델의 텍스트 생성이 끝난 시점과 전체 작업이 끝난 시점을 구분해야 합니다.」 — Generic closer. ⇒ 루프가 끝났는지는 `agent_end`로 판단합니다.
- P `agent-loop.ts:103` 「새 입력과 취소는 같은 신호가 아니다」 — Negation title. ⇒ 실행 중에 끼어들기: steer, followUp, abort
- P `agent-loop.ts:106` 「기본 `interruptMode: "immediate"`에서도 이미 생성된 호출을 모두 폐기하지는 않습니다.」 — Leads with a negative. ⇒ 기본값 `interruptMode: "immediate"`에서 새 입력이 오면, 루프는 `interruptible`로 표시된 도구만 중단합니다. (name one interruptible tool) [INFERENCE: which tools]
- P `agent-loop.ts:70` 「한 응답 안의 호출은 어떻게 배치되는가」 — "배치" is ambiguous (batch vs arrangement). ⇒ 한 응답 안의 여러 호출: 동시 실행과 순차 실행
- B `agent-loop.ts:15` 「에이전트 루프는 모델 요청 → 도구 실행 → 결과 추가를 반복하며, 각 도구의 결과를 다음 모델 요청에 전달합니다.」 — Restates the description and the diagram title. ⇒ delete
- B `agent-loop.ts:24` 「하네스가 입력과 실행 가능한 도구를 준비하면 모델은 그 안에서 다음 행동을 선택합니다.」 — Filler. The chain itself duplicates ch04 L127. ⇒ Delete the last sentence. Keep the chain here, since ch06 is its home, and remove it from ch04.
- B `agent-loop.ts:36` 「인자 스트림 처리와 도구 실행은 별도 단계입니다.」 — Restates the preceding sentence. ⇒ delete
- B `agent-loop.ts:72` 「`shared` 호출은 앞선 배타 호출이 끝난 뒤 …」 — Walks through the code diagram at L73 label by label. ⇒ Cut down to the definitions of shared/exclusive and the default.
- B `agent-loop.ts:76` 「이 스케줄러는 … 제공자 동시성 제어와 별개입니다. `coding-agent`는 응답 스트림 함수를 제공자 동시성 제한기로 감쌉니다.」 — Tangent (belongs to ch20). ⇒ delete both sentences
- B `agent-loop.ts:39` 「일반 스키마 검증을 사용하는 경로를 비교합니다.」 — Meta sentence inside the input box. ⇒ delete
- I tool-sequence L16-23 — Half of ch03's sequence, ending at the edit call: no consequence and nothing new. ⇒ Replace with a view specific to the loop. Either zoom into one turn (stream → toolcall_end → validate → beforeToolCall → execute → toolResult → stopReason check), or show the final turn: no tool call → followUp check → agent_end. Otherwise delete it and point to ch03.
- I execution-path L39-67 — Strong. The three cases share stages, diverge at a visible point, and each changes what goes into the next request. ⇒ Keep; only remove the meta sentence from the input box.
- I code L73 (barrier diagram) — An effective static diagram. ⇒ Keep.
- I (missing) steer/abort timing, L105-108 — The behaviour depends on timing, which prose explains poorly. L107's key consequence (an edit already in the batch still runs) is never shown. ⇒ Add an execution-path with the setup "편집이 이미 배치에 들어간 뒤" and tabs `steer: 편집하지 마` / `abort` / `steer while an interruptible tool runs`, showing which calls run, which are skipped, and what stays in the queue.
- I (missing) continuation rules, L91-92 — Six stop/continue conditions written as prose. ⇒ Add a small table, stopReason → loop action: toolUse/stop with calls → execute and call again; stop without calls → followUp or agent_end; length → synthetic results and call again; pause_turn → continue up to 8 times; error/aborted → end.
- R [tool-sequence ✓] TS L16 도구 결과가 다음 모델 요청의 입력이 된다 (6 steps; 1048px / 950px) — It stops at the `edit` call, with no result, check or answer. This is the loop chapter, yet ch03 shows the longer trace. ⇒ Make this the canonical full loop (read → edit → test → answer). Label each request by ordinal and say what it adds (n0023 pattern). Shrink ch03's version.
- R [execution-path ✓] EP L39 같은 편집 의도, 다른 실행 경계 (3 tabs × 4 stages; 720px / 913px; tabs wrap to 2 rows at 390) — Meets the bar: one call, three outcomes, and the blocked stage is visible. The title uses the abstract noun '실행 경계', and the input label '읽기는 끝난 상태' is noun-heavy. ⇒ Retitle '같은 `edit` 호출의 세 가지 결과'. Change the input label to '주어진 상황'.

## 07 도구 정의와 레지스트리

- **Verdict:** keep. The tool object contract, building the registry from factories, the exposure sets and ToolSession are distinct mechanisms. ch04 should hand its tool-list paragraph over to this chapter.
- **Overall:** Accurate: the createTools excerpt matches the snapshot verbatim, and every accessor's meaning was verified. The chapter's real contributions are the registered/enabled/active distinction and the ToolSession point (the same path can read a different file). Both are shown as exchange blocks that list fields instead of showing the consequence. The opening re-defines name/description/parameters from ch02. The final trace re-runs ch02's package.json example with the OMP internals packed into one arrow. ch04 already covered the three tool lists.
- **Organization:** Sections 1–4 each answer one question: what a tool object is / how a session gets its tools / which tools the model sees / what environment a tool runs in. Section 5 (the trace) duplicates ch02's sequence and ch06's validation. Its only new part is the schema-normalization subheading (L210-214), which belongs in section 1 next to the model-facing definition. Delete the section-5 tool-sequence, move L214, and keep a two-line dispatch summary.
- **Ordering:** "Code Mode 브리지", "Eval 브리지" and `getToolForEvalBridge()` (L109, L119) appear without setup; they belong to ch14. ch04 L77 used `xd://` before this chapter explains it (L119).
- **Missing/stale:** Verified at the snapshot: AgentTool fields, the ReadTool schema string, OpenAI conversion, the createTools excerpt (verbatim), the bash.enabled check, conditional factories, the eval probe, auto-included tools, merging extension tools into the registry (same name replaces), the wrapper on every tool, the accessors, ToolSession services, reading the editor buffer first, execute arguments, fallback lookup, the intent field `i`, and description pruning.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD: the ReadTool schema description is now "Local path, internal URI, or URL; selectors inline." (packages/coding-agent/src/tools/read.ts:692-694). The code block at L36-41 quotes the snapshot text; update it or say it's from the snapshot in the caption.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: the intent field `i` now has a 200-character limit (`MAX_INTENT_LENGTH`, packages/agent/src/agent-loop.ts). Anchors have drifted (BUILTIN_TOOLS ~L563; session-tools accessors ~L576-737).
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: the per-tool scheduling and interrupt properties (`concurrency`, `interruptible`, `lenientArgValidation`, `openArgStream`) that ch06 relies on are part of the tool contract but are absent from the field list.

- P `tool-definitions-and-registry.ts:44` 「모델이 `path` 값을 생성하면 하네스는 같은 이름으로 등록된 실행 함수를 찾습니다.」 — Logic slip: the lookup is by tool name and is triggered by the call, not by `path`. ⇒ 모델이 `read` 호출을 생성하면 하네스는 `read`라는 이름으로 등록된 실행 함수를 찾습니다.
- P `tool-definitions-and-registry.ts:83` 「팩터리에 등록된 도구가 모든 세션에 만들어지는 것은 아닙니다.」 — "~것은 아닙니다" negative opener. ⇒ 세션에 실제로 만들어지는 도구는 설정과 실행 환경에 따라 달라집니다.
- P `tool-definitions-and-registry.ts:106` 「OMP는 도구 상태를 세 가지로 구분합니다.」 — "상태" for what are actually sets of tools. ⇒ OMP는 도구 목록을 세 겹으로 나눕니다.
- P `tool-definitions-and-registry.ts:123` 「`setActiveToolsByName()`은 새 구현을 만드는 함수가 아니라 …」 — Leads with a negative. ⇒ `setActiveToolsByName()`은 레지스트리에 이미 있는 도구 중에서 사용할 도구를 고르고 노출 방식을 다시 적용합니다.
- P `tool-definitions-and-registry.ts:210` 「모델용 스키마에는 실행 전처리가 반영된다」 — "실행 전처리" is opaque. ⇒ 모델이 받는 스키마는 원본과 다를 수 있다
- P `tool-definitions-and-registry.ts:102` 「등록된 도구와 지금 노출된 도구」 — The section covers three sets, and the title nearly repeats ch04 L69. ⇒ 등록·활성화·노출: 세 가지 도구 목록
- B `tool-definitions-and-registry.ts:17` 「`name`은 호출할 구현을 찾는 키이고, `description`은 기능과 사용법을 설명하며, `parameters`는 인자의 구조를 정합니다.」 — Repeats ch02 L246. ⇒ Delete; keep the OMP-specific point that one object carries both the model-facing fields and the execution fields.

"도구 표시 방식" also overlaps the UI output. ⇒ Move `loadMode` to the model-facing output. Consider adding `concurrency`/`interruptible` to the execution output (ch06 uses them).

- I tool-sequence L190-202 — A near copy of ch02's sequence: same file, same question, same answer. The new OMP-specific steps are packed into L197's detail. ⇒ Delete, or make the actors 에이전트 루프 / ExtensionToolWrapper / ReadTool so the dispatch layers become the visible steps.

The chapter's subject (registry lookup → validation → wrapper) is crammed into step 3's detail. ⇒ Drop the model request/answer framing already in ch02. Use harness steps instead: 이름으로 레지스트리 조회 → 스키마 검증 → 실행 래퍼 → `ReadTool.execute` → toolResult.

## 08 도구 권한

- **Verdict:** keep. Catalogue scope (approval/interception policies and isolation limits). Move the niche parts out: provider safety → ch45, interceptor internals → ch11, settings-merge mechanics → ch29.
- **Overall:** Thorough and accurate: the error strings, precedence order, ACP cache keys and read-path handling were all verified. The denied-invocation execution-path (L57) delivers the catalogue's trace well. It is the longest and densest chapter in 03–08: 6 sections and 7 subheadings, 15 negative qualifications, 16 uses of "경로", precedence rules written as run-on sentences, and several niche gates that belong elsewhere (provider safety checks, interceptor regex details, settings-merge mechanics, the hook dedupe marker). The default `yolo`, under which nothing prompts, is buried at L34. It omits Plan Mode's read-only gate, an independent permission layer present at both the snapshot and HEAD.
- **Organization:** Add ch01/02-style group markers. 승인 결정: approval-policy, denied-invocation, matching-and-precedence. 다른 차단 지점: extension hooks, interceptor, ACP, plus a new plan-mode note. 승인이 다루지 않는 것: trust-and-isolation. Open section 1 with the default `yolo` fact (now L34), since it is the most consequential setting for a reader. xd:// approval inheritance is explained three times (L163, L270, L311); keep it in the precedence section only. For L181, keep only the security consequence here (project files override global policy) and move the merge mechanics to ch29.
- **Ordering:** ACP (L244) has no gloss (ch39). "컴퓨터 사용 호출" (L270) is undefined (ch45). `tool_call` hooks (L199) come before extensions are introduced (ch31). `override` (L159) is used without introduction. The subagent worktree isolation (L328) relies on ch36.
- **Missing/stale:** Verified at the snapshot: capability tiers with exec as default, ReadTool returning exec for ssh://, the mode table, the default yolo with `tools.approval: {}`, the wrapper on every tool, both error strings verbatim, start event emitted before the gate, prompt without UI → error, bash.patterns normalization/wildcard/first-match, compound-command rules, resolveApproval precedence, autoApprove→yolo, policyKey, merge order with arrays replaced, hook ordering and timeouts, the interceptor, ACP gate tools/keys/reset/skip condition, pendingSafetyChecks, read path handling, bypassing the wrapper via BUILTIN_TOOLS, ctx.invokeTool, worktree isolation.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing (snapshot and HEAD): Plan Mode's independent write gate. While plan mode is active, edit/write to the working tree are rejected ("Plan mode: the working tree is read-only…"), renames and deletes are rejected, and only the `local://` sandbox stays writable, whatever approvalMode says (packages/coding-agent/src/tools/plan-mode-guard.ts, enforced from packages/coding-agent/src/edit/index.ts). Without it readers will conclude that yolo approves every call. Add one paragraph linking to ch33.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD: packages/coding-agent/src/config/settings-schema.ts no longer exists. Approval defaults now live in packages/coding-agent/src/tools/settings.ts and interceptor settings in packages/coding-agent/src/exec/settings.ts. The links at L40 and L230 point to a path removed at HEAD (the snapshot permalinks still work). Default values are unchanged.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: approval resolution moved into `resolveApprovalFromContext()` (approval.ts), which falls back to `always-ask` when settings/context are absent. Also new: renamed tools (legacyName) inherit deny/prompt, and settings gained an overlay-parent layer for child sessions.
- **Missing/stale:** L110 says real execution happens at the wrapper's `this.tool.execute(...)`, but gives an event consumer nothing observable. State which event shows the outcome (the tool_execution_end result with `isError` and the denial text). [INFERENCE: check that the end event carries the result]

- P `tool-permissions.ts:100` 「설정의 거부 항목을 설명하는 문구가 함께 반환된다.」 — 해라체 inside a 합쇼체 chapter. ⇒ 설정의 거부 항목을 설명하는 문구도 함께 반환합니다.
- P `tool-permissions.ts:159` 「이 함수는 도구가 반환한 `deny`, 사용자 설정의 `deny`, … 승인 모드에 따른 등급 비교 순으로 판단합니다. `yolo`는 … 정책이 없는 `override`의 강제 확인을 생략하지만」 — A five-step precedence packed into one sentence, and `override` is never introduced. ⇒ Render as a numbered list (1. 도구가 반환한 deny 2. 사용자 설정 deny 3. 도구의 명시적 정책·강제 확인 4. 나머지 사용자 설정 5. 모드와 등급 비교), and gloss override as "도구가 요구하는 강제 확인".
- P `tool-permissions.ts:252` 「설정 스키마의 기본값인 `yolo`만으로 ACP 게이트가 생략되지는 않습니다.」 — An "X만으로 ~지는 않습니다" negative. ⇒ ACP 게이트는 `autoApprove`나 사용자가 직접 설정한 `tools.approvalMode: yolo`가 있고 해당 도구의 정책이 없거나 `allow`일 때만 건너뜁니다. 기본값으로 들어간 `yolo`는 여기에 해당하지 않습니다.
- P `tool-permissions.ts:290` 「작업 디렉터리도 파일 접근의 최상위 경계는 아닙니다.」 — Abstract negative; and ch02 L323 says a harness *should* check this. ⇒ `read`는 작업 디렉터리 밖의 파일도 읽습니다. 2장에서 본 작업 공간 경로 검사를 OMP의 `read`는 하지 않습니다.
- P `tool-permissions.ts:286` 「지금까지 설명한 정책은 하네스의 도구 호출 경로를 통제합니다.」 — "경로" is abstract; this word appears 16× in ch08, mostly non-literally. ⇒ 지금까지 설명한 정책은 모델이 낸 도구 호출이 실행되기 직전까지만 작동합니다.
- P `tool-permissions.ts:155` 「명령 규칙 다음에는 공통 승인 해석기가 있다」 — Awkward subheading. ⇒ 최종 판단: `resolveApproval()`의 우선순위
- P `tool-permissions.ts:177` 「어느 설정 파일을 읽었는지도 정책의 일부다」 — Vague; the point is that project settings override global ones. ⇒ 프로젝트 설정이 전역 정책을 덮어쓴다
- P `tool-permissions.ts:240` 「연결된 클라이언트의 승인은 별도 경계다」 — An abstract contrast title, and ACP is never named. ⇒ 편집기(ACP)가 요구하는 추가 승인
- B `tool-permissions.ts:34` 「`always-ask` 모드에서는 … 묻습니다. … ACP 편집기의 추가 승인은 뒤에서 별도로 설명합니다.」 — Repeats the exchange row, then adds a signpost. ⇒ Delete both sentences. Move the default-yolo sentence to the top of the section.
- B `tool-permissions.ts:106` 「사용자가 호출을 거절하면 셸 프로세스는 시작되지 않습니다. … 이 결과의 `isError`는 `true`입니다.」 — Restates the execution-path results. ⇒ Keep only the new fact: the loop turns the wrapper's exception into a `toolResult` with the original call ID.
- B `tool-permissions.ts:146` 「뒤에 있는 구체적인 거부 규칙이 앞선 일치 결과를 덮어쓰지는 않습니다.」 — Restates the previous sentence. ⇒ delete
- B `tool-permissions.ts:203` 「루프에서 이미 훅을 실행했다면 마커를 사용해 중복 호출을 피합니다.」 — Implementation trivia. ⇒ delete the last two sentences
- B `tool-permissions.ts:225` 「잘못된 정규식은 건너뜁니다. 파이프 앞부분의 출력을 …」 — Internals of a default-off steering feature that L225 itself says is not a security boundary. ⇒ Cut the interceptor subsection to two sentences; move the details to ch11.

### Range patterns (ch 03–08)

- Negative qualifications with no positive rule: 38 instances of "~지 않습니다 / ~이 아닙니다 / ~만으로" across 03–08 (ch08 15, ch05 8, ch06 7), against 9 across ch01–02. Examples: ch08 L332 "분리하는 것만으로 이러한 제한이 생기지는 않습니다", ch07 L157 "이름과 스키마만으로 … 정해지지는 않습니다", ch05 L94 "줄이는 기능이 아닙니다". Replace each with the positive rule or delete it.
- Contrast/negation section titles, about 8: "실행 능력과 실행 허가는 별개다", "새 입력과 취소는 같은 신호가 아니다", "규칙의 목록과 자동 매칭은 다르다", "작업을 멈추는 것과 세션을 닫는 것", "연결된 클라이언트의 승인은 별도 경계다", "작업 트리 분리는 호스트 접근 제한과 다르다", "호출 승인과 프로세스 격리를 구분하기", "등록된 것과 현재 사용하는 것". ch01/02 titles name the subject instead ("반복해서 읽는 입력, 프롬프트 캐싱").
- Abstract nouns used non-literally: 경로 33× (ch08 16), 경계 20×, 상태 21×. Examples: "별도 경로" (ch05 L78), "수명 경계" (ch04 L191), "도구 상태" (ch07 L106), "도구 호출 경로" (ch08 L286).
- Filler "실제": 27× in 03–08 (12 in reference labels such as "OMP · 실제 도구 execute 호출", "AgentState의 실제 필드", "실제 공통 승인 우선순위"), against 3× in ch01–02. Remove it from the labels.
- Misused exchange blocks: 03–08 contain 13 exchanges, and only 3 express a real input→outputs relation (ch03 L113 message_end fan-out, ch04 L24 factory, ch05 L51 rule variants). The rest are field lists, question→answer lists or matrices under a misleading arrow (ch03 L34/L150/L184, ch04 L155, ch07 L109/L146, ch08 L24). Note that the renderer (src/components/exchange-example.ts) is static, with one arrow from input to outputs.
- Tool-sequences that hide steps inside the harness in one detail string: ch05 L75, ch07 L197, ch04 L118. A three-actor message diagram can't show the transformation the chapter is about; use payload excerpts or change the actors.
- Closing sentences that restate the heading or diagram: ch04 L191; ch05 L36, L57; ch06 L15, L36; ch07 L143; ch08 L146, L286, L332.
- Vague umbrella nouns that are never defined: "후속 처리" (ch04, 4×), "컨텍스트 정리" (ch04, 3×; the term is compaction), "호출 전 게이트" (ch06 L92), "실행 전처리" (ch07 L210).
- Section `group` markers are unused in 03–08, although ch01/02 use them to group sections by the question they answer.
- The running example drifts: the average.ts request is worded 7 ways (ch03 L22, L35, L67; ch04 L114, L246; ch06 L15, L16), and "테스트" alternates with `check-average.ts`.
- Reference anchors: all pin 3b3a6dc9, so they resolve, but HEAD line drift is large everywhere, and ch08's settings-schema.ts no longer exists at HEAD.
- Cross-chapter: Overlap among 03/04/06. (a) The transformContext→convertToLlm→transformProviderContext chain is explained in ch04 L127 and again in ch06 L24, and the same agent-loop.ts L1593-1639 is cited by ch03 L97, ch04 L141, ch05 and ch06 L26. Its home is ch06; ch04 keeps only the image fallback the session supplies. (b) message_end persistence and event fan-out appear in ch03 L111-137 and in ch04 L58-66 and L152-169, both citing agent-session.ts L1920-1924 and event-controller.ts L585-615. Keep the fan-out diagram in ch04 and one sentence in ch03. (c) The read→edit trace appears in full in ch03 and as a truncated copy in ch06; ch06 should zoom into one turn instead. (d) "Abort doesn't undo file changes" is stated in both ch04 L223 and ch06 L107.
- Cross-chapter: ch04 L77 (the three tool lists) duplicates ch07 L106-123, and ch04's subheading at L69 is nearly identical to ch07's title at L102. Keep this in ch07 only. The validate → hook → revalidate order is described three times (ch06 L37, ch07 L205, ch08 L199): ch06 should own the order, and ch08 should add only the policy part. ch07's section-5 trace repeats ch02's file-reading sequence.
- Cross-chapter: Ordering of 04–08. The forward dependencies are: ch04 → ch06 (steer/followUp/aside, the transform chain), ch04 → ch07 (tool lists, xd://), ch05 → ch06 (TTSR interrupts streaming), and ch06 → ch07 (tool properties). Recommendation: 03 → 06 → 04 → 05 → 07 → 08. ch03's trace leads straight into the loop that runs it. ch04 then explains what the session adds around the loop, with steer/followUp already defined. ch05 follows ch04 because the system prompt is built while the session is constructed. ch07 and ch08 stay together because the approval wrapper is installed over the registry. This requires updating the catalogue and reading route in n0022.
- Cross-chapter: Pi package map in ch03: n0022 explicitly requires "Introduce the Pi and OMP package maps", so the material stays. Merge the two sections into one. Map the core four packages (same names in Pi and OMP) to the trace's actors; L165 has the right idea. State the fork lineage in one sentence. List OMP's additions briefly, with links to their chapters. Drop chord/telemetry (L161) and L196's list. Replace both exchanges with one table.
- Cross-chapter: ch02 L323 (reviewed) says a harness should check that a target path is inside the workspace. ch08 L290 shows OMP's `read` doesn't. Make the link explicit in ch08; it is the clearest harness-design consequence in the chapter.
- Cross-chapter: Terminology across chapters: model providers are called "공급자" in ch05/ch06 (L95, ch06 L24) but "제공자" in ch01–04, ch07 and ch08. Compaction is "컨텍스트 정리" in ch04 but "컨텍스트 압축" in ch03 and ch25. Pick one term for each.
- Cross-chapter: Running example: fix one canonical request and check script in ch03 and reuse them word for word in ch04 (L114, code L246) and ch06 (L15-16). Reusing ch02's pnpm/package.json examples in ch05/ch07 is good continuity, but ch07 should not re-run ch02's whole sequence.
- Cross-chapter: ch08 should point to Plan Mode (ch33) as an approval-independent write gate, and ch06 to IRC peer interrupts (ch37) as a third interrupt source.

## 09 파일 읽기와 검색

- **Verdict:** keep. Three distinct tools (glob/grep/read) with their own scoping, paging, ignore rules and output formats; read alone covers summaries, archives, SQLite, documents and URLs. Clearly separate from editing.
- **Overall:** Facts check out against the snapshot: glob returns up to 200 results, `.gitignore` and hidden files are on by default, grep shows 20 files per page with `skip`, read stops at 300 lines by default, and selectors behave as described. The teaching has three problems. (1) The example contradicts the chapter's own advice. Section 1 runs `glob` first even though the exact error string is already known, while section 2's chooser says a known identifier means start with `grep`. (2) The read behaviour that best fits the chapter's question ('필요한 원문만 문맥으로') is missing: reading a code file without a selector returns a structural summary with an elision footer. (3) Several closing sentences tell 'the harness' what it 'should' do instead of saying what OMP actually does. Section 3 mostly belongs to ch10.
- **Organization:** Sections 1 and 2 answer the same question: which tool, in which order, for a given clue. Section 1 traces glob→grep→read; section 2 then explains when glob or grep can be skipped. Merge them into one section: start with the chooser, then trace one path. Section 3 ('검색 결과는 다음 행동을 준비한다') mixes two topics. The snapshot-tag/edit-anchor material duplicates ch10 §2; the multi-format `read` paragraph belongs to a missing section on what `read` returns. Suggested grouping: (a) choose the tool by the clue you have; (b) what read returns: ranges, structural summary, other formats; (c) limits and pagination, with a one-line forward pointer to ch10 for tags.
- **Ordering:** The sequence at L35 and L39 already shows '줄 번호와 스냅샷 태그' and '새 스냅샷 태그', but tags are only explained in §3 (L120). Either drop tags from the sequence or introduce the `[PATH#TAG]` header before the trace. The decision guide (§2) should come before the trace (§1): readers need the selection rule before watching one application of it. The example at L21 states its expected result, which is good, but never says which repository it is. The glob pattern uses real OMP paths (`packages/coding-agent/src/**`), yet the string 'Unknown model alias' does not exist in OMP at 3b3a6dc9, and the exchange at L124 switches to `src/model.ts`. Pick one fictional repo and use it consistently.
- **Missing/stale:** Structural summary: reading a code file of 100+ lines with no selector returns declarations with elided spans and a `re-read needed ranges` footer (`read.summarize.*` in docs/tools/read.md at both snapshot and HEAD). This is the core mechanism for 'only the needed text' and is absent.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing-path recovery in read (`findUniqueWorkspaceSuffix` resolves a mistyped path to a unique workspace match and says so in the result).
- **Missing/stale:** grep per-file caps (20 matches per file in multi-file scopes, 200 in single-file scopes) and round-robin selection so one file cannot monopolise a page; glob's 5 s timeout returns partial results (HEAD: a timed-out scan with zero matches says the scan was incomplete and never claims absence).
- **Missing/stale:** Bash interceptor rules that route `cat/grep/rg/find` to read/grep/glob (docs/tools/bash.md), the harness decision that pushes models toward these tools. Could live here or in ch11.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: a new `find` tool (docs/tools/find.md, `packages/coding-agent/src/tools/jfind/`) does semantic search with relevance scores. It is enabled only when the `judge` role resolves to a supported model. Worth one sentence if the chapter is updated to HEAD.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: `packages/coding-agent/src/session/streaming-output.ts` moved to `packages/tui/src/tools/streaming-output.ts`; glob/grep now walk internal URLs through `InternalUrlFilesystem`.

- P `tools-and-execution.ts:17` 「하네스는 앞 단계의 결과를 다음 도구의 좁은 입력으로 바꾸어야 합니다.」 — Wrong actor. L45 itself says the model chooses the next query and range; the harness only executes. ⇒ 모델은 앞 도구의 결과를 보고 다음 도구에 넘길 범위를 좁힙니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:21` 「기대 결과는 오류 문자열을 만드는 파일과 별칭을 조회하는 함수의 본문을 확보하는 것입니다.」 — '~하는 것입니다' plus '확보' is noun-heavy translationese. ⇒ 이 오류를 던지는 줄과 별칭을 조회하는 함수 본문을 찾으면 조사가 끝납니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:45` 「빈 검색 결과도 중요한 결과입니다. 하네스는 “없다”는 텍스트뿐 아니라 어떤 디렉터리와 무시 규칙, 결과 제한으로 조사했는지를 보존해야」 — A generic importance claim followed by advice that gives no case. OMP already returns `scopePath`, skipped missing paths and a timeout notice; HEAD explicitly never reports absence on a timed-out scan. ⇒ 결과가 비어도 파일이 없다는 뜻은 아닐 수 있습니다. `gitignore`가 켜져 있으면 `dist/` 안의 파일은 검색되지 않습니다. 그래서 OMP는 검색한 범위와 건너뛴 경로, 시간 초과 여부를 결과에 함께 적습니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:98` 「비밀 파일을 모델 문맥에 넣지 않도록 하네스의 읽기 권한과 검색 루트를 별도로 제한해야 합니다.」 — Generic advice with no OMP behaviour behind it: grep hard-codes `hidden:true` and has no flag to exclude dotfiles. Either state the consequence or delete. ⇒ `grep`은 숨김 파일을 항상 검색하므로 `.env`의 줄이 결과에 섞일 수 있습니다.

The 'should' sentence describes what OMP already does (`Use skip=<N> for the next page`) as if it were advice. ⇒ 끝 줄을 지정하지 않으면 `read`는 300줄까지 읽습니다. 결과가 잘리면 OMP는 `Use skip=20 for the next page`처럼 다음 위치를 덧붙여, 모델이 조사를 이어 갈지 정하게 합니다.

The real mechanism, read's structural summary with a re-read footer, goes unnamed. ⇒ 범위를 지정하지 않고 100줄 이상의 코드 파일을 읽으면 `read`는 선언만 남긴 요약을 돌려주고, 끝에 `[…40ln elided; re-read needed ranges, e.g. src/a.ts:5-16]`처럼 생략한 범위를 적습니다. 모델은 이 범위를 다시 읽어 원문을 확인합니다.

Harness→model is drawn as `result`, with no new model request. ⇒ Cut to 6 steps: one call/result pair per tool, with the 작업 공간 hop moved into the detail text. ## 10 파일 편집

- **Verdict:** keep. A distinct component with its own contracts: write versus edit, five edit modes, the hashline grammar, snapshot store and recovery, streaming preview, and LSP writethrough.
- **Overall:** Accurate on the hashline contract: line numbers refer to the original snapshot, seen-range checks run, all sections are prepared before writing, partial writes are possible on OS errors, preview and application are separate, and LSP writethrough is cited. The examples show only the happy path, so the chapter's central mechanism (validation against the snapshot the model saw) never visibly changes an outcome. The stale-tag recovery that the chapter cites (recovery.rs) is reduced to '검증합니다'. One claim about write's result is inaccurate.
- **Organization:** §1 mixes two questions: which tool, write or edit (good), and which edit mode with which schema. The mode paragraph (L164) belongs in §2 next to hashline. §2 lacks a failure or recovery case. §3's final paragraph (L261) repeats the exchange right above it. Suggested grouping: (a) write versus edit; (b) edit modes and the hashline anchor, including validation, a rejection case and a recovery case; (c) what happens after a write (preview versus applied diff, LSP format and diagnostics, cache invalidation).
- **Ordering:** The §2 sequence spends its first four events re-reading the file, which ch09 and the §1 execution-path already covered. Start from an established given state: '모델은 이미 `[src/config.ts#7C2A]`를 읽었다'. The mode paragraph uses '태그와 줄 기준점' before §2 explains anchors; that is acceptable only because of ch09.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale-tag recovery: recovery applies only when the snapshot chain proves a unique safe result (docs/tools/edit.md). It is cited but not explained.
- **Missing/stale:** Block anchors (`PUT N*`, resolved with tree-sitter) are mentioned only as '구문 블록'. Replacing a whole function without counting lines is a distinctive part of the contract.
- **Missing/stale:** Other modes and per-model selection: `apply_patch`, `patch`, `replace` (HEAD adds `sloppy`); a model exclusion list can force `replace`.
- **Missing/stale:** The no-op guard: a byte-identical edit is rejected or diagnosed and repeated no-ops escalate. HEAD changed this so a single no-op returns a diagnostic without writing.
- **Missing/stale:** write guards: generated-file detection (both versions); at HEAD write refuses an overwrite whose content ends in a read-truncation notice and covers less than the source (prevents the model from writing a summarised read back).
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: seen-line enforcement is now behind `edit.enforceSeenLines` (default true); `MV` overwrites an existing destination.

- P `tools-and-execution.ts:152` 「모델이 본 내용에 수정 의도를 고정하고, 검증한 변경을 파일과 세션 기록에 반영하는 도구.」 — '수정 의도를 고정' is abstract translationese; the line compresses the section list into a subtitle. ⇒ 모델이 읽은 파일 내용을 기준으로 수정을 적용하는 도구.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:156` 「새 파일과 기존 파일을 다르게 다룬다」 — 한다-style title. ⇒ 새 파일은 `write`, 기존 파일은 `edit`
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:164` 「하네스는 활성 모드의 스키마와 안내문, 미리 보기를 함께 바꿔야 모델이 한 형식의 인자를 다른 실행기에 보내지 않습니다.」 — A 'should…so that not' construction for something OMP already does (resolveEditMode switches schema, prompt and renderer together). The per-model choice of mode is the interesting fact, and it is omitted. ⇒ OMP는 모드를 고르면 도구 스키마, 모델용 안내문, 미리 보기를 함께 바꿉니다. 모드는 모델별 설정, 환경 변수, `edit.mode` 순서로 정해지며, 일부 모델에는 기본값 대신 `replace`를 씁니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:204` 「본 파일을 기준으로 수정한다」 — 한다-style title; '본' is ambiguous (it can read as 'this/main'). ⇒ 읽은 스냅샷에 맞춰 수정하기
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:208` 「모델이 보지 못한 범위나 겹치는 수정, 내용이 달라진 오래된 태그는 적용 전에 검증합니다.」 — '범위를 검증한다' is the wrong collocation (they are rejected, not verified). It also hides the recovery behaviour. ⇒ 모델이 보지 못한 줄을 고치거나 수정 범위가 겹치면 적용 전에 거부합니다. 읽은 뒤 파일이 바뀌어 태그가 오래되었다면, 기록된 스냅샷으로 결과가 하나로 정해질 때만 복구해 적용하고 그렇지 않으면 다시 읽으라고 돌려보냅니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:230` 「하네스는 호출 전체를 한 번의 성공 표시로 뭉개지 말고 파일별 적용 결과와 diff를 남겨야」 — '뭉개지 말고' is colloquial and negative. OMP already returns per-file results; state that as a fact. ⇒ 그래서 OMP는 파일별 적용 결과를 따로 돌려줍니다. 모델은 실패한 파일만 다시 읽고 고치면 됩니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:248` 「완성된 부분만 읽어 쓰지 않는 diff 미리 보기를 만들 수 있습니다 … 화면의 미리 보기는 설명용 표시이고」 — '쓰지 않는 diff' reads as 'unused diff'. '설명용' is the wrong word. ⇒ 모델이 편집 인자를 보내는 동안 OMP는 도착한 부분까지만 해석해, 파일에는 쓰지 않고 diff 미리 보기를 그립니다. 미리 보기는 참고용이고, 적용 여부는 실행 시점에 다시 검증한 결과로 정해집니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:261` 「같은 쓰기 경계에 묶을 수 있습니다. 따라서 편집 성공 뒤의 검증은 모델이 의도한 문자열이 있었는지만 보는 일이 아닙니다.」 — An abstract '경계' plus a negative qualification without a decision; it also repeats the exchange. ⇒ 언어 서버가 연결되어 있으면 OMP는 파일을 쓴 직후 포맷을 적용하고 진단을 받아 편집 결과에 함께 넣습니다. 포맷이 바꾼 줄이나 새 타입 오류도 같은 결과에서 보입니다.
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:188` 「새 파일의 전체 내용과 생성 사실이 도구 결과에 기록됩니다.」 — Inaccurate. A plain write returns `Successfully wrote <n> bytes to <path>` (docs/tools/write.md), not the file content. ⇒ Rewrite: '`write`는 기록한 바이트 수와 경로를 돌려줍니다.'
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:160` 「승인 화면도 생성과 변경을 구분할 수 있습니다.」 — [INFERENCE] Not verified against OMP's approval UI; ch08 owns approval. ⇒ Delete, or cite the approval code that distinguishes them.
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:184` 「같은 이름의 파일이 없는지 먼저 확인합니다.」 — Unclear actor. OMP's write does not check; it overwrites. If the model is meant to check, the step needs a tool (glob or read). ⇒ Delete the stage, or name the tool call that performs the check.
- I L167 execution-path '변경 단위에 맞춰 도구를 선택하기' — A two-row comparison padded with skipped stages ('`write`는 사용하지 않습니다', '`edit`를 사용하지 않습니다'). It contains the inaccurate write-result claim. ⇒ Remove the skipped stages and fix the write result; or convert it to a static two-row table. The timeout task itself is a good setup.
- I L211 tool-sequence '한 줄 변경이 파일에 적용되기까지' (10 events) — Four of the ten events repeat ch09's read. The self-loops ('입력 검증', '변경 준비') correctly show that validation happens before writing, but because only the happy path appears, validation never changes an outcome. ⇒ Start from the given state (tag already read). Turn it into an execution-path with three tabs: fresh tag → applied with a new tag; file reformatted after reading → stale tag recovered with a warning; edit to an unread line → rejected with 're-read' guidance. That makes the consequence of the snapshot check observable.
- I L251 exchange '검증을 통과한 파일 변경' — A good fan-out of one write into three effects (cache invalidation, LSP sync and diagnostics, session snapshot); prose cannot show these side effects as compactly. ⇒ Keep it; delete the restating paragraph after it (L261) or reduce it to the diagnostics consequence.

- R [tool-sequence ✓] TS L211 한 줄 변경이 파일에 적용되기까지 (10 steps; 1649px / 1512px) — Steps 1–4 repeat ch09's read, routed through 'OMP 편집기'. Self-steps 6–7 (validation, diff) use kind `read`. The single chip `#edit-7C2A` repeats on all 10 steps. ⇒ Start at the edit call: edit → 검증 (self, `call`) → 기록 → 결과 → model, 5 steps. Show the hashline payload as a code block and drop the chips.
- R [exchange ✓] EX L251 검증을 통과한 파일 변경 → 파일 시스템 / 언어 서버 / 세션 (3 outputs; 426px / 582px) — A real fan-out. ⇒ Keep.

## 11 셸 실행

- **Verdict:** keep. A substantial distinct mechanism: input normalisation, three backends, embedded shell, non-interactive environment, timeouts and cancellation, output truncation and artifacts. It is the base that ch12/13 build on.
- **Overall:** Mostly accurate for the snapshot: 300 s default timeout, `timeout:0`, merged stdout/stderr, `exitCode` only on non-zero, the PTY fallback notice, the client-terminal bridge, and the 50 KiB window with artifact spill. The chapter's strength is the output exchange. Weaknesses: the sequence credits approval to BashTool (in OMP approval runs before `BashTool.execute`), several prescriptive 'harness should' sentences have no OMP behaviour behind them, and OMP's most distinctive shell mechanisms are missing (embedded brush shell with in-process utilities, session-reused shell, interceptor routing). The input list is now stale: HEAD has no `env` input and adds `name`/`ready`.
- **Organization:** The sections are grouped sensibly: (1) inputs and result, (2) backend choice, (3) output, deadline and cancellation. Approval and interception are squeezed into one sequence step; approval belongs to ch08 (link it), and interception deserves a sentence in §1 because it ties back to ch09/10. The catalogue lists 'background-job integration' in ch11's scope, yet the chapter never mentions auto-background; add one sentence pointing to ch12.
- **Ordering:** `async` appears in the input list (L288) with no explanation (ch12 covers it). '인터셉터' (L297) is used undefined. 'ACP' (L351) is used undefined. The §2 execution-path presents three backends as if chosen by task type ('적합한 작업'), but only `pty` is the model's choice; the client-terminal route is picked by the harness when the client advertises the capability. State who chooses before the tabs.
- **Missing/stale:** Embedded shell: non-PTY commands run in OMP's embedded brush shell (crates/pi-shell, vendored brush-core) with in-process utilities (`cat`, `grep`, `sed`, `jq`/jaq, …). Native `Shell` sessions are cached per session and shell config. This is present at 3b3a6dc9 (docs/native-crates.md, docs/bash-tool-runtime.md) and qualifies L305's '실제 자식 프로세스를 만드는 주체는 셸 백엔드입니다'.
- **Missing/stale:** Interceptor routing (`bashInterceptor`, which sends cat/grep/find/sed -i/redirect writes to dedicated tools) is distinct from approval and links ch09/10.
- **Missing/stale:** Timeout clamping (1–3600 s plus `tools.maxTimeout`), direnv preflight, the output minimizer with a raw-output artifact, and shared concurrency for parallel non-PTY calls.
- **Missing/stale:** Auto-background (`bash.autoBackground`, 60 s threshold), which turns a foreground call into a job; it belongs to the catalogue scope 'background-job integration'.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD stale: the `env` input is removed ('There is no `env` input', docs/tools/bash.md); `name` and `ready` add a supervised-service mode to `bash`; plain calls run in brush even when `shellPath` points to PowerShell. The `docs/tools/bash.md` reference is still valid; `packages/coding-agent/src/session/streaming-output.ts` moved to `packages/tui/src/tools/streaming-output.ts`.

## 12 백그라운드 작업

- **Verdict:** keep. The job manager is a session-level mechanism shared by bash, eval and subagent tasks (`AsyncJobType = "bash" | "task" | "eval"`). It has its own ownership, delivery queue with retry and dead-lettering, suppression and acknowledgement, retention, wait and cancel. That is not a sub-feature of `bash`. As written, though, the chapter only shows bash async and reads like a section of ch11. It earns independence only if it presents the manager as shared infrastructure. It should not merge with ch13: the broker is a separate per-project daemon process with readiness and liveness semantics, while a job is in-session with a single completion result.
- **Overall:** Accurate for the snapshot's job manager: the immediate `bg_N` return, owner-scoped delivery, acknowledging a delivery when a wait returns it, 5-minute retention, and no hang on unknown IDs. The key message, '초기 결과는 접수 성공', is well stated. Problems: (1) the model-facing API (`hub wait`, `hub cancel`) no longer exists at HEAD, where `hub` is replaced by an argument-less `wait` tool and `proc://` read/kill; (2) the chapter treats jobs as bash-only, although the manager also runs `task` (subagent) and `eval` jobs, which is what justifies a separate chapter; (3) auto-background is cited in a reference but never explained; (4) §3 previews ch13 at length.
- **Organization:** §1 submit and deliver, and §2 wait/inspect/cancel, are both on-topic. §3 ('끝나는 작업과 계속 살아 있는 서비스를 구분한다') largely duplicates ch13's opening, with the same build-versus-dev contrast and the same readiness and restart vocabulary. Move it to ch13 as that chapter's opener and leave one forward-reference sentence here. Add a section on the other ways work becomes a job: auto-background, plus eval and task jobs.
- **Ordering:** '`hub wait`' appears as a tab label (L462) before the paragraph that introduces `hub` (L483). The §2 input says '다른 조사는 끝났다' (L450), yet the first tab says '모델은 파일 조사나 편집을 이어 갑니다' (L457): the given state contradicts one of its own paths. §3 depends on ch13 concepts (readiness, log cursor, restart policy) before they are introduced.
- **Missing/stale:** The job manager is generic: `AsyncJobType = "bash" | "task" | "eval"` (packages/coding-agent/src/async/job-manager.ts). Subagent tasks and eval cells use the same ownership and delivery path.
- **Missing/stale:** Auto-background (`bash.autoBackground.enabled`, `thresholdMs` 60 000) and the job cap (`async.maxJobs`, clamped to 1–100, fallback 15) with foreground fallback at capacity.
- **Missing/stale:** Delivery mechanics: retry with backoff, dead-lettering when the owner session is gone, and suppression while a wait watches the job.
- **Missing/stale:** Deadline semantics: the job deadline counts from job start, so an auto-backgrounded command has already spent its foreground wait (HEAD's background notice states the deadline).
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD stale: `hub` is removed (commit f89a6db15e 'deprecated hub tool in favor of wait and protocol-based interfaces'). The current surface is the `wait` tool (no arguments, returns on the first owned job or peer message, 30-minute safety cap; docs/tools/wait.md), `read proc://` / `read proc://<id>` (inspect without consuming), and `write proc://<id>/kill` (cancel). The `docs/tools/hub.md` and `packages/coding-agent/src/tools/hub/jobs.ts` links are dead at HEAD.

- P `tools-and-execution.ts:406` 「오래 걸리지만 끝이 있는 실행을 맡기고, 다른 작업을 이어 가며 완료 결과를 회수하는 관리자.」 — A long chained subtitle; '회수' is stiff. ⇒ 실행을 맡겨 두고 다른 일을 하다가, 끝난 결과를 대화로 돌려받게 하는 관리자.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:414` 「몇 분 뒤 끝나는 명령을 앞에서 기다리면 에이전트가 다른 조사를 시작하지 못합니다.」 — '앞에서 기다리면' is a literal rendering of 'foreground'. ⇒ 통합 테스트처럼 몇 분 걸리는 명령이 끝날 때까지 기다리면, 그동안 에이전트는 다른 조사를 할 수 없습니다.

- P `tools-and-execution.ts:483` 「하네스는 자동 전달을 놓친 화면도 작업 목록에서 최종 결과를 다시 확인할 수 있게 합니다.」 — An odd actor ('화면이 놓친다') and an unsupported framing. ⇒ 완료된 작업은 5분 동안 남아 있으므로, 자동 전달이 실패해도 작업 목록에서 결과를 다시 볼 수 있습니다.

- B `tools-and-execution.ts:500` 「그 관리자는 준비 조건, 안정된 이름, 로그 커서와 재시작 정책을 별도로 보존합니다.」 — A ch13 feature list placed inside ch12, and partly stale at HEAD (log cursor and restart policy are no longer model-facing). ⇒ Delete; leave '오래 실행되는 서버는 다음 장의 프로세스 관리자가 맡습니다.'
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:518` 「OMP · Bash 자동 백그라운드 처리」 — The reference promises auto-background, but no prose explains it. ⇒ Add one paragraph (60 s threshold, foreground call converted to a job, skipped for PTY and client-terminal runs, falls back to foreground at the job cap) or remove the link.

The actor 'OMP BashTool·작업 관리자' merges two actors exactly where the handoff matters. ⇒ Split the actors into BashTool and the job manager. ## 13 프로세스 관리

- **Verdict:** keep. The process broker is a separate, out-of-session daemon (private socket under `~/.omp/run/daemons/<project-hash>/`). It brings its own lifecycle (readiness, liveness, rotation, persist/session/detached modes) and cross-instance sharing. Neither the bash executor (ch11) nor the in-session job manager (ch12) has these. At HEAD the launch path moved into `bash name`, but the broker mechanism is unchanged and still distinct. Merging with ch12 would combine two different lifecycles (one completion result versus ongoing liveness) under one heading; a shared `proc://`/`wait` note in both chapters covers the overlap.
- **Overall:** Faithful to the snapshot's `hub` process ops: separate `application`/`args`, readiness requiring both log regex and TCP port, readiness timeout without killing, a per-project broker shared across instances, `persist`, restart policies with backoff, and log cursors with follow. At HEAD most of the model-facing surface described here no longer exists. Services start through `bash` with `name`/`ready` (the command runs through the user's shell, not argv fields), a live name is restarted with the new spec rather than refused, inspection and control go through `proc://`, and restart policy is fixed to `no` on that path. The chapter also never explains what the broker is (a detached daemon per project) until §3, although every section depends on it.
- **Organization:** §1 readiness, §2 control by name, §3 cross-instance lifecycle. §3 contains the setup that §1–2 need: the broker is a per-project process that outlives a call, which is why names persist. Suggested order: (0) the finite-job-versus-service contrast moved from ch12 §3; (1) the broker and stable names; (2) readiness; (3) logs, input and stop; (4) persist and restart modes. The restart-policy paragraph (L632) sits under 'ownership' but is about health.
- **Ordering:** The actor 'OMP 프로세스 브로커' appears in the §1 sequence (L544) before 'broker' is explained (L603). `hub ps/logs/send` (L573) are used without introducing `hub` (ch12 did not introduce it properly either). The `persist` path in §3 depends on 'last client leaves', which needs the broker explanation first.
- **Missing/stale:** What the broker is: a detached daemon started on the first process op, reached over a private socket under `~/.omp/run/daemons/<project-hash>/`, which exits after the last client unless a persistent process remains.
- **Missing/stale:** `detached` mode (implies persist, forces `pty:false`, disables stdin); input is one shared stream across all project clients; approval tier `exec` for start/stop/restart/send.
- **Missing/stale:** Log limits: 25 MiB current log plus one rotated file; terminal-query replies for supervised PTYs (HEAD broker answers cursor/device queries).
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD stale (whole model surface): services start via `bash` `name` and `ready` (docs/tools/bash.md; packages/coding-agent/src/launch/services.ts). The command runs through the user's shell (`application: shell.shell`, args include the command string). Restart policy is fixed to `no`. A live name is replaced with the new spec. Control goes through `proc://` read/write/kill and `/mode`; exit notifications auto-deliver. The `docs/tools/hub.md` links are dead at HEAD.

- P `tools-and-execution.ts:530` 「개발 서버처럼 오래 사는 프로그램의 준비 상태와 로그, 입력, 재시작, 종료를 이름으로 관리하는 구성 요소.」 — A subtitle that enumerates sections; '오래 사는' is a literal rendering of 'long-lived'. ⇒ 개발 서버처럼 계속 실행되는 프로그램을 이름으로 시작하고 관리하는 구성 요소.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:534` 「프로세스 시작과 서비스 준비를 구분한다」 — 한다-style title. ⇒ 프로세스가 뜬 뒤 준비될 때까지
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:538` 「개발 서버는 프로세스가 생성된 순간부터 요청을 받을 수 있는 것이 아닙니다.」 — A '~것이 아닙니다' negative construction. The next clause is also stale at HEAD ('셸 문자열이 아닌 별도 필드'). ⇒ 개발 서버는 프로세스가 뜬 뒤에도 설정을 읽고 번들러를 시작해 포트를 열어야 요청을 받습니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:556` 「하네스는 “시작됨”, “준비됨”, “준비 확인 시간 초과”, “종료됨”을 나눠야 후속 도구가 너무 일찍 접속하지 않습니다.」 — 'Should…so that not' for states the broker already reports. ⇒ 결과에는 준비 완료, 준비 시간 초과(실행 중), 종료 중 하나가 표시되므로, 모델은 브라우저 검사를 시작할지 로그를 더 볼지 정할 수 있습니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:573` 「명령을 다시 조립하는 대신 같은 프로세스를 계속 가리키므로 로그와 입력의 주체가 분명합니다.」 — '주체가 분명합니다' is abstract and adds nothing. ⇒ Delete, or: '모델은 PID를 기억하지 않고 `web`이라는 이름으로 같은 서버의 로그를 읽고 입력을 보냅니다.'
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:599` 「프로젝트 안에서 프로세스 소유권을 공유한다」 — '소유권을 공유' is self-contradictory translationese. ⇒ 여러 OMP 인스턴스가 함께 쓰는 프로세스
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:632` 「하네스는 자동 재시작 횟수와 현재 PID, 마지막 종료 이유를 표시해야 “서버가 있다”는 이름만으로 건강 상태를 오판하지 않습니다.」 — Generic advice. At HEAD the bash service path hard-codes `restart: "no"` (packages/coding-agent/src/launch/services.ts), so the model cannot set a policy. ⇒ Delete, or state the HEAD fact: '`ps`/`proc://` 목록에는 상태, 실행 시간, PID가 함께 표시됩니다.'
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:586` 「재시작 뒤에는 새 실행을 같은 이름에 연결하면서 이전 출력 로그를 회전해, 현재 실행과 과거 실행을 구분합니다.」 — Overstated. The broker rotates the old log when it opens a new launch and at the 25 MiB cap, but log reads combine the previous and current files (`DaemonLog.readFiles`), so runs are not kept apart for the reader. ⇒ Rewrite: '새로 시작할 때 이전 로그는 한 개까지 보관하고, 현재 로그는 25 MiB를 넘으면 회전합니다.'
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:603` 「이미 살아 있는 이름으로 다시 시작하려 하면 새 복제본을 만들지 않고 중지나 재시작을 요구합니다.」 — True at 3b3a6dc9, reversed at HEAD: 'A live name is stopped and restarted with the new spec' (docs/tools/bash.md). ⇒ Update to HEAD behaviour or mark it as snapshot-specific.
- I L541 tool-sequence '개발 서버가 준비된 뒤 브라우저 검사를 시작하기' — A good relationship to show: process spawned ≠ ready, and both conditions are checked. But the title promises a browser check that never appears, and the event-1 payload uses the removed `hub start` and `application/args` API. ⇒ Add the final model event (start the browser check after `ready`). At HEAD the call becomes `bash {command:"bun run dev", name:"web", ready:{log:"Local:.*5173", port:5173}}`. Consider a second tab where readiness times out and the process keeps running, which makes L556 observable.
- I L575 exchange '관리 중인 `web`' — An API option list dressed as a fan-out; the three outputs are independent operations, not transformations of the input. Cursor, signal and restart are stale at HEAD. ⇒ Convert to a short paragraph or a static table of HEAD operations: `read proc://web` (status and log tail), `write proc://web` (stdin, Enter appended), `write proc://web/kill` (stop), `write proc://web/mode` (persist/session/detached).
- I L606 execution-path '호스트 세션이 끝날 때의 서비스 수명' — Two minimal paths; the consequence (the service survives or not) is clear. It misses `detached`, and at HEAD the mode is changed after start via `proc://<name>/mode`. ⇒ Keep it; add a `detached` tab (implies persist, no PTY, no stdin) and show the HEAD mode switch.

### Range patterns (ch 09–13)

- Prescriptive 'harness should' closers: '하네스는/하네스가' appears 14 times in L1–644, mostly as the last sentence of a section, phrased as '…해야 (모델이) …하지 않습니다'. Most describe something OMP already does (skip offsets, artifact notice, job-ID linkage, readiness states) or give ungrounded advice. Examples: L102, L387, L432, L556, L632. Fix: state OMP's actual behaviour and its observable consequence, or delete.
- Subtitles that enumerate the section list (the t0014 lesson). Five of five descriptions do this: L152, L280, L406, L530, and L9 in part. Model them on ch01/02's one-clause descriptions.
- Section titles in declarative 한다 form ('~한다/~는다') in 14 of 15 sections, unlike ch01/02's noun-phrase or ~하기 titles. Examples: L13 '이름에서 내용으로 범위를 좁힌다', L204 '본 파일을 기준으로 수정한다', L599 '프로젝트 안에서 프로세스 소유권을 공유한다'.
- Happy-path-only interactive examples. Each chapter's central safeguard (edit snapshot validation, exit-code error marking, readiness timeout, job cancellation versus failure) is described in prose, but no block shows the case where it changes the outcome. Each chapter should have one consequential failure tab.
- Padded diagrams: tool-sequences with four hops per call and generic details (ch09: 12 events; ch10: 10 events, 4 of them a repeated read), and execution-paths padded with 'skipped' stages that restate 'this tool is not used' (ch09 L72/89/90, ch10 L177/186). Cut to call/result with concrete payloads; drop skipped stages.
- Abstract nouns where nothing literal is meant: '상태' appears 15 times, many legitimately (process states), but also '실행 표면' (L322), '쓰기 경계' (L261), '주체가 분명' (L573), '수정 의도를 고정' (L152), '단서를 좁혀' (L9).
- Blurred actors: approval attributed to BashTool (L297); the 'harness' choosing next search inputs (L17); merged actor 'OMP BashTool·작업 관리자' at the very handoff being taught (L420); the backend chooser implying the model picks the ACP terminal (L321).
- Speech-level and prompt style inconsistency: ch09's prompt uses a request form ('찾아 줘', as ch02 does), while ch10–13 prompts and inputs use plain 한다체 ('바꾼다', '확인한다'). Pick the ch02 convention.
- Undefined terms used before introduction: 스냅샷 태그 (ch09 L35), 인터셉터 (L297), ACP (L351), `hub` (L462, L573), 브로커 (L544).
- Cross-chapter: HEAD invalidates the model-facing API of ch12 and ch13 (and every other chapter that teaches `hub`, e.g. peer messaging and subagent chapters). Commit f89a6db15e replaced `hub` with the `wait` tool, `proc://` read/write/kill/mode, `agent://` writes, and `bash name/ready` for services. The source mechanisms (job manager, launch broker) remain, so the chapters need surface updates rather than removal.
- Cross-chapter: ch12 §3 duplicates ch13's motivation (build versus dev, readiness, restart). Move the contrast to the start of ch13 and keep one forward sentence in ch12.
- Cross-chapter: ch09 §3 (snapshot tags as edit anchors, the exchange's 'editor' output) duplicates ch10 §2. Introduce the `[PATH#TAG]` format once in ch09 and explain validation and recovery only in ch10.
- Cross-chapter: ch11's sequence folds approval into BashTool. Approval belongs to ch08 (tool permissions); ch11 should link to it and keep only the interceptor and cwd checks.
- Cross-chapter: The catalogue assigns 'background-job integration' to ch11, but neither ch11 nor ch12 explains auto-background (ch12 only links the doc). Explain it once, preferably in ch12, with a forward pointer from ch11.
- Cross-chapter: The job manager also carries `task` (subagent) and `eval` jobs. ch12 should say so and the ch14 (eval) and subagent chapters should link back, otherwise ch12 reads as a bash subsection.
- Cross-chapter: The bash interceptor (cat/grep/find/sed -i/redirect → read/grep/glob/edit/write) is the explicit harness mechanism tying ch09–11 together and is absent from all three; HEAD's default rules also route unmanaged services to named `bash` services (ch13).
- Cross-chapter: ch14 onward (from line 646) were not reviewed here; the peer ReviewCh14to19 covers them.

## 14 Python과 JavaScript 실행

- **Verdict:** keep. Retained kernels, the prelude/tool bridge, and the per-language reset/cancel rules are distinct from shell execution (Ch11) and background jobs (Ch12). The mechanism is large and specific to eval.
- **Overall:** The technical claims mostly hold against the 3b3a6dc9 eval docs: one call is one cell, state is kept per language, reset applies to one language, the default timeout is 30 s and `timeout: 0` disables it, Python interrupts and then escalates, and `bash.patterns` does not cover eval. The chapter teaches less than it shows. The persistence example appears three times (prose, two code blocks, and an 8-step sequence), and no example shows a consequence such as a reset making a variable disappear. The bridge diagram leaves out the model and the harness, though the harness is the subject of the paragraph below it. The chapter also omits the parts of eval that later chapters depend on: extension preludes like `browser` (Ch19), plus agent()/workpool() and kernel-defined tools.
- **Organization:** Section 2 ('출력 채널과 커널 수명') puts two unrelated questions under one title: what comes back from a cell, and when the kernel restarts. Split it into 'what the model receives' and 'when state is lost (reset, cancel, timeout)'. The bridge section is the right place to introduce extension preludes (`browser`, at HEAD also `judge`) and to point to Ch36 for `agent()`/`workpool()`. Ch19 currently assumes the reader already knows that `browser` is an Eval global.
- **Ordering:** Section 1 uses `display()` in code and in the sequence. Section 2's exchange explains the output channels only afterwards. That is acceptable because the code makes the meaning plain, but the sentence about what `display` returns should come before the first sequence. Nothing depends on later chapters, but Ch19 depends on a concept (preludes) that this chapter never sets up.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: `agent()`, `completion()`, `wait()`, `workpool()` and kernel-defined tools (`@tool`/`tool(fn)`), which let subagents call back into the kernel (docs/tools/eval.md at 3b3a6dc9). At minimum add one paragraph with a pointer to Ch36; kernel-defined tools are unique to eval.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: extension preludes. `browser` is an Eval prelude, not an AgentTool (docs/tools/browser.md), and Ch19 depends on this. At HEAD 3f000c52 preludes are documented at `xd://eval/<name>`.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: `eval.autoBackground` (default off, threshold 60 s), which turns a long cell into a managed job. It ties to Ch12.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: `python.kernelMode="per-call"` disables persistence. Python kernels are keyed by session, cwd, and interpreter.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD: `timeout` is now an 'active-runtime timeout window'. Waits on agent/completion handles, `judge()`, and `%pip` pause the watchdog and start a fresh window. HEAD also adds standalone `%load`/`%pip install`/`%bun add` cells.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD: the snapshot retried a dead kernel once. At HEAD the cell is not replayed and the next call starts a fresh kernel. This supports the chapter's 'don't assume state survived' advice; cite it concretely.

- P `tools-and-execution.ts:650` 「변수를 유지하는 실행 커널에서 계산을 나누어 수행하고 구조화된 결과와 하네스 도구를 연결하는 런타임.」 — The subtitle is a noun-chain table of contents (persistence + results + tools), the pattern the user rejected in Ch01. ⇒ 호출이 끝나도 변수가 남아 다음 호출에서 이어 쓸 수 있는 Python·JavaScript 실행 환경.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:658` 「다음 호출은 같은 언어와 세션 ID를 사용해 앞선 값을 이어 씁니다.」 — This implies the model sends a session ID. In fact OMP derives the executor id from the session (`getEvalSessionId`/`defaultEvalSessionId`), and the model only picks `language`. ⇒ 변수와 불러온 모듈은 언어별 런타임에 남습니다. 모델이 같은 언어로 다음 셀을 보내면 앞에서 만든 값을 그대로 쓸 수 있고, 어느 런타임에 보낼지는 OMP가 세션에 따라 정합니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:706` 「출력 채널과 커널 수명을 따로 관리한다」 — The title is abstract, mixes two topics, and '커널 수명을 관리한다' names nobody acting. ⇒ 셀이 돌려주는 결과 / 커널을 새로 시작하는 경우 (two sections)
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:719` 「하네스는 재설정의 대상을 언어별 세션으로 기록해야 합니다.」 — Noun-heavy directive. The concrete fact is already stated in the previous clause. ⇒ `reset: true`는 고른 언어의 런타임만 새로 만듭니다. Python을 재설정해도 JavaScript 쪽 변수는 그대로 남습니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:723` 「취소 뒤에는 기존 변수가 남았다고 가정하지 않고 실행 결과의 커널 상태를 확인해야 합니다.」 — Vague: '커널 상태를 확인' gives no action the reader can take. ⇒ 취소된 Python 셀 뒤에는 커널이 새로 시작됐을 수 있습니다. 다음 셀에서는 필요한 변수를 다시 만들거나, 먼저 남아 있는지 확인합니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:741` 「Eval의 사전 로드는 단순 계산 함수뿐 아니라 `read`, `write`, `tool.<name>(args)`를 제공합니다.」 — '사전 로드' is a literal translation of 'prelude', and '단순 계산 함수' misdescribes `display`/`print`. The closing sentence '내부 URL, 도구 결과 형식을 그대로 재사용' is vague. ⇒ 셀에는 `display`, `read`, `write`, `tool.<name>(args)` 같은 도우미가 미리 들어 있습니다. `await tool.read({...})`는 모델이 직접 부른 `read`와 같은 작업 디렉터리에서 실행되고 같은 형식의 결과를 돌려줍니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:757` 「실행 권한을 제한하려는 호스트는 `eval`의 승인 정책도 함께 정해야 합니다.」 — Names no actual setting. The source names `tools.approval.eval` explicitly (docs/approval-mode.md, docs/bash-tool-runtime.md). ⇒ `bash.patterns`의 `deny` 규칙은 Eval 코드가 `subprocess.run`이나 `Bun.$`로 띄운 셸에는 적용되지 않습니다. 같은 명령을 막으려면 `tools.approval.eval`도 `prompt`나 `deny`로 설정합니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:745` 「커널 계산과 도구 호출의 경계를 유지하기」 — The diagram title rests on the abstract noun '경계' and says nothing about what happens. ⇒ 셀 안에서 `grep` 도구 부르기
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:757` 「도구의 승인과 경로 처리, 아티팩트 제한은 일반 호출과 같은 계층에서 적용됩니다.」 — '같은 계층' is vague. The bridge validates arguments and calls the registered tool's `execute` (packages/coding-agent/src/eval/js/tool-bridge.ts); approval comes from the tool wrapper [INFERENCE that bridged tools are wrapped]. Only enabled tools are callable (`getToolForEvalBridge`, per Ch07), and `checkpoint`/`rewind` are refused. ⇒ State precisely: '셀에서 부른 도구도 모델이 직접 부른 것과 같은 인자 검사와 승인 정책을 거칩니다. 현재 세션에서 켜진 도구만 부를 수 있고, `checkpoint`·`rewind`는 셀에서 부를 수 없습니다.'
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:662` 「아래 두 Python 셀은 요청 네 건의 지연 시간을 한 번만 정의합니다.」 — The setup is good, but the code captions (L667, L675) and the 8-step sequence then restate the same fact twice more. ⇒ Keep the setup sentence and the two code blocks, and drop the sequence (see interactive).
- I tool-sequence '호출은 나뉘어도 Python 커널은 이어진다' (L680-694) — Eight steps make two identical four-hop rounds. The EvalTool→kernel→EvalTool hops only say '실행합니다/보냅니다', and the code blocks above already show the whole relationship. Nothing would look different if the kernel did not persist. ⇒ Replace it with an execution-path of two branches over the same second cell. Branch 1, same kernel: returns `{"slow_requests": [300, 240]}`. Branch 2, `reset: true`: `NameError: name 'durations_ms' is not defined`. This makes both persistence and the cost of reset visible.
- I exchange (L709-716) — The input is an abstract list ('계산 코드 · stdout/stderr · display() 호출 · 선택한 제한 시간') and the outputs are prose descriptions. This is a bullet list dressed as a diagram. ⇒ Use one concrete cell (`print("loaded")`, `display({"n": 4})`, a plot) and show what the model actually receives: the text block with JSON inline, the image block, and `details.cells[0].status`. Otherwise convert it to a short list.
- I tool-sequence '커널 계산과 도구 호출의 경계를 유지하기' (L744-754) — The actors are muddled: '모델의 Eval 코드' and '유지 커널·도구 브리지' are the same process, and neither the model nor the harness appears. Its prompt (grep for `timeout`) differs from the prose example (large JSON plus `tool.read` for a config file). The paragraph's main point, that approval passes through the harness, is not drawn. ⇒ Use one example with these actors: 모델 / Eval 커널 / OMP(검증·승인·도구 실행) / 작업 공간. Steps: `eval` call → kernel reaches `await tool.grep(...)` → OMP validates and approves, then runs grep → value returns into the kernel → cell `display()`s the combined result → one tool result goes to the model.
