## 15 AST 검색과 편집

- **Verdict:** keep. It has its own pattern grammar (single-node/multi-node captures, whole-node rule, per-language compilation) and a two-phase preview/resolve edit protocol. None of this overlaps the text-based edit chapter beyond the shared resolve device.
- **Overall:** This is the most accurate chapter in the range. The metavariable rules, the 50-match limit, gitignore/`node_modules` handling, parse-error reporting, skipping syntax-error files when editing, and the preview→`xd://resolve`→rerun→count check all match docs/tools/ast-grep.md and ast-edit.md. Its weak points: the grep-vs-ast_grep comparison reports generic verdicts instead of actual matches, and the most important consequence is buried in a subordinate clause (a stale preview can still leave files written when apply reports an error). Two sentences give the harness a job that OMP already does. The sequence shows a direct `ast_edit` call, but Ch07 uses exactly `ast_edit` as its example of a tool mounted under `xd://` and called via `write`.
- **Organization:** The sentence on capture expansion (L886: '한 캡처를 문법이 허용하지 않는 여러 형제 노드로 늘릴 수 없으며') is a pattern-grammar rule. Move it into section 2. Section 3 should then cover only preview, apply, and the stale-preview case.
- **Ordering:** The order is fine. `$A` appears in section 1 before section 2 defines metavariables, but the stage text explains it inline. `xd://resolve` appears without introduction; one clause should say it is the device for confirming a pending proposal, or point to where Ch07 introduces `xd://`.
- **Missing/stale:** Presentation: `ast_grep`/`ast_edit` are discoverable tools. With `tools.xdev` (default `true` at both 3b3a6dc9 and HEAD) they are mounted as `xd://ast_edit` etc. and called through `write`. Ch07 (tool-definitions-and-registry.ts:119) uses exactly this example, so this chapter's direct `ast_edit` call contradicts it.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: the pending-proposal enforcement. While a preview is unresolved, the session raises a `SoftToolRequirement` with a reminder and forces `write` to `xd://resolve`/`xd://reject` if the model ignores it (docs/tools/ast-edit.md flow step 8). This is a real harness mechanism and answers 'what if the model never confirms?'.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: rules are applied in pattern-string sort order, not `ops` order. File cap `PI_MAX_AST_FILES`=1000. Overlapping matches abort the whole run (mentioned) but independent matches in one file coexist.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD 3f000c52 additions: `ast_grep` has a `lang` override (e.g. `.h` as `cpp`), JSON member fragments are accepted as patterns, `local://` URLs can be searched and rewritten, and identity rewrites report matches but apply skips the write.

- P `tools-and-execution.ts:813` 「하네스는 질문이 이름·텍스트에 관한지, 구문 역할에 관한지에 따라 도구를 골라야 합니다.」 — The model chooses the tool, not the harness, so the actor is wrong. The sentence is also noun-heavy. ⇒ 찾는 대상이 글자 자체라면 `grep`을, 호출이나 선언 같은 구문이라면 `ast_grep`을 씁니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:848` 「하네스는 검색 수, 결과 제한, 파싱 오류를 함께 반환해야 합니다.」 — OMP already does this. A zero-match result with parse issues reads 'No matches found. Parse issues mean the query may be mis-scoped; narrow path before concluding absence.' Describe the behaviour instead of issuing a directive. ⇒ 그래서 OMP는 일치가 없을 때도 파싱하지 못한 파일을 함께 알리고, 경로를 좁혀 다시 검색한 뒤에 '없다'고 판단하라고 안내합니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:886` 「현재 구현은 재계산한 변경을 쓴 뒤 개수를 대조하므로, 하네스는 오류 때도 실제 diff를 다시 읽고」 — The key consequence (an error does not mean nothing was written) is buried in a causal clause. ⇒ 적용 단계는 변경을 다시 계산해 파일에 쓴 다음 개수를 미리 보기와 대조합니다. 그래서 개수가 달라 오류가 나도 파일은 이미 바뀌어 있을 수 있습니다. 오류를 받으면 먼저 diff를 읽어 실제로 바뀐 내용을 확인합니다.

- B `tools-and-execution.ts:798` 「빠르고 폭넓지만 바꿀 코드가 아닌 문자열도 함께 나옵니다.」 — This is a generic verdict where the example could show the actual result lines. ⇒ Replace with the observed output, e.g. '2번째 줄(호출)과 5번째 줄(문자열) 두 곳이 나옵니다.'
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:784` 「주석, 문서, 문자열 리터럴에도 나타날 수 있습니다」 — The diagram's input has only a call and a string, so 주석/문서 are promised but never shown. ⇒ Add a comment line to the example file or drop 주석/문서 from the sentence.
- I execution-path '로그 호출만 찾는 두 가지 검색' (L787-810) — The comparison is real, but the stage '문자열 제외' is marked `skipped`. That misuses the state: nothing was skipped, the string simply does not match. The results are opinions ('빠르고 폭넓지만…') rather than outputs. ⇒ Convert it to an exchange. Input: a 5-line file with a `console.log(user.id)` call, a `// console.log` comment, and a `"do not replace console.log"` string. Outputs: `grep` → 3 hit lines; `ast_grep console.log($A)` → 1 hit with `$A = user.id`.
- I tool-sequence '구조 변환의 제안과 적용을 연결하기' (L867-883) — Nine steps for the happy path. 'Native dry-run' → '계산된 제안' → '변경 제안' is a relay chain. The interesting branch, a file changed after preview, is only described in prose. n0001 also records a layout failure at step 8. ⇒ Cut it to 5 steps: preview call → proposal (count + `xd://resolve`/`xd://reject`) → `write xd://resolve` → rerun → `Applied N replacements`. Then add a small execution-path for apply: '파일 그대로' → applied; '미리 보기 뒤 파일 변경' → count mismatch error, files already written.
- R [execution-path ✓] EP L787 로그 호출만 찾는 두 가지 검색 (2 tabs × 2/3; 605px / 746px) — A real same-input comparison, but the results are prose instead of the actual matches. ⇒ In each result card, show the match list: grep gives 2 lines including the string; ast_grep gives 1 call with `$A = user.id`.
- R [tool-sequence ✓] TS L868 구조 변환의 제안과 적용을 연결하기 (9 steps; 1583px / 1420px) — The two-phase preview → resolve is chapter-specific. Step 8's self-check uses kind `read`, and the single chip repeats ×9. ⇒ Cut to 6 by merging the native round trips. Use `call` for verification and drop the chips.

## 16 언어 서버

- **Verdict:** keep. Server routing, a shared broker, JSON-RPC lifecycle, semantic navigation, and versioned diagnostics form a substantial subsystem distinct from text search and editing.
- **Overall:** Routing, lazy initialization, definition output with one context line each side, approval tiers, and the version-aware diagnostics wait (writethrough inline/deferred race) are all source-backed. The execution-path gets `rename` wrong: it shows preview, then approval, then apply, but `rename` applies by default and previews only with `apply: false`. The chapter never covers the `diagnostics` action itself or workspace diagnostics (`tsc --noEmit`, `cargo check`), the most common use of LSP for verification. It also skips the defaults that decide when diagnostics run at all (`diagnosticsOnWrite` true, `diagnosticsOnEdit` false). The ledger sentence is garbled and conflicts with the advice to treat empty diagnostics as evidence.
- **Organization:** Section 3's writethrough mechanism overlaps Ch10 (tools-and-execution.ts:255, 261), which already describes LSP sync/diagnostics at the write boundary. Let Ch16 own versioning and Ch10 keep a one-line pointer. Add a short section 'diagnostics를 직접 요청하기' covering the single-file, glob, and `file: "*"` workspace checks; it answers 'how do I verify?' before the timing subtleties. The config/reload paragraph (L935) belongs under configuration, not after the definition trace.
- **Ordering:** Section 1 intro → example setup → sequence → config paragraph. The config paragraph repeats the intro and interrupts the move to section 2; trim it and put it before the example. Moving diagnostics (section 3) up to second place would follow the reader's priority (verify edits before refactor tools), but the current order is acceptable.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: the `diagnostics` action itself (single file, glob up to 20 files, `file: "*"` running `cargo check`/`npx tsc --noEmit`/`pyright`/`go build`). This is the main LSP use for verification.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: defaults `lsp.diagnosticsOnWrite: true` and `lsp.diagnosticsOnEdit: false` (both revisions). Edits made with `edit` do not get automatic diagnostics by default, and section 3 implies they do ('파일을 연속해서 고치면').
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: custom linter clients (Biome, SwiftLint) take part only in diagnostics. `references` retries while the project loads when only the declaration came back.
- **Missing/stale:** Presentation: `lsp` is discoverable and mounted as `xd://lsp` under `tools.xdev`. At HEAD, docs/approval-mode.md says `write` to `xd://lsp` uses `tools.approval.lsp`.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD 3f000c52: diagnostics also use pull (`textDocument/diagnostic`) when supported, and a failed pull with no fresh publish is a failure, not `OK`. Externally changed files are reconciled from disk (`didChange`, stale diagnostics discarded) before queries. Project-aware single-file waits are 10 s. Workspace diagnostics run every detected language (up to 2 concurrent).

- P `tools-and-execution.ts:904` 「프로젝트의 언어 의미를 질의하고, 코드 변경과 같은 문서 버전의 진단·이름 변경을 연결하는 LSP 통합.」 — '언어 의미를 질의' is translationese. 'rename' is not tied to document versions, so the second clause is garbled. It is also a contents-list subtitle. ⇒ 정의와 참조를 찾고, 이름을 바꾸고, 고친 파일의 오류를 언어 서버에 묻는 기능.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:912` 「첫 요청이 오면 서버 프로세스나 공유 전송에 연결하고」 — '공유 전송' is opaque jargon for the `lsp.shared` broker. ⇒ 첫 요청이 오면 서버를 새로 띄우거나, 같은 프로젝트를 연 다른 omp 프로세스와 함께 쓰는 서버(`lsp.shared`)에 연결한 뒤 `initialize`를 마치고 파일을 엽니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:916` 「기대 결과는 선언 위치와 주변 원문이며, 문자열 검색으로 이름이 같은 문구를 모으는 것이 아닙니다.」 — The negative contrast adds nothing. Say what is returned and excluded. ⇒ 기대 결과는 `formatInvoice`가 선언된 파일과 줄, 그리고 그 앞뒤 한 줄입니다. 이름만 같은 주석이나 문자열은 결과에 들어가지 않습니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:935` 「설정을 바꾼 뒤에는 작업 공간 `reload`가 캐시를 비우고 서버 목록을 다시 만들며」 — '작업 공간 reload' is a literal rendering of 'workspace reload' and hides the call shape. The first sentence repeats L912. ⇒ OMP는 서버 설정을 작업 디렉터리별로 캐시합니다. 설정 파일을 고쳤다면 `lsp`를 `action: "reload", file: "*"`로 호출해야 새 설정이 적용됩니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:949` 「질의와 코드 변경의 경계를 드러낸다」 — An abstract title ('경계를 드러낸다'). ⇒ 읽기만 하는 요청과 파일을 바꾸는 요청
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:988` 「미리 보기와 적용 결과를 같은 메시지로 취급하지 말고, 하네스는 서버가 제안한 편집과 실제 기록된 파일 diff를 연결해야 합니다.」 — A vague directive. The concrete fact that matters (rename applies unless `apply: false`) is missing. ⇒ `rename`과 `rename_file`은 `apply: false`를 주지 않으면 바로 파일을 고칩니다. 바꿀 범위를 먼저 보려면 `apply: false`로 호출하고, 적용한 뒤에는 결과에 나온 변경 줄을 확인합니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1005` 「OMP의 쓰기 연동은 편집 전에 서버별 진단 스트림 버전을 기록하고, 새 문서를 동기화한 뒤 기대 문서 버전을 캡처해」 — A noun stack of translated terms ('쓰기 연동', '진단 스트림 버전', '기대 문서 버전을 캡처'). ⇒ OMP는 파일을 쓰기 전에 서버가 지금까지 보낸 진단의 번호를 적어 둡니다. 새 내용을 서버에 보낸 뒤에는 그보다 나중에 온 진단만 이번 편집의 결과로 받아들입니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1018` 「OMP의 진단 장부는 이미 보여 준 같은 오류를 다시 줄이고」 — '다시 줄이고' is garbled. `DiagnosticsLedger.reduce` drops messages already shown for that file (identity ignores the line:col prefix). An empty reduced result can therefore mean 'no new errors', which contradicts the next sentence about keeping empty diagnostics as evidence [INFERENCE on which result paths use the ledger]. ⇒ OMP는 같은 파일에서 이미 보여 준 오류를 다음 결과에서 빼고, 새로 생긴 오류만 보여 줍니다. 그래서 편집 뒤 결과에 오류가 없다고 해서 파일에 오류가 없는 것은 아닙니다. 남은 오류 전체를 보려면 `diagnostics`를 직접 호출합니다.
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:935` 「별도 서버 설정이 없을 때 OMP는 현재 작업 디렉터리의 루트 표식과 실행 파일을 함께 확인합니다.」 — It repeats L912, and it is stale at HEAD: there is no longer a separate 'no config' mode, and every server passes the same root-marker/binary/`disabled` filter (docs/lsp-config.md at 3f000c52). ⇒ Delete it, or replace it with: '설정을 덮어써도 루트 표식이 맞고 실행 파일이 설치된 서버만 씁니다.'
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:1018` 「늦은 진단에는 어떤 편집 뒤에 수집했는지 표시해야 모델이 과거 오류를 다시 고치지 않습니다.」 — This states as a requirement something OMP's version gate already handles. Whether the deferred delivery is labeled is not shown. ⇒ Drop it, or state what OMP actually does: deferred diagnostics arrive later as a separate message (writethrough `onDeferredDiagnostics`).
- I tool-sequence '심볼 위치를 언어 서버에 질의하기' (L919-932) — The relationship is useful (lazy start happens inside the first request). The self-loop '서버 선택' and the '서버 준비' return are padding. The distinctive OMP behaviour, `symbol` resolving the column on a 1-indexed `line` (required for `definition` on project-aware servers), appears only as '심볼 열 위치'. ⇒ Trim to 5 steps: call (`file`, `line: 42`, `symbol: "formatInvoice"`) → start + initialize (first use only) → `textDocument/definition` at the resolved column → `src/formatters/invoice.ts:18:1` → result with ±1 line.

The '조사' path pads with a skipped '파일 변경' stage. ⇒ Use three paths that differ by consequence: `rename` (default) → files changed immediately; `rename` + `apply: false` → preview only; `code_actions` → list, and only `apply: true` + `query` applies. Alternatively, a static table: action / approval tier / writes files?
- I exchange (L1008-1015) — The three 'outputs' are three different scenarios, not results of one input, and '느린 서버' is a separate condition. The v17 error label ('`number` 할당 오류') is ambiguous because v17 was the `string` version. ⇒ Use a tool-sequence (or two-path execution-path). Write v18 → a late publish for v17 arrives ("Type 'string' is not assignable to type 'number'") → discarded → publish for v18 → `OK`. A second path shows the slow server: write returns first, diagnostics arrive later.

## 17 디버거

- **Verdict:** keep. Adapter selection and transports, launch/attach, breakpoint sync, stop-state caching, and frame/scope/variable references are a distinct protocol and runtime with their own tool and approval tiers.
- **Overall:** The DAP facts are correct: one root session tree, child sessions via `startDebugging`, `scopes`/`evaluate` defaulting to the current frame, `variables` requiring a reference, capability-gated actions, `terminate` cleaning up the tree. The chapter has three structural problems. (1) It never defines DAP or a debug adapter; section 1 opens straight into a scenario. (2) The 24-step diagram draws every request as four relay hops and merges the debug tool with an HTTP 'execution tool'. It shows the stop event returning through the HTTP call, which misstates causality: a request paused at a breakpoint does not return until execution resumes. (3) The motivating bug (`qty` vs `quantity`) can be seen by reading the code, so the debugger looks unnecessary.
- **Organization:** Content is grouped as trace → lifecycle → inspection rules. The trace uses all the concepts that the later two sections define. Section 3's exchange lists action groups rather than results. The reproduction step depends on Ch12/Ch13 (running the server and sending a non-blocking request) but never says so.
- **Ordering:** Reorder. First, two sentences defining DAP (OMP ↔ adapter ↔ debugger; requests, events). Then launch vs attach (current section 2). Then the trace. Then inspection rules. Today `attach`, breakpoints, `frameId`, and `variablesReference` all appear in step details before they are explained.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: a definition of DAP and the debug adapter role (the equivalent of Ch16's L912 LSP intro).
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: approval tiers. Inspection actions (`stack_trace`, `scopes`, `variables`, `threads`, `output`, …) request read approval; all others request exec (docs/tools/debug.md).
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: `output` (captured program output), `pause`, `threads`, and conditional breakpoints (`condition`), which suit a loop over cart items. `evaluate` with the default `repl` context can forward raw debugger commands. Idle sessions are cleaned up after 10 minutes. The tool is `exclusive`.
- **Missing/stale:** Presentation: `debug` is discoverable and mounted as `xd://debug` under `tools.xdev` (default `true`).
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD 3f000c52: `pid` is sent as both DAP `pid` and `processId`, which affects the JS attach example. Delve picks `debug`/`exec` mode automatically, `skipAttachRequest` exists, and `output` now includes stdout from `runInTerminal` debuggees. The chapter's claims are not invalidated.

- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1081` 「디버거가 값을 관찰한 것과 편집기가 코드를 바꾼 것, HTTP 검사가 `24000`을 확인한 것을 각각 기록해야 합니다.」 — Nominalized '것' triplet with no named actor. ⇒ 값을 확인한 것은 디버거이고, 코드는 `edit`가 고치며, 고친 결과는 같은 HTTP 요청을 다시 보내 `24000`이 나오는지로 확인합니다.

- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1124` 「설치된 어댑터를 설정으로 해석합니다. 한 번에 하나의 루트 디버그 세션 트리를 유지하며」 — '설정으로 해석' is unclear, and '루트 디버그 세션 트리' is jargon without explanation. ⇒ OMP에는 GDB, LLDB, debugpy, Delve, JavaScript 디버거 등의 어댑터 정의가 들어 있고, 그중 실행 파일이 설치된 것만 고릅니다. 디버그 세션은 한 번에 하나만 열 수 있습니다. - P `tools-and-execution.ts:1155` 「하네스는 어댑터 기능 부족, 실행 중 대기 시간 초과, 대상 프로그램 종료를 서로 다른 결과로 남겨야 다음 행동을 선택할 수 있습니다.」 — OMP already distinguishes these (capability errors thrown; `details.timedOut`/`state: "running"`; terminated state). The directive restates the implementation as advice. ⇒ OMP는 이 셋을 다르게 돌려줍니다. 어댑터가 지원하지 않는 기능은 오류로 끝나고, 시간 초과는 `state: "running"`으로, 프로그램 종료는 종료 상태로 알립니다. 시간 초과라면 `pause`로 멈추거나 중단점을 다시 확인합니다.

- B `tools-and-execution.ts:1060` 「재현 준비 · 모델이 중단점이 활성화된 것을 확인합니다.」 — A padding step that adds no information beyond the previous '중단점 확인'. ⇒ Cut it (see interactive).
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:1045` 「구현은 `item.price * item.qty`를 더합니다.」 — With the handler code shown, a reader spots `qty` vs `quantity` without a debugger, which undermines the chapter's premise. ⇒ Choose a bug that only runtime values reveal. For example, a form parser delivers `price` as the string "12,000", so `Number(item.price)` is `NaN`; the handler code looks correct, and `variables` shows `item.price = "12,000"`.
- I tool-sequence '실행 중인 요청을 중단하고 잘못된 필드를 확인하기' (L1047-1078) — Twenty-four steps. Every DAP request is drawn as model→OMP→adapter→OMP→model, and the OMP hops only '전달합니다'. Actor 'OMP DAP·실행 도구' merges two separate tools (`debug` and bash/HTTP), and '디버그 어댑터·서비스' merges adapter and debuggee, which is the blurred-executor pattern n0023 warns about. Causality is wrong: the HTTP call is drawn as returning the stop event. In fact the request stays pending while the server is paused. The debug session caches the stop, and the model reads it via `stack_trace`/`threads` [INFERENCE: a foreground curl would block until its timeout, so it must run as a background job (Ch12)]. ⇒ Use 4 actors: 모델 / `debug` 도구 / `bash`(백그라운드) / 개발 서버+어댑터. About 9 steps: `attach` → `set_breakpoint checkout.js:27` → `bash` async `curl /total` → server stops, debug session records it → `stack_trace` (frame 18) → `scopes` → `variables` (shows the bad value) → `continue` (curl finishes with `null`) → after the fix, rerun shows `24000`.
- I execution-path '새 실행과 기존 실행에 연결하는 방법' (L1097-1121) — A genuine choice with a useful '적합한 대상' result. It omits the precondition that matters for the chapter's own example: attaching to a Node server requires it to have been started with an inspector (e.g. `node --inspect`) [INFERENCE about js-debug attach requirements]. ⇒ Keep it. Add the precondition to the attach path's first stage and link to Ch13's `hub start` for starting the dev server that way.
- I exchange (L1145-1152) — The input is a stop location; the 'outputs' are three menus of actions. This is prose dressed as a diagram. ⇒ Show actual outputs from that frame: `evaluate item.price * item.quantity` → `NaN`; `step_over` → stops at `checkout.js:28`; `variables` → the field values. Otherwise convert it to a list.
- R [tool-sequence ✗] TS ~L1048 실행 중인 요청을 중단하고 잘못된 필드를 확인하기 (24 steps; 3628px @1280 (~4 viewports) / 3257px @390) — The longest block in the guide. Steps 13–24 are three identical 4-hop queries (stack_trace, scopes, variables). Every model↔adapter hop is drawn twice. ⇒ Cut to ≤8 steps: attach → breakpoint → reproduce → stopped event → `variables` result `item.qty = undefined`. The stack/scopes/variables chain is already listed in EX L1145.
- R [execution-path ✗] EP ~L1097 새 실행과 기존 실행에 연결하는 방법 (2 tabs × 3; 603px / 744px) — launch vs attach is a two-row taxonomy. ⇒ Use a 2-row table (방식 / 지정 값 / 적합한 대상).
- R [exchange ✗] EX ~L1145 현재 중지 위치 → 실행 제어 / 프레임 조사 / 표현식 (3 outputs; 505px / 687px) — A menu of operations. ⇒ Use a table, or absorb it into the shortened TS.

## 18 웹 검색과 문서 가져오기

- **Verdict:** keep. Provider chain and fallback, query-directive translation, and URL rendering (site handlers, content negotiation, `llms.txt`, caching) are a distinct subsystem from file reading. The `read` URL section fits here rather than in Ch09.
- **Overall:** Snapshot facts hold: one-time directive parsing, sequential fallback that stops at the first renderable response, lenient post-filtering with a leading 'Note:', and `read` URL rendering with a 300-line/50 KiB default and cached range selectors. The running example is badly chosen: the agent web-searches its own OMP setting, even though OMP exposes its own docs internally (`omp://`) and the repo docs. The read step describes generic HTML extraction for a GitHub URL, which OMP routes to a site-specific GitHub handler. The provider-outcome diagram's first two paths end in the same next action. At HEAD, provider selection has moved to the `web` model role.
- **Organization:** The sections answer clear questions (search→read, provider differences, page bounds). Missing: trust. Fetched text is data, not instructions. Ch19 says this for pages (L1441), but retrieval is where it first applies.
- **Ordering:** Fine. The forward reference to Ch19 (L1301) is appropriate.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD 3f000c52: provider choice is now the `web` model role. Candidates are `web/<engine>` catalog models or chat models with declared grounding, replacing `providers.webSearchOrder`. Grounded chat answers without sources/citations are rejected (status 204), so the '답변과 출처' path needs that condition.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: site-specific handlers (GitHub, npm, PyPI, MDN, Stack Overflow, …) run before generic HTML extraction. `:raw` returns the original HTML.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: per-provider transport timeout `providers.webSearchTimeoutSeconds` (default 60 s, cap 300). It is not a whole-chain deadline.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: fetched and search text is untrusted input. Page text cannot grant new instructions; this should be stated here, where it first applies, not only in Ch19.
- **Missing/stale:** Presentation: `web_search` is discoverable and read-approved (docs/tools/web_search.md at HEAD), so under `tools.xdev` it is mounted as an `xd://` device unless requested top-level.

- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1173` 「검색 후보에서 원문 URL을 선택하고, 추출한 문서와 출처를 모델의 검증 가능한 근거로 전달하는 도구.」 — A noun-chain subtitle ('검증 가능한 근거로 전달하는 도구'). ⇒ 웹에서 문서를 찾고, 그 원문을 읽어 모델에게 넘기는 도구.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1181` 「웹 검색 결과의 URL과, 제공자가 돌려준 제목·발췌문이 있다면 어떤 문서를 읽을지 고르는 단서가 됩니다. … 관련성과 권위를 보고」 — Contorted conditional; '권위' is a literal rendering of 'authority'. ⇒ 검색 결과의 URL과 제목, 발췌문은 어떤 문서를 읽을지 고르는 단서입니다. … 모델은 질문과 관련 있는 공식 문서를 골라 `read`로 원문을 가져옵니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1185` 「이 값이 전경 대기 시간을 정한다는 원문입니다.」 — '전경' is a literal rendering of 'foreground'. ⇒ 명령을 백그라운드로 넘기기 전까지 기다리는 시간을 이 값이 정한다는 문장입니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1213` 「검색 발췌문만으로 세부 API 계약을 단정하지 않게 합니다.」 — 'API 계약' is translationese, and the first sentence of the paragraph restates the obvious. ⇒ 검색 결과의 요약이나 발췌문만 보고 API의 세부 동작을 단정하지 않습니다. 답변에는 실제로 읽은 원문의 URL을 붙입니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1227` 「검색 제공자의 차이를 결과에 남긴다」 — A vague title. ⇒ 제공자마다 다른 검색 조건 처리
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1270` 「제공자 전환은 순차적으로 이루어지며 첫 번째로 읽을 수 있는 응답에서 멈춥니다.」 — Noun subject plus '이루어지며'. ⇒ OMP는 제공자를 하나씩 차례로 시도하고, 내용이 있는 응답을 처음 받으면 멈춥니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1284` 「웹 응답을 읽을 수 있는 문서로 제한한다」 — '문서로 제한한다' is an unnatural collocation. ⇒ 웹 페이지를 읽을 만한 분량의 텍스트로 바꾸기
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1301` 「페이지를 모델 문맥에 넣는 일과 브라우저를 조작하는 일은 목적이 다릅니다.」 — A generic opener. The usable criterion comes only in the last sentence. ⇒ 로그인이 필요하거나, JavaScript가 그린 뒤에야 내용이 보이거나, 클릭·입력이 필요한 페이지만 다음 장의 브라우저로 엽니다. 나머지는 `read`로 충분합니다.
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:1252` 「독립 답변이 없으므로 하네스가 내용을 지어내지 않습니다.」 — A defensive non-step drawn as a skipped stage. ⇒ Delete the stage.
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:1185` 「OMP의 `bash.autoBackground.thresholdMs`를 조사한다고 합시다.」 — The agent would not need the web for its own setting: OMP documents itself through `omp://` (internal URL registry at 3b3a6dc9) and the docs ship with the repo. The example teaches an unrealistic step. ⇒ Use a third-party dependency question, e.g. 'Vitest의 `testTimeout` 기본값' → official docs URL → the rendered page shows the default value.
- I two tool-sequences (L1188-1210) — Four steps each; the split is reasonable. In the second, the extraction ('추출기를 적용') is drawn on the arrow to the web server, though OMP performs it. For a GitHub URL, OMP uses its GitHub site handler before generic extraction (packages/coding-agent/src/web/scrapers/github.ts; about 78 site scrapers at 3b3a6dc9). ⇒ Make extraction an OMP self-step ('사이트 전용 처리기 또는 HTML→Markdown 변환'). With the example changed (see bluff), show the header the model gets: `URL`, `Content-Type`, `Method: …`.
- I execution-path '한 검색 호출이 끝나는 방식' (L1234-1267) — The first two paths lead to the same next action. The input's `site:`/`after:` constraints never visibly change anything ('가능한 범위에서 후처리' is vague). Stages are padded. ⇒ Branch on how the constraint is handled: provider supports `site:` natively → filtered results; provider ignores it → OMP post-filters; post-filter would remove everything → constraint relaxed + leading `Note: no results matched …`; provider error → next provider; all fail → combined error.
- I exchange (L1291-1298) — Good. 1,240 lines → 300-line/50 KiB body and a `:301-600` follow-up makes the bound visible. ⇒ Keep. Replace '가능한 경우 아티팩트에 보관' with the actual reference the model sees, e.g. `artifact://<id>`.
- R [tool-sequence ×2 ✗] TS ~L1188 공식 문서 후보 찾기 + TS ~L1200 후보 URL에서 원문 읽기 (4 + 4 steps; 802+844px / 757+730px) — Two identically shaped call/result ladders in the same section. Labels '정규화된 결과', '읽을 수 있는 문서' are translationese. ⇒ Merge into one 6-step TS (search → pick URL → read), or replace both with two sentences.
- R [execution-path ✓] EP ~L1234 한 검색 호출이 끝나는 방식 (3 tabs × 3; 603px / 719px) — Real alternatives (answer+sources / sources only / failure). ⇒ Keep.
- R [exchange ✓] EX ~L1291 웹 응답 → 모델 본문 / 후속 범위 읽기 / 전체 자료 (3 outputs; 452px / 633px) — Acceptable decomposition. ⇒ Keep.

## 19 브라우저 자동화

- **Verdict:** keep. Live page state, element handles and their invalidation, observation modes, and ownership of headless vs attached browsers are distinct from static retrieval (Ch18) and desktop control (Ch45).
- **Overall:** Facts match docs/tools/browser.md: named tabs, observe ids and aria refs, handles invalidated by navigation, `tab.run` with one run per tab, the browser modes, `close` detaching rather than closing CDP/relay tabs, and the relay safety guidance. The chapter hides the most important OMP fact. `browser` is an Eval prelude, not a tool, and the docs' rule is 're-observe and act in the same Eval cell'. The 14-step sequence instead shows the model calling a 'browser facade' step by step, with a re-observation started by nobody. The authenticated example needs relay/CDP, which the chapter explains only in its last section.
- **Organization:** Three sections: observe/act, observation means, modes/relay. The relay internals paragraph (L1437) is a tangent for this chapter's question. HEAD-era controls that bound what the browser may do (`allowed_domains`, exec-tier approval) belong with the safety paragraph.
- **Ordering:** Move section 3 (headless vs login tabs) first, or switch section 1's example to a headless page. Section 1's '인증된 스테이징 계정' and '릴레이 또는 CDP 백엔드' rely on concepts defined only in section 3. The chapter also assumes Ch14 introduced Eval preludes, which it did not.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: `browser` is an Eval prelude (not an AgentTool) and exists only while Eval and `browser.enabled` are on. The model's actual call is `eval`.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing: the explicit same-cell rule from docs/tools/browser.md ('re-observe and act in the same Eval cell') and the recovery table (missing tab → `browser.open`; stale ref → re-observe; busy tab → await).
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD 3f000c52: all browser host operations request exec-tier approval, including inspection. HEAD adds `allowed_domains` per tab (aborts requests to other hosts; not a sandbox), `persist`/idle-close, and the Tern backend in the selection order. It also adds diagnostics (`console`, `errors`, `requests`) and `waitForText`, which directly support the 'verify after acting' point.
- **Missing/stale:** Missing link: Ch13's managed dev server (tools-and-execution.ts:542) is already set up as '브라우저 검사를 시작하기'. Ch19 should continue that example instead of starting a new scenario.

- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1320` 「이름 붙인 브라우저 탭의 DOM과 화면을 관찰하고, 인증된 세션을 포함한 실제 UI 상호작용을 검증하는 통합.」 — A noun-chain subtitle; '검증하는 통합' is translationese. ⇒ Eval 코드로 브라우저 탭을 열어 화면을 읽고, 클릭하고, 입력하는 기능.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1324` 「관찰한 요소에 행동하고 바뀐 화면을 다시 확인한다」 — '요소에 행동하고' is unnatural. ⇒ 찾은 요소를 조작하고, 바뀐 화면을 다시 읽기
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1328` 「브라우저 자동화에는 현재 페이지라는 살아 있는 상태가 있습니다. OMP의 Eval `browser` 파사드는」 — '살아 있는 상태' is a literal phrase, and '파사드' is unexplained jargon. The key fact (browser is accessed from `eval`, not as a tool) is only implied. ⇒ 브라우저 탭은 호출이 끝나도 열린 채로 남고, 그 사이 페이지 내용이 바뀔 수 있습니다. OMP에서는 별도 도구가 아니라 `eval` 셀 안의 `browser` 객체로 탭을 다룹니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1358` 「하네스는 관찰과 행동을 가깝게 실행하고, UI가 바뀐 뒤 새 관찰에서 요소를 다시 찾아야 합니다. 클릭 호출의 성공만 기록하지 말고」 — '가깝게' is vague (the docs say same Eval cell), the harness is named instead of the model, and the closing sentence is a negative qualification. ⇒ 그래서 요소를 찾는 `observe`와 그 요소를 누르는 `click`은 같은 Eval 셀에서 실행하고, 화면이 바뀌면 다시 `observe`해 새 ID를 받습니다. 작업이 끝났는지는 저장 뒤 다시 읽은 배송지 값과 확인 배너로 판단합니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1407` 「하네스는 실행을 직렬화하고 다시 관찰할 복구 경로를 제공해야 합니다.」 — Translationese ('직렬화', '복구 경로'). OMP already enforces one run per tab. ⇒ 같은 탭에 다음 실행을 보내려면 앞 실행이 끝나기를 기다립니다. 시간 초과 뒤에는 워커가 바뀌어 기존 요소 핸들이 무효가 될 수 있으므로 다시 `observe`합니다.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1424` 「cmux 표면 중 하나를 선택합니다.」 — 'cmux 표면' is unexplained. ⇒ Drop cmux from this list, or explain it: '(cmux 터미널 앱 안의 웹 뷰)'.
- P `tools-and-execution.ts:1441` 「스크린이나 웹 페이지의 문구는 새로운 권한을 부여하지 않습니다.」 — '스크린' is odd, and '권한을 부여하지 않는다' is abstract. ⇒ 웹 페이지에 적힌 지시는 사용자의 요청이 아닙니다. 페이지가 시키는 대로 행동 범위를 넓히지 않습니다.
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:1437` 「서버가 CDP 탐색 엔드포인트와 `Target.*` 계층을 제공하고, 한 탭에 허용되는 하나의 `chrome.debugger` 연결 위로 여러 탭 워커 연결을 다중화합니다.」 — True, but it is relay internals that answer no question this chapter raises. ⇒ Shorten to: '`browser-relay`는 Chrome 확장과 로컬 서버로 이루어져, 사용자가 이미 로그인한 실제 탭을 OMP가 조작할 수 있게 합니다. `app.target`에 URL이나 제목 일부를 주면 그 탭을 고릅니다.'
- B `tools-and-execution.ts:1390` 「전체 페이지나 특정 요소를 실제 렌더링 해상도로 저장합니다.」 — It omits the consequence that matters: the screenshot is saved to a file and, unless `silent: true`, also returned to the model as an Eval image. ⇒ '전체 페이지나 특정 요소를 파일로 저장하고, `silent: true`가 아니면 이미지로 모델에게도 보여 줍니다.'
- I tool-sequence '인증된 탭에서 값을 바꾸고 결과를 검증하기' (L1335-1355) — Fourteen steps with triple relays (tab→facade→model) per observation. Actor 'OMP 브라우저 파사드' hides that the model sends JS through `eval`. Step 12 '다시 관찰' is started by the facade with no model call, so who decided is unclear. This contradicts the same-cell rule the chapter should teach. ⇒ Use 3 actors: 모델 / Eval 셀(`browser`) / 탭, and 2 calls. Call 1: `browser.open({name:"profile", app:{relay:true, target:…}})` + `observe()` → refs + current value "서울 중구". Call 2, one cell: `fill` → `click` → `waitForText("저장되었습니다")` → re-read value → returns `{banner, value:"서울 종로구"}`. About 6 steps.
- I execution-path '무엇을 확인할지에 따라 관찰 수단을 고른다' (L1374-1404) — The three 'paths' are not alternative executions. The input asks for all three things at once, and the result field is '적합한 질문'. It is a comparison table in tabs. ⇒ Make it a static table (수단 / 돌려받는 것 / 맞는 질문), or an exchange over one page showing the concrete `ariaSnapshot` excerpt, a screenshot, and the `tab.run` array.
- I exchange (L1427-1434) — The input names two different tasks, and the outputs are mode descriptions. The real consequence, who owns the browser and what `close` does, sits in the paragraph above. ⇒ Use a small table: 모드 / 브라우저 소유 / `close`의 효과 / 로그인 상태 사용. Rows: 헤드리스 (OMP, 탭 닫힘, 아니오), CDP (외부, 연결만 해제, 예), 릴레이 (사용자 Chrome, 연결만 해제, 예).
- R [tool-sequence ✓] TS ~L1335 인증된 탭에서 값을 바꾸고 결과를 검증하기 (14 steps; 2276px / 2044px) — Observe → act → re-observe is the point, but 4-hop rounds inflate it. The chip `#browser-profile` repeats ×14. ⇒ Cut to ~7 steps: 탭 열기 → 관찰('서울 중구') → 입력·저장 → 재관찰('서울 종로구'). Drop the chips.
- R [execution-path ✗] EP ~L1374 무엇을 확인할지에 따라 관찰 수단을 고른다 (3 tabs × 2; 513px / 629px) — A taxonomy (question → tool). ⇒ Use a 3-row table: 질문 / 수단.
- R [exchange ✗] EX ~L1427 브라우저 작업의 요구 → 헤드리스 / CDP / 릴레이 (3 outputs; 503px / 710px) — A taxonomy of backends. ⇒ Use a table: 백엔드 / 소유자 / 로그인 상태.

### Range patterns (ch 14–19)

- Noun-chain subtitles that list contents and end in '~하는 도구/통합/런타임'. 5 of 6 chapters: Ch14 L650 '…연결하는 런타임', Ch16 L904 '…연결하는 LSP 통합', Ch19 L1320 '…검증하는 통합' (also Ch17 L1037, Ch18 L1173). This is the subtitle pattern the user corrected in Ch01 (t0014). Rewrite each as one plain sentence about what the feature does.
- Formulaic paragraph-ending directives 'X 하네스는 …해야 합니다'. 13 occurrences across the 6 chapters. Many either name the wrong actor because the model chooses or acts (L813, L1358, L1441) or prescribe what OMP already does (L848, L1155, L1407). State the OMP behaviour and the reader's next action instead.
- Relay-hop padding in tool-sequences. Each round trip is drawn model→OMP→backend→OMP→model, and the OMP hops say only '전달합니다/반환합니다'. Ch17 has 24 steps, Ch19 14, Ch15 9, Ch14 8, Ch16 7. Collapse the relays, and keep a hop only when OMP adds information (validation, caching, version gating).
- Merged actors that blur execution boundaries (n0023 lesson): 'OMP DAP·실행 도구' (two tools), '디버그 어댑터·서비스' (adapter + debuggee), '유지 커널·도구 브리지', 'OMP ast_edit·write', 'OMP read·fetch'. Ch17's merger produces a causality error (the stop event returns via the HTTP call).
- execution-path/exchange used for lists or comparison tables rather than alternative outcomes from one input: Ch14 exchange L709, Ch17 exchange L1145, Ch19 execution-path L1374 and exchange L1427, Ch18 L1234 (paths 1-2 share an outcome). Padded `skipped` stages that state non-events appear in Ch15 L805, Ch16 L962, Ch18 L1252.
- Abstract or literal section titles: '출력 채널과 커널 수명을 따로 관리한다', '질의와 코드 변경의 경계를 드러낸다', '프로그램과 디버그 어댑터의 수명을 조립한다', '웹 응답을 읽을 수 있는 문서로 제한한다', '관찰한 요소에 행동하고…'.
- Literal translations of English technical terms where ordinary Korean exists: '사전 로드'(prelude), '전경'(foreground), '권위'(authority), 'API 계약'(contract), '실시간 재실행'(live run), '공유 전송'(shared transport), '쓰기 연동'(writethrough), '파사드'(facade), '직렬화/복구 경로'.
- Examples with weak motivation: Ch17's bug is visible in the handler code, so the debugger is unnecessary. Ch18 web-searches OMP's own setting, which OMP can read via `omp://`. Both violate n0023's 'choose alternatives with an observable consequence'.
- Cross-chapter: Ch07 (tool-definitions-and-registry.ts:119) teaches that `ast_edit` is mounted as an `xd://` device and called through `write` (`tools.xdev` defaults to `true` at 3b3a6dc9 and HEAD). Ch15–Ch18 draw `ast_edit`, `lsp`, `debug`, and `web_search` as direct top-level calls. Pick one convention: either show the device call once in Ch15 with a back-reference to Ch07, or note in Ch07 that later chapters draw the logical call.
- Cross-chapter: Ch10 (tools-and-execution.ts:255, 261) and Ch16 section 3 both describe LSP sync and diagnostics at the write boundary, citing the same `writethrough.ts`. Ch16 should own version gating and deferred delivery; Ch10 should keep one sentence and a pointer. Both currently omit `diagnosticsOnEdit: false`.
- Cross-chapter: Ch14 must introduce Eval preludes because Ch19's whole API (`browser`) is one. Ch14 should also point to Ch36 for `agent()`/`workpool()` and kernel-defined tools, which are part of eval's surface but are subagent mechanisms.
- Cross-chapter: Ch12 and Ch13 set up exactly what Ch17 and Ch19 need. Ch17's reproduction request must run as a background job (Ch12) because a request paused at a breakpoint blocks; its attach target is a managed dev server (Ch13, started with an inspector). Ch13's sequence (L542) is already titled '개발 서버가 준비된 뒤 브라우저 검사를 시작하기', so Ch19 should continue that example instead of starting a new one.
- Cross-chapter: Ch15's `write` to `xd://resolve` is labeled '적용 승인', which collides with Ch08's approval terminology. The proposal/resolve mechanism is shared with plan mode's `xd://propose` (chapters-33-46.ts:42) and should be introduced once (Ch07 or Ch10) with consistent wording ('확정/거부').
- Cross-chapter: Untrusted-content guidance appears only in Ch19 (L1441). It applies first to Ch18's search and fetch results and to Ch14's tool-bridge outputs; state it once in Ch18 and refer back from Ch19.
- Cross-chapter: Ch14 L757 and Ch08 cover the same approval-gap fact (`bash.patterns` vs `tools.approval.eval`). Keep the full explanation in Ch08 and a one-line reminder with the setting name in Ch14.

## 20 모델 제공자

- **Verdict:** keep. `packages/ai` adapters, schema normalization, compat routing and error flags form a large distinct mechanism. Ch22 should be folded into this chapter, not the other way round (see ch22).
- **Overall:** The adapter idea is correct, but the chapter does not show the adapter doing anything observable. The schema example has nothing to normalize. The exchange describes three formats in prose (ch02 already shows real shapes). The execution-path compares a real path with an invented 'ignore compat' path and uses the wrong flag (`disableStrictTools` applies only to Anthropic). The error section credits the adapter with classification that the session actually performs. The most important provider mechanism is missing: replaying stored history to a different provider (`transformMessages`).
- **Organization:** The four sections answer distinct questions: common format, schema conversion, endpoint compat, errors. Two blocks are misplaced: L81 is about call-ID round-tripping and registry/approval (ch02/ch07/ch08 material) inside the schema section, and the L126 result repeats the error section. After merging ch22, add one section on how responses come back as events.
- **Ordering:** L18 and L99 depend on ch21 concepts: the model record, `api`, `baseUrl`, `compat`, and 'the catalog's compatibility rules' (L99). Putting ch21 (catalog) before ch20 would introduce the model record before it is used. Within the chapter, strict mode is mentioned at L61 but not explained until the execution-path at L102; the order is otherwise fine.
- **Missing/stale:** Cross-provider history replay is missing. `transformMessages()` normalizes tool-call IDs (Responses `call_id|item_id` composites, `MAX_TOOL_CALL_ID_LENGTH`), strips or neutralizes other providers' thinking signatures, drops malformed tool calls, and redacts credential-shaped tokens. This is why a session can switch models mid-conversation, and it is the main reason the common message type exists. The chapter links transform-messages.ts but never explains it.
- **Missing/stale:** Adapters place prompt-cache markers (`cache_control` appears 16 times in anthropic.ts; `prompt_cache_key` appears in openai-responses.ts). This is the ch01 caching topic applied to adapters.
- **Missing/stale:** Retries are split between two layers: the provider retries empty completions, strict-tool rejections, and Codex websocket→SSE failures before any replay-unsafe output; the session runs its own auto-retry. The chapter does not draw this line.
- **Missing/stale:** The retry link points to session-maintenance.ts, but the classification and retry decisions are in agent-session.ts and turn-recovery.ts.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD 3f000c52: `streamSimple()` now also resolves credentials and applies provider in-flight limits and thinking-loop guards; schema normalization moved to `packages/ai/src/utils/schema/normalize.ts`; Apple Foundation Models was added; OpenRouter now defaults to Responses.

- P `chapters-20-32.ts:18` 「현재 모델의 `api` 값을 확인하면 `packages/ai`의 스트림 함수가 … 어댑터를 고릅니다. 이 경계 덕분에 루프는…」 — The subject of 확인하면 is unclear, and 이 경계 덕분에 is an abstract noun standing in for the actor. ⇒ `packages/ai`의 `streamSimple()`은 현재 모델의 `api` 값(`anthropic-messages`, `openai-responses`, `google-generative-ai` 등)을 보고 맞는 어댑터를 호출합니다. 그래서 에이전트 루프는 제공자별 HTTP 본문을 만들지 않고 같은 메시지와 도구 정의를 넘깁니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:43` 「세션과 화면은 이 공통 이벤트를 구독하므로 모델을 바꾸어도 에이전트 루프의 도구 실행 계약은 유지됩니다.」 — '도구 실행 계약은 유지됩니다' is vague. It is also slightly inaccurate: the session and UI subscribe to agent events that wrap these provider events, not to the provider events directly. ⇒ 에이전트 루프는 이 공통 이벤트만 읽으므로, 모델을 바꿔도 도구 호출을 찾아 실행하는 코드는 바뀌지 않습니다.

- P `chapters-20-32.ts:95` 「같은 API 이름만으로는 충분하지 않다」 — The section title is a negative qualification, not a subject. ⇒ 엔드포인트마다 다른 호환성 설정
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:146` 「어댑터는 HTTP 실패와 스트림 오류를 제공자 응답의 원문으로 끝내지 않고 … 오류로 분류합니다.」 — Wrong actor plus a translated construction. Adapters emit an `error` event whose assistant message carries `stopReason: "error"` and the raw `errorMessage`. `AgentSession` and `turn-recovery.ts` classify it with `AIError.classifyMessage()` (verified at the 3b3a6dc9 snapshot, agent-session.ts:3355 and turn-recovery.ts:1177). ⇒ 어댑터는 실패를 `stopReason: "error"`와 원문 `errorMessage`를 가진 `assistant` 메시지로 돌려줍니다. 세션은 `AIError.classifyMessage()`로 이 메시지를 인증 실패, 사용량 제한, 컨텍스트 초과, 일시적 전송 오류로 나눕니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:150` 「컨텍스트 길이 오류는 입력 예산의 실패입니다」 — Noun-heavy ('입력 예산의 실패'). ⇒ 컨텍스트 길이 오류는 입력이 모델 한도를 넘었다는 뜻입니다
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:150` 「경계는 분명해야 합니다.」 — A generic claim that only restates the section's point. ⇒ delete
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:150` 「모두 “모델 호출 실패”로만 기록하면 재시도해도 해결되지 않는 요청을 반복하거나…」 — A negative hypothetical in place of the actual decision table. ⇒ Replace with: "세션은 `401`이면 다른 자격 증명으로 바꾸고, 컨텍스트 초과면 압축(25장)을 시작하고, 일시적 전송 오류만 지연 후 다시 보냅니다."
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:81` 「스키마 변환은 전송 형식의 호환성을 맡고, 이름 조회·승인·실행은 세션의 도구 레지스트리가 맡습니다.」 — A boundary statement that belongs to ch07/ch08 (approval is not the registry's job), placed in a section about schema conversion. ⇒ delete
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:122-126` 「기본 제공자 주소나 잘못된 경로를 사용할 수 있습니다 … 게이트웨이가 지원하지 않는 엄격 스키마를 거부할 수 있습니다」 — Describes a path OMP never takes, and states the failure more confidently than the source supports. For `openai-completions`, OMP automatically retries without strict tools after a rejection and remembers that for the session (`shouldRetryWithoutStrictTools` / `disableStrictToolsForScope` in openai-completions.ts; provider-streaming-internals.md: 'capability fallbacks such as retrying without rejected strict-tool fields'). `disableStrictTools` is wired only for Anthropic (catalog compat/axes.ts:193; models.md: 'disables the Anthropic `strict` marker'). For `openai-completions` the relevant field is `compat.supportsStrictMode`. ⇒ Replace the counterfactual path with real OMP behavior (see the interactive fix).
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:126` 「하네스는 이 실패를 로컬 도구 오류가 아니라 제공자 요청 오류로 다뤄야 합니다.」 — Duplicates the error section. ⇒ delete

Ch02 already shows the OpenAI and Anthropic shapes concretely. ⇒ Show one stored tool call replayed to two targets as wire fragments, e.g. OpenAI `function_call` with `call_id: "call_x|fc_y"` and Anthropic `tool_use` with a normalized `id`, to make `transformMessages` ID normalization visible. Alternatively cut the block and reference ch02.
- I code L64 '하네스가 보유한 도구 스키마' — The schema contains nothing that any provider normalizes, so it demonstrates no conversion. The paragraph after it talks about call IDs, not about the schema. ⇒ Make it an exchange with an input schema that uses `oneOf` and `pattern`. Show outputs for OpenAI Responses (`anyOf`, empty `properties` added, regex removed) and Google (keywords moved into `description`), following docs/ai-schema-normalize.md.
- I execution-path L102 '사내 OpenAI 호환 게이트웨이…' — The comparison is between a real path and a fictional 'ignore compat' path, using the wrong flag. ⇒ Use a real two-case comparison. Option 1: `compat.supportsStrictMode: false` (no `strict` field is sent) versus auto (strict is sent → 400 → automatic retry without strict, remembered for the session; the observable cost is one extra round trip). Option 2: a static exchange showing that `maxTokensField: max_tokens` and `max_completion_tokens` produce different request bodies.

The user sentence is inside a code span, so it renders as spaced monospace Hangul ('스 크 립 트'). At 390px, the third card is left alone in a 148px two-column grid. ⇒ Show the actual converted snippets (`tool_use` / `function_call` / `functionCall`). Move the Korean sentence out of backticks.

Its blocked stages are hedged ('…할 수 있습니다'). ⇒ Replace it with a real contrast (`disableStrictTools` false vs true, with the gateway's actual rejection), or delete it.

## 21 모델 카탈로그

- **Verdict:** keep. Separate code: `packages/catalog`, model-registry.ts (merge, discovery, availability) and model-resolver.ts (selectors, roles, initial model). Ch20's adapters consume its output.
- **Overall:** The mechanism content is largely accurate (getAll/getAvailable, merge order, tiny→smol, initial-selection order all verified). However, both interactive blocks restate the surrounding prose, the note box is generic and partly wrong about context promotion, the YAML is never walked through, and the role list is stale at HEAD. Context promotion is a catalog field that ch25 relies on, but no chapter explains it.
- **Organization:** The sections map to clear questions: what identifies a model, where the list comes from, what metadata constrains, and how roles resolve. Availability should live only here; ch23 L459 repeats it. Context promotion (`contextPromotionTarget`) should be defined here, since ch25 uses it.
- **Ordering:** This chapter should come before ch20, because ch20 already uses the model record, `api`, `baseUrl` and `compat`. Inside the chapter, the `:minimal`/`:high` suffixes and `"@slow"` chaining appear in the YAML (L285) without any explanation.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD: chat roles now include `memory` (resolving through `@tiny`), and there are new model-kind roles `image`, `web`, `speech`, `dictation`, `judge`. `getAll()`/`getAvailable()` return chat models only by default. Availability neither runs secret commands nor refreshes OAuth (docs/models.md at HEAD lines 542-608).
- **Missing/stale:** Context promotion (`contextPromotionTarget`, temporary `model_change`, saved roles left unchanged) is not explained but is used by ch25 L734.
- **Missing/stale:** Switching models mid-session is missing: `/model` and role edits, temporary versus saved, and the `model_change` entry with `role` that ch24's reconstruction replays.
- **Missing/stale:** Thinking selectors (`:minimal`…`:high`) and the `thinking` metadata are not explained.
- **Missing/stale:** The shared remote catalog refresh (models.md 'Shared catalog refresh') is not covered.

Drop the staleness sentence.

## 22 응답 스트리밍

- **Verdict:** merge → 20 모델 제공자 (1–2 sections). Consumer-side material stays in 04/06; the persistence boundary stays in 24.. The same provider stream functions that ch20 describes produce this event stream (provider-streaming-internals.md step 2), and ch20 L43 already introduces `AssistantMessageEvent`. Of the four sections, 1 and 4 duplicate ch04 L152-169 and ch24 L700, section 2 duplicates ch06 L36, and section 3 duplicates ch06 L91-92 plus ch24 replay. The distinct material left (terminal reason mapping, partial-JSON parsing, watchdogs/provider retry) fits in one or two sections. If the chapter is kept standalone, it must be rebuilt around TTSR, the stream guards, and the watchdogs.
- **Overall:** The event contract is stated accurately: block triplets, `done`/`error` reasons, `end`/`fail`, no merging, no backpressure. But almost every harness-facing point repeats ch04 (message_update, text_delta, EventController batching), ch06 (toolcall_start/delta/end, stop reasons), or ch03/ch24 (partial text is not persisted). The mechanisms that make streaming a separate harness concern are missing: watchdogs, provider retry before replay-unsafe output, partial-JSON recovery, TTSR, and the streaming edit guard. Thinking events are omitted entirely.
- **Organization:** Sections 1 and 4 both cover delivery and batching. Section 3 mixes terminal-event semantics with session replay filtering (`buildSessionContext` dropping aborted turns), which is ch24 material.
- **Ordering:** It uses ch04/ch06 terms without referring back to them. '합성 `toolResult`' and '모델 재생 문맥' (L391) appear without any setup.
- **Missing/stale:** `thinking_start/delta/end` is missing, even though ch01 discusses reasoning tokens.
- **Missing/stale:** Watchdogs are missing: first-progress and idle; the synthetic `start` does not count; `trackLocalWork()`. At HEAD: Anthropic and the OpenAI family own their watchdogs, and a watchdog abort stays `error` rather than `aborted`.
- **Missing/stale:** Provider-owned bounded retry is missing: it is allowed only before replay-unsafe output; Codex websocket→SSE fallback; no resumption from a malformed chunk.
- **Missing/stale:** Session consumers of deltas are missing: TTSR (aborts mid-stream on a rule match, injects the rule, retries; persisted as `ttsr_injection`) and the streaming edit guard on `edit` toolcall deltas. These are the main harness uses of streaming.
- **Missing/stale:** Partial-JSON details are missing: RelaxedJson fallback, `{}` on failure, throttled re-parse (256-byte steps at the snapshot, a geometric gate at HEAD).
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD: `message_update` now carries read-only snapshots. Stop-reason mapping has grown (Anthropic `model_context_window_exceeded`→`length`; OpenAI `content_filter`→`error`, not `length`).

- P `chapters-20-32.ts:387` 「`done`은 정상적인 스트림 종단이며」 — '종단' is a literal translation of 'terminal'. ⇒ `done`은 응답이 정상적으로 끝났음을 알리는 마지막 이벤트이며
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:391` 「세션은 실패한 트랜스크립트 항목으로 남길 수 있습니다. … 화면·감사 기록과 모델 재생 문맥을 분리합니다.」 — Noun-heavy and translated in style ('트랜스크립트 항목', '모델 재생 문맥'). It also belongs to ch24. ⇒ 중단된 응답도 세션 파일에는 남습니다. 다음 요청을 만들 때 `buildSessionContext()`는 이 응답과 짝을 이룬 도구 결과를 빼고 보냅니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:370` 「이 차이는 어댑터가 흡수합니다. 하네스가 지켜야 할 조건은 … 넘기는 것입니다.」 — Uses the '~하는 것입니다' pattern and replaces the actual mechanism with an obligation. ⇒ 어댑터는 조각이 올 때마다 누적 문자열을 관대한 JSON 파서로 다시 읽습니다. 중간 `arguments`는 `{}`이거나 일부만 채워질 수 있고, `toolcall_end`에서 한 번 더 파싱한 값이 최종 인자가 됩니다.
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:333` 「최종 메시지 조립과 UI 갱신 주기를 같은 버퍼로 묶어서는 안 됩니다.」 — A prescriptive negative with no decision behind it, and a duplicate of ch04 L169. ⇒ delete
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:415` 「그래서 하네스는 첫 진행과 유휴 시간을 감시하고」 — The causality is false. The watchdogs detect stalled providers and have nothing to do with the lack of backpressure (provider-streaming-internals.md step 4). ⇒ Split into two facts: "제공자가 멈추면 첫 이벤트·유휴 시간 감시가 요청을 오류로 끝냅니다." / "느린 소비자는 제공자를 늦추지 않고 메모리 큐만 늘립니다."
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:419` 「이 분리로 사용자는 빠르게 텍스트를 보면서도 … 저장 비용도 커집니다.」 — A fourth copy of the persistence boundary (ch03 L128, ch04 L63, ch24 L700). '저장 비용도 커집니다' is speculative. ⇒ delete (keep it only in ch24)
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:394-396` 「`end()`와 `fail()`도 결과를 끝내야 합니다」 — An `EventStream` API guarantee aimed at adapter authors, with no harness decision for this reader. The title is prescriptive while the text is descriptive. ⇒ Shorten to one clause in the event-contract paragraph, or delete.
- I exchange L322 (조각 "테"→"스트"… → 에이전트 상태/세션 이벤트/완료 메시지) — A sequence over time is drawn as a fan-out. It also duplicates the ch04 exchange at L155-160 (텍스트 표시/도구 진행/완료). ⇒ Delete it. If kept after the merge, replace it with a timeline where each chunk produces a `text_delta` and the growing message snapshot, ending with `text_end`/`done` → `message_end`.

Only one delta is shown even though the concept is accumulation. The last two steps (validate/execute/result) belong to ch02/ch06. ⇒ Use four rows: `toolcall_start` (id, `read`) → `toolcall_delta` `{"path":"pack` (parsed: partial, not executable) → `toolcall_delta` `age.json"}` → `toolcall_end` `{"path":"package.json"}`. End with 'the loop dispatches' (ch06).

The self-events (chunk received) use kind `call`, the model-call colour, and the chip `#call_7` repeats ×7. ⇒ Keep. Use `result` for chunk arrival and drop the repeated chips.

## 23 인증과 자격 증명

- **Verdict:** keep. A large distinct subsystem: the `packages/ai` auth store, ranking/rotation, OAuth refresh, broker/gateway, plus coding-agent secret obfuscation.
- **Overall:** The precedence order, `/login`, the SQLite `agent.db` store, the broker sentinel, `omp config list` masking and the secrets placeholders all match the source. Problems: a section title states the opposite of what the broker does; the OAuth tool-sequence is a linear happy path; the secrets execution-path presents two sequential halves as alternatives; the outputs include defensive negations; the key design point, session-sticky credentials kept for prompt-cache reuse, is reduced to a vague sentence.
- **Organization:** The questions are clear: which credential, refresh, where tokens live, exposure. Section 4 combines two separate mechanisms (credential masking in settings output, and obfuscation of conversation secrets in `coding-agent/src/secrets`); give each its own lead sentence. L459 (`disabledProviders`, keyless local) repeats ch21.
- **Ordering:** The broker section depends on refresh, so the order is fine. The precedence list (L446) names sources such as 'login-sourced API key' and 'other stored key' before the reader knows how each is created.
- **Missing/stale:** Session-sticky credential selection is missing: start index from an xxHash of the session ID; ranking is skipped while the Anthropic prompt cache is warm. Also missing: the `credential_pin` session entry used on resume.
- **Missing/stale:** Usage-limit blocking is missing: `blockedUntil`, usage-ranked rotation across accounts, blocks persisted across processes.
- **Missing/stale:** There is a separate redaction path in `packages/ai` (`configureCredentialRedaction` in transformMessages) that the chapter does not distinguish from `secrets.enabled`.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD: auth-storage.ts was split into `packages/ai/src/auth/*` (select, rotation, refresh, pool, affinity, health), so the cited path is outdated. Precedence has a new 'extension config fallback' slot (eight entries). `omp login` works outside a session. `streamSimple()` resolves credentials. Secrets gained Anthropic-token and connection-URL password patterns plus native Responses replay obfuscation, and the code moved to `secrets/message-transform.ts`.

- P `chapters-20-32.ts:446` 「자격 증명은 제공자별로 해결됩니다. Anthropic에 로그인해도 OpenAI 요청이 인증되지는 않습니다.」 — '해결' is a literal translation of 'resolve', and the second sentence is an obvious negative. ⇒ 자격 증명은 제공자마다 따로 찾습니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:477` 「OAuth 행에는 액세스 토큰, 갱신 토큰, 만료 시각과 계정 범위가 들어갈 수 있습니다.」 — '행' is database jargon, and '계정 범위' is unclear. ⇒ 저장된 OAuth 자격 증명에는 액세스 토큰, 갱신 토큰, 만료 시각, 계정 정보가 들어 있습니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:493` 「세션에 어느 계정을 고정할지와 토큰 자체의 수명은 별개의 상태입니다.」 — '별개의 상태' is abstract and leaves out the actual mechanism. ⇒ OMP는 세션 ID별로 마지막에 쓴 계정을 기억해 같은 세션이 같은 계정을 계속 쓰게 합니다. 계정이 바뀌면 그 계정에서 프롬프트 캐시를 다시 채워야 하기 때문입니다. 재개할 때는 세션의 `credential_pin` 항목으로 같은 계정을 다시 고릅니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:507` 「토큰을 실행 호스트 안에 가둔다」 — Factually inverted. The broker moves refresh tokens off the execution host (laptop or container) onto a broker host (auth-broker-gateway.md: 'move OAuth refresh tokens … off developer laptops'). ⇒ 원격 환경에서는 브로커가 갱신 토큰을 맡는다
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:511` 「클라이언트에는 갱신 토큰 대신 센티널이 든 스냅샷을 제공합니다」 — '센티널' is unexplained jargon. ⇒ 클라이언트가 받는 스냅샷에는 갱신 토큰 자리에 `REMOTE_REFRESH_SENTINEL`이라는 표시값만 들어 있습니다
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:532` 「원문으로 노출할 이유가 없습니다 … 다른 표시 계약을 가집니다」 — Two vague phrases where the source gives a precise rule. ⇒ `omp config list`는 자격 증명 값을 `********`로 가리고, `--json`에서는 값을 빼고 `redacted: true`를 붙입니다. 키 하나를 지정하는 `omp config get <key>`는 값을 그대로 출력합니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:536` 「도구 결과나 파일 내용에 우연히 등장한 비밀은 인증 저장소만으로 보호되지 않습니다.」 — A 'X만으로는' negative qualification. ⇒ 파일이나 명령 출력에 섞인 비밀은 `secrets.enabled`로 따로 가립니다.
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:459` 「`disabledProviders`는 이 탐색보다 먼저 적용됩니다. … Ollama 같은 키 없는 로컬 제공자는…」 — Duplicates ch21 L212 and its execution-path. ⇒ Delete, or keep a single cross-reference clause to ch21.
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:453-454` 「모델 메시지나 도구 인자에는 넣지 않음 … 원문 토큰을 대화 메시지로 저장하지 않음」 — Defensive negatives that state the obvious; they add no decision. ⇒ Delete both outputs.
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:515` 「이 경계도 네트워크 보안을 대신하지 않습니다.」 — A defensive disclaimer. ⇒ "브로커·게이트웨이 사이의 TLS나 사설망, bearer 토큰은 운영자가 직접 설정합니다."
- I exchange L449 (`openai` 요청 직전) — The case is useful: a user who set `OPENAI_API_KEY` sees it lose to stored OAuth. But two of the three outputs are negations. ⇒ Use an execution-path with three configurations: OAuth plus env key → OAuth; env key only → env; custom gateway with `models.yml` `apiKey` plus OAuth → the config key wins, so the upstream OAuth token is not forwarded to the proxy (providers.md rationale).
- I tool-sequence L480 '만료가 가까운 OAuth로 모델을 호출할 때' — A four-step linear happy path that restates L477. The prompt is unrelated to the event chain. ⇒ Add the branch with consequences: refresh succeeds, versus refresh definitively rejected → credential disabled (`credential_disabled`) → next ranked account, or a `/login` prompt when none is left. Alternatively show a mid-request 401 → `rotateSessionCredential` → retry.
- I execution-path L539 '로그 파일에 API 키가 들어 있을 때' — The two tabs ('제공자에게 보냄' / '후속 로컬 도구를 실행함') are consecutive halves of one flow, not alternatives. ⇒ Use a tool-sequence: `read` result containing the key → obfuscated to `$$…$$` → the provider sees the placeholder → the model calls `bash` with the placeholder → the harness restores it → the tool runs. Use `replace` mode as the contrasting case where nothing is restored.
- R [exchange ✗] EX L449 openai 요청 직전 → 선택 / 전송 / 기록 (3 outputs; 502px / 634px) — Sequential stages (select → send → record) drawn as parallel outputs. ⇒ Use a numbered static list, or a single-path stage list.
- R [tool-sequence ✗] TS L480 만료가 가까운 OAuth로 모델을 호출할 때 (4 steps; 643px / 569px) — A trivial refresh round trip. The credential lookup uses kind `request` (the model-request colour). ⇒ Replace it with one sentence.
- R [execution-path ✗] EP L539 로그 파일에 API 키가 들어 있을 때 (2 tabs × 3; 604px / 746px; tabs wrap to 2 rows) — The two tabs are the outbound and inbound halves of one round trip, and both happen. Tabs hide the continuity. choose='데이터가 이동하는 곳'. ⇒ Use one TS: read result → 치환 → model → placeholder in arguments → 복원 → local tool.

## 24 세션 저장과 재개

- **Verdict:** keep. A distinct mechanism: SessionManager, entry taxonomy, `buildSessionContext`, resume/fork operations, storage abstraction and write pipeline.
- **Overall:** This is the strongest chapter in the range. The JSONL tree example and the saved-tree versus model-input execution-path match the catalogue scope. Weaknesses: the branch example has no setup (which user action created `u3`?); there are abstract closing sentences and tangents; the claim that nothing is ever deleted is too strong; the persistence boundary is a fourth copy of material in ch03/04/22; crash-resume handling and branch summaries are missing.
- **Organization:** Good grouping: file format, reconstruction, resume/fork, durability. Make this the only home for 'partial text is not persisted' and delete the copies in ch03 L128, ch04 L63 and ch22 L419.
- **Ordering:** Explain how a branch is created (`/tree` navigation, then sending a different request) before the JSONL at L592. L589 says '논리적인 첫 항목' without explaining why ('logical'), and the physical first line is a 256-byte title slot.
- **Missing/stale:** Branch creation (`/tree`) and `branch_summary` are missing: the abandoned branch is summarized when switching, controlled by `branchSummary.enabled` (default false; compaction.md 'Branch summarization pipeline').
- **Missing/stale:** Resume after a crash is missing: the `session_exit` and `tool_execution_start` custom entries; on resume, a synthetic `stopReason: "aborted"` assistant message is appended so an interrupted turn is not shown as still live (session.md L265-271).
- **Missing/stale:** Runtime state replayed along the path (`model_change` with role, `thinking_level_change`, `mode_change`, `credential_pin`) is only hinted at.
- **Missing/stale:** Size controls are missing: 500k-character truncation and blob externalization of images to `blob:sha256:`.
- **Missing/stale:** Session discovery and `--continue` per-terminal breadcrumbs are missing.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD: session IDs are UUIDv7; there is a new `model_usage` entry; `session-index.ts` (history.db) and `exit-diagnostics.ts`; the docs now describe `leafId` as an in-memory pointer and say that targeted rewrite/discard helpers can change existing records.

- P `chapters-20-32.ts:604` 「현재 잎을 가리키는 `leafId`만 옮깁니다. 새 메시지는 선택한 잎을 부모로 삼아」 — '잎' is a literal translation of 'leaf'. ⇒ 현재 위치를 가리키는 `leafId`만 옮깁니다. 새 메시지는 선택한 항목 뒤에 붙고
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:627` 「현재 `leafId = u3`」 — Presented as part of the saved file, but `leafId` is not stored. On load, the leaf falls back to the last entry (session.md step 1; at HEAD: 'in-memory pointer'). ⇒ 마지막으로 기록된 항목: `u3`
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:628` 「읽는 표면」 — '표면' is an abstract noun. ⇒ 읽는 쪽
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:652` 「방출 범위가 더 좁아집니다」 — '방출' is a literal translation of 'emission'. ⇒ 모델에 보내는 범위가 더 좁아집니다
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:665` 「재개는 열고, 포크는 새 정체성을 만든다」 — '정체성' is a literal translation of 'identity'. ⇒ 재개는 같은 파일에 이어 쓰고, 포크는 새 파일을 만든다 (or noun form: 재개와 포크)
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:681` 「빈 정체성과 빈 대화에서 시작」 — Same issue. ⇒ 새 ID와 빈 대화로 시작
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:696` 「완료된 항목을 내구성의 경계로 삼는다」 — Abstract nouns ('내구성의 경계'). ⇒ 완료된 메시지부터 파일에 남는다
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:652` 「전체 기록을 보존하는 일과 다음 호출의 컨텍스트를 구성하는 일은 같은 연산이 아닙니다. 하네스는 … 분리해야 분기·압축·초기화를 되돌릴 수 있습니다.」 — The first sentence restates the section title and the execution-path. The second is generic, and '되돌릴 수 있습니다' overclaims for compaction [INFERENCE: no source shows compaction being undone]. ⇒ delete both sentences
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:669` 「세션 전환 중 오류가 나면 캡처해 둔 이전 상태를 복구합니다.」 — A tangent to the resume-versus-fork question. ⇒ delete

Mention in ch25 that pruning blanks old tool results in place.

- I execution-path L625 'r1 뒤에서 B 분기를 선택한 경우' — The comparison is right (saved tree versus model input), but the stages are padded ('표시: 필요하면 두 분기를 모두 탐색할 수 있습니다' says nothing). ⇒ Use a static tree with the active path highlighted, or two stages per tab. A better variable: selecting `u2` versus `u3` changes which request reaches the model.
- I exchange L676 (재개/포크/새 세션) — Works: one input, three outcomes with observable file and ID differences. ⇒ Keep it; only fix the '정체성' wording.
- R [execution-path ✗] EP L625 r1 뒤에서 B 분기를 선택한 경우 (2 tabs × 3; 631px / 669px) — Two readers of the same file, both true at once. choose='읽는 표면' (abstract noun). ⇒ Use a static tree (like ch02's conversation-history) with the active path u1→a1→r1→u3 highlighted and u2 greyed.
- R [exchange ✗] EX L676 원본 세션 → 재개 / 포크 / 새 세션 (3 outputs; 478px / 634px) — '새 세션' is not derived from the input. ⇒ Use a 3-row table: 작업 / 파일 / ID / 기록.

## 25 컨텍스트 압축

- **Verdict:** keep. Substantial distinct machinery: triggers, cut points, split turns, summary generation, a five-method pipeline, async compaction, pruning.
- **Overall:** Cut-point rules, `CompactionEntry` fields, the trigger list and the default method order are accurate for the snapshot. Problems: the budget/threshold rule (in catalogue scope as 'context budgets') is never given; the trigger sentence lists six items in one breath; the strategy paragraph name-drops five methods without their preconditions; the pruning description is partly inaccurate and contradicts the execution-path's claim that the original entries remain; context promotion is used without ever being introduced; incomplete-output recovery is stale at HEAD.
- **Organization:** Section 4 bundles three questions: method choice, failure, and extension hooks. The hooks belong in ch31. Per-turn pruning (`supersedeReads`, `dropUseless`) runs every turn and deserves its own short subsection rather than being folded into cut-point selection.
- **Ordering:** '임계값' (threshold, L734) is used but never defined. Context promotion (L734) is assumed from ch21, which does not explain it. The default `methodOrder` puts the LLM summary ('soft') last, yet the chapter explains it first and the other methods only in the final paragraph. Introduce the method order, then trace the summary method.
- **Missing/stale:** The budget rule is missing: `reserveTokens` uses a 16384-token floor and at least 15% of the window; `thresholdPercent`/`thresholdTokens`; `keepRecentTokens` is 20000 (compaction.md 'Settings and defaults').
- **Missing/stale:** Async speculative compaction is missing (`compaction.asyncEnabled`, default true): a background summary starts in the band just below the threshold and is committed instantly when the threshold is crossed.
- **Missing/stale:** Per-turn pruning is missing (`supersedeReads`, `dropUseless` → `[Uneventful result elided]`), with its prompt-cache-aware timing.
- **Missing/stale:** Context promotion is never defined here or in ch21.
- **Missing/stale:** Branch summarization (also in compaction.md) needs a home, in ch24 or here.
- **Missing/stale:** The display transcript is mentioned only in the note at L809 (`buildSessionContext({ transcript: true })`).
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD: incomplete-output recovery may keep the truncated turn and only warn when context is below the threshold; `CompactionEntry` gains `method`, `tokensAfter` and `providerReplayThroughEntryId`; a 'maintenance progress guard' was added; settings moved to `session/context-settings.ts`.

- P `chapters-20-32.ts:734` 「압축은 사용자의 `/compact`, 컨텍스트 초과 복구, 출력 길이 제한으로 끝난 응답의 복구, 성공한 턴 뒤의 임계값 검사, 도구 루프 중간의 안전한 경계, 유휴 유지 관리에서 시작될 수 있습니다.」 — A dense six-item list with no grouping by cause. ⇒ 압축은 세 경우에 시작됩니다. 사용자가 `/compact`를 실행할 때, 요청이 실패한 뒤(컨텍스트 초과, `length`로 잘린 응답), 그리고 유지 관리 검사에서(턴이 끝난 뒤, 도구 반복 중간, 유휴 시간)입니다.

- P `chapters-20-32.ts:758` 「압축 뒤 보는 표면」 — '표면' is an abstract noun. ⇒ 압축 뒤 읽는 쪽

- P `chapters-20-32.ts:823` 「압축 방식이 달라도 같은 경계를 지킨다」 — '경계' is an abstract noun. ⇒ 압축 방식과 실패 처리
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:730` 「세션 기록은 계속 늘지만 모델은 한 번에 유한한 컨텍스트만 읽습니다.」 — Restates ch01. ⇒ delete
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:782` 「오래된 검색 결과나 뒤의 읽기로 대체된 출력은 자리표시자로 줄입니다. 이 단계도 저장 항목의 호출·결과 짝을 유지합니다.」 — '오래된 검색 결과' is inaccurate: pruning targets results flagged as useless (zero-match search, a `wait` cap) and superseded reads. It also hides that pruning rewrites the stored session (compaction.md: 'session storage is rewritten'). ⇒ "뒤의 `read`로 대체된 출력과, 결과가 없던 검색처럼 쓸모없다고 표시된 결과는 `[Output truncated - N tokens]` 같은 자리표시자로 바뀝니다. 이때 세션 파일의 해당 결과도 같이 바뀌지만 호출·결과 짝은 남습니다."
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:827` 「방식마다 보존 표현은 다르지만」 — Vague. The decision-relevant facts are the preconditions. ⇒ Replace with the preconditions: `remote` needs a Responses-family provider; `snapcompact` needs an image-input model; `handoff` is skipped for overflow; `shake` swaps tool results for `artifact://` links (ch27); `soft` is the LLM summary.

The source lists per-path error messages and says overflow is excluded from the generic retry. ⇒ "자동 압축이 실패하면 경로별 오류(`Context overflow recovery failed: …` 등)를 내고, 컨텍스트 초과는 일반 재시도 대상에서 뺍니다." Move the hooks sentence to ch31.
