The JSONL tab's '원래 항목이 남습니다' is false whenever pruning ran. ⇒ State the window (e.g. 128k) and the threshold derived from `reserveTokens`, and show the result as summary plus 20k. Better still, show a before/after fixed-scale occupancy bar like ch01's context window, mirroring compaction.md's 'What the LLM sees' figure. Add 'pruned results are blanked in place' to the JSONL tab.
- R [execution-path ✗] EP L755 100k 토큰 대화에서 최근 20k를 남기는 예 (2 tabs × 3; 603px / 694px) — Both tabs are true at once. The numbers (60k/20k/20k → 80k summarised) have no proportional figure. choose='압축 뒤 보는 표면'. ⇒ Use a before/after token bar on a stable 100k scale (reuse the ch01 context-window component), with a note that the JSONL keeps the originals.

### Range patterns (ch 20–25)

- Section-title register (24 of 24 in ch20–25): every section title is a plain declarative '-다' sentence ('에이전트는 공통 형식으로 요청한다', '도구 인자는 완성될 때까지 조립한다', '완료된 항목을 내구성의 경계로 삼는다'). Body text and note titles use 합니다체 ('카탈로그 값은 정책 입력입니다', '`end()`와 `fail()`도 결과를 끝내야 합니다'). The reviewed ch01/ch02 use noun phrases or '-기' ('입력에서 출력으로', '질문을 요청으로 구성하기', '이전 대화도 이번 입력에 포함하기'). Convert titles to that form.
- Abstract nouns in text fields (lines 1–844): '경계' 16 occurrences (L18 '이 경계 덕분에', L150 '경계는 분명해야', L696 '내구성의 경계', L823 '같은 경계를 지킨다'), '상태' 12 (L493 '별개의 상태'), '경로' 13 (literal and fine in ch24's tree walk, abstract at L122/L150), '정체성' 4 (L169, L177, L665, L681), '계약' 4 (L43 '도구 실행 계약', L532 '다른 표시 계약'), and '해결' as 'resolve' 6 (L212, L446, L515).
- Generic closing 'harness must…' sentences with no decision, about 7: L126, L150 ('경계는 분명해야'), L190, L261, L333, L370, L652. Delete them or replace with the concrete rule.
- Negative qualifications and defensive negations, about 8: L95 title, L150, L177 '잃지 않습니다', L446, L453–454 exchange outputs, L515, L536 '…만으로 보호되지 않습니다'.
- Execution-path misuse in 4 of 6 blocks: a counterfactual path OMP never takes (L102); sequential halves shown as alternatives (L539); padded restatement of the preceding paragraph (L215, L625). One tool-sequence pads with self-events that duplicate the next arrow (L354). The two exchanges at L179 and L322 present a table or a timeline as a fan-out.
- Jargon used without setup: '결합자' L61, '센티널' L511, '합성 `toolResult`'/'모델 재생 문맥' L391, '간접 참조' L279, '방출 범위' L652, '메시지 열' L752, 'context promotion' L261/L734.
- Claims attributed to the wrong layer or stated more strongly than the source supports: adapter 'classifies' errors (L146; the session does); `disableStrictTools` on `openai-completions` (L106); broker 'confines tokens in the execution host' (L507); 'nothing is deleted' (L604, L763); '무한 재시도를 막습니다' (L831).
- Cross-chapter: Ch22's independence: merge it into ch20. Every consumer-side point already exists elsewhere: `message_update`/`text_delta`/EventController batching in ch04 L152-169 (ch22 L320-333, L415); toolcall start/delta/end and execution waiting in ch06 L36 (ch22 L351-370); `toolUse`/`length`/`error`/`aborted` in ch06 L91-92 (ch22 L387); 'partial text is not persisted' in ch03 L128, ch04 L63 and ch24 L700 (ch22 L419). The adapter-side contract comes from the same provider functions as ch20. If 22 stays standalone, rebuild it around watchdogs, provider retry before replay-unsafe output, TTSR and the streaming edit guard, which no other chapter covers.
- Cross-chapter: Placement of 'model access' (20–23) after tools (09–19) is defensible, so keep it. No chapter in 09–19 depends on 20–23; ch10's streamed edit preview and ch18's search 'providers' are self-contained, though ch18 uses '제공자' for search backends, a terminology clash worth noting. Ch24–25 consume 20–21 directly (overflow classification, `contextWindow`, promotion), so keeping 20–23 next to 24–25 helps. The catalogue's introductory route skips 20–23 (Shell → Session storage). The cost: ch04 L21/L73 and ch06 L36/L91 refer forward to ModelRegistry, auth storage, stop reasons and toolcall deltas roughly 14 chapters ahead. 20–23 should refer back to ch02/ch04/ch06 instead of re-explaining them. Moving the part directly after core runtime (after 08) would also work, but it separates 20–21 from 25.
- Cross-chapter: Within the part, put 21 (catalog) before 20 (providers): ch20 L18/L99 already depends on the model record, `api`, `baseUrl` and `compat`, and ch21 L177 is the natural opener for the part.
- Cross-chapter: Duplication to resolve: availability is in both ch21 L212+L215 and ch23 L459 (keep ch21); initial model selection is in both ch04 L73 and ch21 L293 (keep ch21 and have ch04 refer to it); the persistence boundary appears four times (keep ch24 L700).
- Cross-chapter: Context promotion is cited in ch21 (L261, L268) and ch25 (L734) but defined nowhere; define it in ch21.
- Cross-chapter: Content that belongs elsewhere: compaction hooks (ch25 L831) → ch31; SnapCompact detail (ch25 L827) → a one-line pointer to ch43; `shake` → `artifact://` should link to ch27; replay filtering of aborted turns (ch22 L391) → ch24.
- Cross-chapter: Cross-links that should be made explicit: ch20 errors → ch25 overflow trigger; ch23 credential stickiness → ch24's `credential_pin` on resume; ch21 `model_change` → ch24 reconstruction.
- Cross-chapter: Citation hygiene: ch20 L157 cites session-maintenance.ts for retry and classification, but the logic is in agent-session.ts and turn-recovery.ts. Ch23 cites auth-storage.ts, which was split into `packages/ai/src/auth/*` at HEAD. All links are pinned to the snapshot, so they still resolve, but HEAD behavior differs in the places listed under missingOrStale.

## 26 세션 간 메모리

- **Verdict:** keep. The mechanism is distinct and none of it appears in 24/25: backend selection, retention cadence, recall query composition, injection budget, and memory:// reads/edits.
- **Overall:** The sources are correct, and the opening three-way comparison (session record / compaction summary / long-term memory) is useful. But the chapter is organized by backend rather than by the questions a reader asks (when memory is written, how it comes back, how to correct it). Its only sequence uses Mnemopi before Mnemopi is introduced and stops before the model acts on the memory. It also leaves out two mechanisms with harness consequences: where injected memory lands (the system prompt) and the stripping of recalled blocks from retention. Recurring literal phrasing: '내구성'.
- **Organization:** §3 (Mnemopi) and §4 (local) are backend tours, so retain/recall concepts end up split across §2, §3 and §4. Regroup by question: (1) keep the session/compaction/memory contrast; (2) when memory is written — `local` extracts offline from finished sessions at a later startup, while Mnemopi/Hindsight retain the transcript every N turns plus explicit `retain`/`learn`. The backend list at 871 becomes the lead of this section; (3) how it returns — first-turn recall query (request + recent turns), `recallLimit`/`injectionTokenLimit`, injection location, trust rule; (4) correcting it — preview vs `read memory://<id>` before `memory_edit update`, `/memory` commands.
- **Ordering:** The sequence title at 888 ('Mnemopi에 보존한 규칙…') uses Mnemopi before §3 introduces it. `read memory://<memory-id>` (938, link 910) relies on internal URLs, which ch27 introduces later, so ch26 depends on ch27. Swap 26/27 or add a forward reference. Backward references to ch24/25 (활성 경로, 압축) at 858 are fine.
- **Missing/stale:** Injection location: recalled `<memories>` blocks go into the system prompt (packages/coding-agent/src/hindsight/content.ts comment; Mnemopi 'refreshes the base prompt'), and `local` injects a 'Memory Guidance' block (prompts/memories/read-path.md). The chapter says only '첫 모델 입력'. Link to ch05 and to caching: per docs/memory.md, `learn` affects only later sessions, so the active prompt-cache prefix is unchanged.
- **Missing/stale:** Anti-feedback: recalled `<memories>`/`<mental_models>` blocks are stripped from the retention transcript so memories are not re-retained as new memories (packages/coding-agent/src/hindsight/content.ts). This is a real harness design rule.
- **Missing/stale:** Retention cadence and recall query: `mnemopi.retainEveryNTurns` (4), Hindsight every 3 user turns, `recallContextTurns`, `recallMaxQueryChars`.
- **Missing/stale:** Subagents alias the parent's memory state and run no retain loop of their own.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD (docs/memory.md): `memory://root/...` resolves only with `memory.backend: local`. Mnemopi LLM work now uses the `memory` model role instead of tiny/smol. `retain`/`learn` accept `scope: "global"` under global/per-project-tagged scoping.

- P `chapters-20-32.ts:866 (also 900, 942, link 949)` 「응답 필드 규칙과 근거를 내구성 있는 지식으로 보관」 — '내구성 있는' is a literal rendering of 'durable'; it recurs 3 more times ('내구성 있는 사실', '더 강한 처리 경계', '종료와 내구성 경계'). ⇒ 응답 필드 규칙과 근거를 다른 세션에서도 쓸 지식으로 저장
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:871` 「세션은 공통 메모리 인터페이스를 통해 주입·회상·종료 처리를 요청합니다」 — '~를 통해' plus a stacked noun list; the sentence never says what the uniform interface buys. ⇒ 세션은 어느 백엔드를 고르든 같은 메모리 인터페이스로 회상, 주입, 종료 처리를 맡깁니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:942` 「종료 시에는 현재 턴의 보존과 진행 중 추출을 제한된 시간 동안 비웁니다. 더 강한 처리 경계가 필요하면」 — '보존을 비웁니다' is an unnatural collocation (literal 'flush retention'). '더 강한 처리 경계' is opaque. The time bound is vague, though the source gives 1.5 s. ⇒ 종료할 때는 현재 대화를 저장하고, 이미 시작된 추출이 끝나기를 1.5초까지 기다립니다. 마지막 몇 턴까지 확실히 정리하려면 종료 전에 `/memory enqueue`를 실행합니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:964` 「최근에 아직 실행 중인 세션, 너무 오래된 세션, 비영속 세션과 하위 에이전트 세션은 처리 대상에서 빠질 수 있습니다.」 — '최근에 아직 실행 중인' is awkward, and '빠질 수 있습니다' hedges where docs/memory.md gives exact rules (minRolloutIdleHours 12, maxRolloutAgeDays 30; the pipeline skips subagents and non-persisted sessions). ⇒ 최근 12시간 안에 쓰인 세션, 30일보다 오래된 세션, 파일로 저장되지 않은 세션과 하위 에이전트 세션은 추출하지 않습니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:922` 「로컬 Mnemopi SQLite 은행을 엽니다」 — '은행' is a literal translation of 'bank'. ⇒ 로컬 Mnemopi SQLite 메모리 뱅크(bank)를 엽니다
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:900 and sequence step 892` 「자동 보존은 모든 문장을 그대로 복사하는 기능이 아닙니다.」 — This is a negative qualification, and it is wrong for Mnemopi/Hindsight: docs/mnemosyne-memory-backend.md says Mnemopi 'Retains completed conversation turns' and 'writes a marker-free transcript projection'; fact extraction happens afterwards. Only `local` extracts instead of storing turns. Step 892 ('재사용할 규칙과 근거를 저장합니다') makes the same overclaim. ⇒ Replace with a backend-specific positive rule: 'Mnemopi는 `retainEveryNTurns`(기본 4턴)마다 완료된 대화를 저장하고, 사실 추출과 통합은 그 뒤에 따로 합니다. `local`은 대화를 저장하지 않고 다음 시작 때 지난 세션에서 결정과 제약만 뽑습니다.' Fix step 892 to '완료된 턴의 대화를 저장합니다'.
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:942` 「자동 회상 결과가 `<memories>` 블록으로 들어갈 수 있고, 압축 때도 백엔드가 추가 문맥을 제공할 수 있습니다」 — Hedged '수 있습니다' where the conditions are known: `mnemopi.autoRecall` defaults to true, and compaction asks the backend for `preCompactionContext`. ⇒ State the conditions: '`autoRecall`이 켜져 있으면(기본값) 첫 턴에 회상 결과가 들어가고, 압축할 때는 백엔드가 회상한 메모리를 추가 문맥으로 넘깁니다.'
- I exchange 861-868 (세션 기록 / 압축 요약 / 장기 메모리) — The concept is right (one fact, three retention layers), but the outputs are prose descriptions, not what each layer actually holds, so nothing is observable. ⇒ Show the same fact as each layer stores it: a JSONL message entry fragment, the compaction summary line, and a memory row preview with its ID. Or convert to a static table with columns 저장 위치 / 언제 다시 읽나.
- I tool-sequence 886-897 (Mnemopi에 보존한 규칙이 새 세션 입력에 들어가기까지) — Setup is missing: the prompt is session B's request, but step 1 is an earlier session A action. There is no model actor, the final self-loop '첫 모델 입력' is not an exchange between actors, and the consequence (the model uses snake_case and checks it against code) never appears. ⇒ Put session A's fact in the setup and add a 모델 actor. End with the model writing `created_at` and reading the existing serializer to confirm. Alternatively cut to three steps (retain → recall → system-prompt injection) and show the literal `<memories>` block text (mnemopi/state.ts:1004) as a code block.
- R [exchange ✓] EX L861 세션 A에서 확인한 사실 → 세션 기록 / 압축 요약 / 장기 메모리 (3 outputs; 477px / 633px) — A comparison table rather than a transform, but the contrast is useful. ⇒ Use a 3-row table: 저장 위치 / 수명 / 사용 시점.
- R [tool-sequence ✓] TS L887 Mnemopi에 보존한 규칙이 새 세션 입력에 들어가기까지 (4 steps; 666px / 614px) — No model actor, so step 4 is a self `request` (2→2). '보존' uses `call`. ⇒ Use actors 세션 A / Mnemopi / 세션 B 모델 입력, or turn it into an exchange (회상 결과 → `<memories>` 블록).

## 27 아티팩트와 내부 URL

- **Verdict:** keep. Session-scoped artifact storage, ID allocation, and the internal URL router form a distinct subsystem. It needs to grow to cover the router (or be retitled to 큰 출력과 아티팩트); otherwise memory://, skill://, agent:// and mcp:// stay scattered and unexplained.
- **Overall:** §1 correctly answers the catalogue's key question (what enters the model context first), but through an exchange that never shows the actual preview text. The 'internal URL' half of the title is almost absent: the router and its schemes are never introduced, although ch26 and ch30 already rely on memory:// and skill://. The blob material duplicates ch24. One limits paragraph is dense and uses transliterations (백잉, 물질화). §4 is partly stale at HEAD, where omp now records the failure itself.
- **Organization:** §3 mixes artifact://, agent:// and blob. Blob is session-persistence of images, and ch24:673 already covers its fork behavior. Move blob to ch24 and keep one contrasting sentence here. Add a router section before §2: scheme → handler → resolve/locate; immutable (artifact, skill, memory, agent) vs writable (local://, vault://); the same URLs work in `read`, `grep`, and (at HEAD) the bash URL filesystem. Keep §4 (failure) and update it.
- **Ordering:** §2 explains ID allocation and resume scanning before the reader has seen why IDs matter; put the read example first. Internal URLs should be introduced here before ch26 uses memory://, so this chapter should come before ch26.
- **Missing/stale:** The internal URL router and scheme list are never introduced. Snapshot router.ts has agent, artifact, history, issue/pr, local, mcp, memory, omp, rule, security, skill, ssh, vault, xd; HEAD adds attachment://, cfg://, conflict:// (docs/tools/read.md 'Internal URLs'). Also missing: writable vs immutable schemes, and `locate` letting grep and the bash URL filesystem work on artifacts of any size.
- **Missing/stale:** Other artifact producers: URL read cache (tools/read.ts), gh tools (tools/gh-common.ts:339), and compaction `shake`, which leaves `recover: artifact://N` placeholders (session/session-maintenance.ts:745). The last one links directly to ch25.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: `OutputSink` moved to packages/tui/src/tools/streaming-output.ts. Artifact files are capped at `tools.artifactMaxBytes` (default 16 MiB: 3 MiB head + rolling tail), so '정제된 전체 스트림' is no longer literally complete. Writes are atomic via a temp file (`writeArtifact`). Capture failures are surfaced as warnings.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD agent:// semantics: a slash path is always JSON extraction, and unpublished agents resolve to a progress document.

- P `chapters-20-32.ts:990` 「출력이 임계값을 넘거나 한 줄의 열 제한으로 일부를 버려야 하면, 세션에 파일 기반 아티팩트 경로가 있을 때 정제된 전체 스트림을 아티팩트 파일에도 씁니다.」 — Literal ('열 제한으로 일부를 버려야', '정제된 전체 스트림'); the threshold is unnamed, though the source gives 50KB. ⇒ 출력이 50KB를 넘거나 너무 긴 줄을 잘라야 하면, `OutputSink`는 제어 문자를 정리한 전체 출력을 세션의 아티팩트 파일에도 씁니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1024` 「백잉 파일에서 필요한 줄만 스트리밍하고 … 프로토콜 수준에서 전체 리소스를 물질화할 때는 8 MiB 상한」 — Transliterated jargon ('백잉 파일' ×2, '물질화'), and a limits dump whose second consumer ('다른 소비자') is never identified. ⇒ 범위를 지정한 읽기(`artifact://7:120-180`)는 파일 크기와 관계없이 그 줄만 읽습니다. 범위 없는 `:raw` 읽기는 50 KiB를 넘으면 거부되므로, 큰 출력은 범위를 지정하거나 `grep`으로 찾습니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1056` 「저장 체계별 정체성과 수명을 지켜야 포크나 재개 뒤에 같은 주소가 다른 데이터를 가리키지 않습니다.」 — '정체성' is literal for 'identity'; this is an abstract moral after a concrete fact. ⇒ 그래서 포크 뒤에도 `artifact://7`은 원래 세션에서 복사한 같은 로그를 가리키고, 새 출력은 `8`부터 번호를 받습니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1039 (section title)` 「텍스트 출력과 바이너리의 수명을 구분한다」 — The section also covers agent:// (Markdown reports), so 'text vs binary' misdescribes it. ⇒ 세 가지 저장 주소는 범위와 수명이 다르다
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1078` 「“전체 출력이 나중에 다시 읽을 수 있다”」 — Grammar: the subject/object case is wrong. ⇒ “전체 출력을 나중에 다시 읽을 수 있다”
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:1078` 「하네스는 “도구가 끝났다”와 “전체 출력이 나중에 다시 읽을 수 있다”를 따로 기록해야 합니다.」 — Generic normative advice. At HEAD omp already does this: `dump()` returns `artifactError` and the tool metadata and terminal warning say the full output was not saved (docs/blob-artifact-architecture.md §3). ⇒ Describe omp's behavior instead: '저장에 실패하면 결과에 `artifactId`가 없고, 전체 출력이 저장되지 않았다는 경고가 붙습니다.'

No actual preview text is shown, which is exactly what the catalogue asks to show. ⇒ Convert to a 4-step tool-sequence: bash → OutputSink → artifact file; tool result to the model with the real marker `[raw output: artifact://7]` (session/streaming-output.ts:677); model → `read artifact://7:120-180` → only those lines return. Or show the literal truncated tool result as a code block.

## 28 체크포인트와 되돌리기

- **Verdict:** demote-to-section → 24 세션 저장과 재개 (new section on branch moves that leave a summary). The only distinct mechanism is one in-memory checkpoint state, the yield guard, application deferred to `turn_end`, and rehydration from the checkpoint tool-result entry. Everything else is `branchWithSummary()`, a leaf move, and `buildSessionContext()`, which ch24 already teaches (604, 622). ch24 lists `branch_summary` as an entry type (589) but never shows what creates one; `/tree` navigation with summarize (docs/tree.md) and `rewind` are the two producers, and one ch24 section can cover both plus the 'files are not restored' contrast. Overlap with ch25 is conceptual (shrinking context), not mechanical; keep a cross-reference only. At HEAD `/rewind` is also an alias of `/branch` (docs/tree.md:233; `doubleEscapeAction: rewind`), so a standalone chapter titled 되돌리기 collides with the user-facing transcript rewind. Merging lets one section tell the two apart.
- **Overall:** The content is accurate, and the catalogue's requested comparison (conversation vs working tree after rewind) is present. But the feature is a thin, disabled-by-default tool pair built entirely on ch24's tree mechanism, and about half the chapter states what it does not restore. The 8-step sequence pads with the generic tool loop. Demote it to a section.
- **Organization:** 'Files are not captured' is said four times: 1103 last sentence, the exchange at 1110, 1170, and 1190-1192. Keep it once, in the §3 comparison. §4's second paragraph (1217) repeats the deferred application already explained at 1132.
- **Ordering:** §1 opens with the config flag ('기본으로 꺼져 있으며 … 켜야 등록됩니다') before saying what problem the tool solves. Start with the use case (a long exploration you want out of context, keeping only the conclusion), then mention the flag briefly.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD (docs/tools/checkpoint.md): `checkpointEntryId` is backfilled with the checkpoint tool-result entry's own ID, and a hidden `checkpoint-active-reminder` steering message is queued and later removed by the rewind cut.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD (docs/tools/rewind.md step 9): completed sibling tool calls/results from the same assistant batch are reparented onto the retained branch. The sequence's '긴 탐색 로그 대신 체크포인트 이전 대화와 보고서' needs that qualifier.
- **Missing/stale:** Fallback: if the checkpoint entry no longer resolves, rewind branches from root and logs a warning.
- **Missing/stale:** Subagents get the pair only through an explicit tools list, and requesting one auto-includes the other.
- **Missing/stale:** Rewind also resets advisor state, resyncs todos, and closes provider sessions whose history was rewritten. This ties to ch01 caching: rewriting history invalidates provider-side state.

- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1095 (description)` 「조사 전 대화 경계를 표시하고 탐색 분기를 짧은 보고서로 접어 다음 모델 입력을 되돌리는 기능.」 — '다음 모델 입력을 되돌리는' is unnatural, and the abstract '경계' obscures the action. ⇒ 조사를 시작한 대화 위치로 돌아가, 조사 기록 대신 짧은 보고서만 남기는 기능.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1152` 「사용자는 세션 트리에서 버린 탐색 분기를 감사할 수 있습니다」 — '감사' is ambiguous (audit vs thanks); ch25:767 has the same problem ('감사 가능한 기록'). ⇒ 버린 탐색 분기도 세션 트리에 남아 나중에 다시 살펴볼 수 있습니다
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1217` 「도구 결과가 보였다는 것만으로 컨텍스트 재구성이 끝났다고 판단해서도 안 됩니다.」 — A negative qualification ('X만으로 … 안 됩니다') that duplicates 1132. ⇒ `rewind` 결과는 요청이 접수됐다는 뜻이고, 메시지 교체는 그 assistant 턴이 끝난 뒤에 일어납니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1170` 「탐색만 버릴 계획이라면 읽기 전용 도구를 쓰거나 파일 복구 수단을 별도로 마련해야 합니다.」 — '파일 복구 수단을 별도로 마련' is vague advice. ⇒ 조사 중에 파일을 바꿀 수 있다면, 체크포인트 전에 커밋이나 `git stash`로 돌아갈 지점을 만들어 두세요.
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:1115` 「한 세션에는 활성 체크포인트를 하나만 둘 수 있어 어느 경계로 돌아갈지 모호하지 않습니다.」 — The rationale is invented; the source states only the rule (nested call rejected with `Checkpoint already active.`). ⇒ Replace with: '체크포인트가 이미 있으면 새 `checkpoint` 호출은 `Checkpoint already active.` 오류로 거부됩니다.'
- I tool-sequence 1135-1149 (두 구현을 조사한 뒤 핵심만 유지하기) — Steps 2–5 (탐색 호출 생성 → 읽기·검색 실행 → 탐색 결과 → 도구 결과) are the ch06 tool loop. The relationship being taught (which messages are in the next request before vs after) is never visible. ⇒ Replace with a before/after message-list view like ch24's execution-path. Before: `u1 · a1 · checkpoint · read A · read B · grep · rewind`. After: `u1 · a1 · checkpoint 결과 · branch_summary · rewind-report`. Or cut the sequence to checkpoint → (탐색 N회, collapsed) → rewind → turn_end 적용 → next request.
- I execution-path 1173-1197 (체크포인트 뒤 config.ts를 수정한 경우) — This is the right comparison, but tabs hide the contrast: both outcomes hold at once, so they are not alternative paths. 'state: skipped' on '파일 스냅샷' misuses 'skipped' for something that never existed. ⇒ Render as side-by-side static columns (모델 대화 | 작업 트리), or as an exchange with one rewind input and two outputs. Drop the skipped stages.
- R [exchange ✗] EX L1106 checkpoint가 포착하는 것 → 포함 / 포함하지 않음 (2 outputs; 328px / 408px) — A list that duplicates EP L1173 in the same chapter. ⇒ Merge with the EP into one 2-column table (되돌아가는 것 / 그대로 남는 것).
- R [tool-sequence ✓] TS L1135 두 구현을 조사한 뒤 핵심만 유지하기 (8 steps; 1138px / 967px) — Good causal chain. Step 7 is a self `result`. The effect on the context would be clearer as a before/after view. ⇒ Keep. Optionally show the context before and after rewind in conversation-history style.
- R [execution-path ✗] EP L1173 체크포인트 뒤 config.ts를 수정한 경우 (2 tabs × 3; 605px / 670px) — Both tabs (모델 대화, 작업 트리) are true at once. ⇒ Fold it into the merged table above.

## 29 설정과 리소스 탐색

- **Verdict:** keep. This chapter owns the capability/provider/priority/dedup model that ch05, ch30 and ch32 each depend on, plus layered settings with their own merge rules.
- **Overall:** Precedence and merge rules are correct, and the array-replacement YAML is a good example. But the execution-path presents one cumulative fold as two alternative paths, the discovery section uses an exchange for sequential pipeline stages, the last section crams three topics together, and several sentences are generic harness advice. Stale at HEAD: a new top precedence layer (setting env vars), skill name collisions now get namespaced instead of dropped, and foreign user-level providers are now opt-in.
- **Organization:** §4 mixes activation filters (1367), per-resource project scope (1371) and profiles (1375). Split it into 'where omp looks' (per-resource scope: settings cwd-only, skills ancestors up to repo root, AGENTS.md walk with a ch05 reference; profiles) and 'what it uses' (provider toggles, include/ignore, `mcp.enableProjectConfig`). Since ch05, ch30 and ch32 re-describe discovery, make ch29 the single owner and have the others reference it.
- **Ordering:** ch05 (project-instructions-and-prompt-assembly.ts:16-17) already uses '`context-files` 기능에 등록된 공급자' and provider priority, 24 chapters before ch29 introduces capability providers. Either put a short primer earlier or have ch05 state the minimum. Within ch29, §3 uses '기능 항목'/'기능 레지스트리' without defining capability.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD docs/settings.md Precedence adds 'setting env var' above runtime overrides (`… <- runtime overrides <- setting env var`), so 1243's five-layer list is stale. HEAD also added `omp config set` reporting `overriddenBy`, live reload of settings files (keeping the last good layer), and the `modelRoleStorage: project` exception to 'writes go global'.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: setting definitions moved from `config/settings-schema.ts` (cited at 1325) to per-feature `register(...)` calls collected by `config/all-settings.ts`.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD docs/skills.md:122,130: foreign user-level providers are opt-in via `enabledProviders`, and colliding skills are namespaced rather than discarded.
- **Missing/stale:** Path-scoped entries in `enabledModels`/`enabledProviders`/`disabledProviders` (snapshot docs/settings.md:278) are a per-directory mechanism alongside project config.
- **Missing/stale:** `disabledProviders` is one id namespace gating both model providers and discovery sources (snapshot settings.md:312). This bears directly on 'activation'.

- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1276` 「이 출처 추적은 “설정을 바꿨는데 동작하지 않는다”는 문제를 해결하는 핵심입니다.」 — A generic importance claim that delays the actual cause. ⇒ `omp config set`은 전역 파일에 쓰므로, 프로젝트 파일이나 `--config` 오버레이가 같은 키를 정하면 바꾼 값이 적용되지 않습니다. `omp config get`으로 현재 디렉터리에서 실제 적용되는 값을 확인합니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1319` 「배열을 자동으로 덧붙이면 프로젝트가 전역 허용 목록을 제거할 수 없고」 — The example is `disabledProviders` (a deny list), not an allow list. ⇒ 배열을 이어 붙이는 규칙이라면 프로젝트는 전역 목록의 `groq`를 다시 켤 수 없고
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1336` 「리소스 탐색은 설정 병합보다 넓은 문제입니다.」 — A vague framing sentence with no information. ⇒ (삭제) — 바로 'OMP는 `.omp`뿐 아니라 …'로 시작
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1367` 「탐색이 “무엇이 존재하는가”를 답하면 활성화 정책은 “이번 세션에서 무엇을 사용할까”를 답합니다.」 — Restates the section title. ⇒ (삭제)
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1349` 「탐색 우선순위와 최종 설정 우선순위를 같은 규칙으로 간주하면 출처를 잘못 설명할 수 있습니다.」 — A hedged negative that repeats the previous sentence. ⇒ (삭제) — 앞 문장 '설정 기능은 항목을 제거하지 않고 하위 시스템의 병합 규칙을 적용합니다'로 충분
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1336/1355` 「기능 항목 … 기능 레지스트리 … capability 중복 제거」 — The same term appears translated and untranslated. ⇒ 처음에 'capability(기능)'로 소개한 뒤 '기능'으로 통일
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:1371` 「하네스는 하나의 “설정 폴더 검색” 함수로 이 차이를 지워서는 안 됩니다.」 — Generic design advice; the preceding facts already make the point. ⇒ Delete.
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:1375` 「프로필을 바꾸었을 때 무엇이 공유되고 무엇이 격리되는지 출처 메타데이터로 표시해야 합니다.」 — A normative claim with no omp evidence; omp offers `omp config path` for this. ⇒ Replace with the facts from docs/settings.md: '`omp --profile work`는 `~/.omp/profiles/work/agent`를 써서 설정·인증·세션·캐시를 분리합니다. 지금 쓰는 디렉터리는 `omp --profile work config path`로 확인합니다.'
- I execution-path 1246-1273 (`compaction.enabled`의 최종 값 추적) — The two tabs are not alternative executions: '지속 설정까지만 읽음' is not an omp mode (`omp config get` returns the process-effective value). Stages like '전역: `true`가 유지됩니다' are padding. ⇒ Use a static layer table (기본 true → 전역 true → 프로젝트 false → `--config` true → 결과 true). Or make the tabs a real choice with a consequence: A = `omp config set compaction.enabled true` writes global, but project `false` still wins and the result is false (at HEAD the CLI reports `overriddenBy: project`); B = edit `.omp/config.yml` and the result is true.
- I exchange 1339-1346 (이름이 같은 review 스킬 세 개) — The outputs are successive pipeline stages (수집 → 정렬 → 중복 제거), not parallel outputs of one input, and the winning file path is never shown. The outcome is also stale at HEAD. ⇒ Use a single-path execution-path or an ordered list ending in the winner `.omp/skills/review/SKILL.md`. Update for HEAD: the user-level Claude source loads only if enabled in `enabledProviders`, and same-named variants get `<namespace>/<name>` instead of being dropped.
- R [execution-path ✗] EP L1246 compaction.enabled의 최종 값 추적 (2 tabs × 3; 631px / 723px; tabs wrap to 2 rows) — Layered overrides are clearer as a stack. Tab labels are full clauses ('현재 프로세스 전체를 읽음'). ⇒ Use a static layer table (계층 / 값 / 누적 유효값) with the persist/overlay boundary marked.
- R [exchange ✗] EX ~L1340 이름이 같은 review 스킬 세 개 → 후보 수집 / 우선순위 정렬 / 키 중복 제거 (3 outputs; 402px / 533px) — A sequential pipeline drawn as parallel outputs. ⇒ Use a numbered flow showing the three candidates being narrowed to one.

## 30 스킬과 프롬프트 템플릿

- **Verdict:** keep. Distinct from ch05: ch05 covers always-on instructions (AGENTS.md, rules, SYSTEM.md) assembled into every request, while skills are on-demand instructions pulled in through `read skill://` or `/skill:`, and prompt templates expand user input before the request. The only overlap with ch05 is the skill-list block in the system prompt, which ch05 never mentions; add a one-line pointer there. There is no overlap with ch24. The real overlap is with ch29: 1402's last sentence and 1437's filter list restate ch29 §3–4 and should become references. The template material is thin but is a proper section, since no slash-command chapter exists.
- **Overall:** Skill format and skill:// are explained accurately. But the discovery/dedup sentences duplicate ch29 (and are stale at HEAD), the sequence spends 5 of 8 steps on the generic tool loop, the prompt-template example never shows its expanded result, and the closing exchange is a table. The central harness idea is left implicit: only name and description sit in context permanently, the body loads on demand, and so description quality decides whether a skill is used.
- **Organization:** The grouping (format → loading/invocation → templates → choosing) is sound. But §2's title '발견과 본문 주입은 다른 순간에 일어난다' promises a contrast the content does not show: the paragraph is a filter list and the sequence covers only user invocation. Show both triggers: the model reading `skill://release` because of the description, and the user typing `/skill:release`.
- **Ordering:** 1490 refers to extension commands, which ch31 introduces later; mark it '확장이 등록한 명령(31장)'. 1437's '자동 학습이 만든 관리 스킬' relies on ch26's local backend generating skills (960) without saying so.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: the skill list enters the system prompt only when an active tool declares `readsSkillUris: true` (including mounted xd:// tools), not when 'read 도구가 활성화' (docs/skills.md:143).
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: the `skillshare` provider (priority 95) with `skills.lock.json` / `omp skill install`, `<namespace>/<name>` collision naming, `enabled: false` frontmatter, and opt-in foreign user-level providers.
- **Missing/stale:** Why only name+description: context cost; and 'matching quality depends on description' (docs/skills.md) is the practical authoring rule.
- **Missing/stale:** `skills.enableSkillCommands` gates the `/skill:` commands.
- **Missing/stale:** Cross-links: local memory consolidation writes skills under `memory://root/skills/…` (ch26:960), and compaction protects skill content (ch25:782).

- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1490` 「세션은 위치 인자와 전체 인자 표기를 치환하고 템플릿 렌더러를 거친 문자열을 일반 사용자 프롬프트로 보냅니다.」 — A noun-stacked description of what should be shown concretely. ⇒ 세션은 `$1`을 `UserService`로, `$@[2]`를 두 번째 인자부터인 `v1 v2`로 바꾼 뒤 그 문장을 일반 사용자 메시지로 보냅니다.

## 31 확장 기능과 플러그인

- **Verdict:** keep. The extension runtime (factory API, event interception, load/runtime phases) and plugin packaging/installation are a distinct mechanism, and other chapters (25 compaction hooks, 29 dedup, 30 commands) depend on them.
- **Overall:** The code example (taken from omp's quick start) and the trust section are strong. But '표면' abstractions recur, the sequence misattributes who fires `tool_call` and never plays out the example's block, 'hooks' are used without definition, and the chapter omits the events that matter most to a harness: `context`, `before_agent_start` and `before_provider_request`, which change what the model sees.
- **Organization:** §1 mixes 'what an extension can register' (tools, commands, providers, renderers) with 'what it can intercept' (events). Split them and put interception next to the sequence in §2. The /reload-plugins detail in §3 (1622) is a plugin operation; move it to §4.
- **Ordering:** '코드로 작성된 훅' (1618) and '훅' (1640, also ch29:1349, ch30:1517) appear without definition. Define hooks vs extensions in §1.
- **Missing/stale:** Events that rewrite model input: `context`, `before_agent_start`, `before_provider_request` (replaces the provider payload), and `session_before_compact`/`session.compacting` (already used in ch25:831). This chapter should be where they are introduced.
- **Missing/stale:** `ctx.ui` and mode differences (TUI vs print/RPC): the example calls `ctx.ui.notify` without explanation.
- **Missing/stale:** Custom tools (`.omp/tools`, 'custom tool' at 1640) vs `registerTool`, and the legacy hooks capability vs extensions.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD (docs/extensions.md +358 lines): `tool_call`/`tool_result` can return passive `additionalContext`; `tool_call` is fail-closed on timeout too (`extensionHandlers.toolCallTimeoutMs`, 30 s); `before_subagent_spawn`; `ctx.runEphemeralTurn`; runtime setting overrides. Only registry tools are wrapped ('Registry tools are wrapped' replaced 'Every tool execution').

- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1538 (title), 1542, 1622` 「확장은 런타임 표면을 등록한다 / 여러 표면을 함께 제공 / 세션 구성 때 고정된 표면」 — '표면' (literal 'surface') is an abstract noun standing in for concrete things (3 occurrences). ⇒ 제목: '확장은 도구·명령·이벤트 처리기를 등록한다'; 1542 삭제(예제가 보여 줌); 1622 '세션을 만들 때 등록이 끝나는 도구·훅·확장 모듈'
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1600` 「다만 `tool_call` 단계의 오류는 안전하게 실행을 막는 방향으로 처리됩니다.」 — A roundabout phrasing of fail-closed. ⇒ `tool_call` 처리기가 예외를 던지면 그 도구 호출은 실행되지 않습니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1622` 「그러나 모든 기능이 같은 순간에 다시 만들어지는 것은 아닙니다. … 재시작이 필요할 수 있습니다.」 — A negative framing, plus a hedge where docs/marketplace.md is definitive ('restart the session for newly installed tools, hooks, or extension modules'). The preceding sentence about mtime import URLs is implementation trivia. ⇒ 플러그인을 설치한 뒤 `/reload-plugins`를 실행하면 스킬·명령·MCP 서버가 갱신됩니다. 새 도구·훅·확장 모듈은 세션을 다시 시작해야 적용됩니다.
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1640` 「custom tool, MCP·LSP 설정 … marketplace 카탈로그는」 — English left in Korean prose, and it is inconsistent with the exchange label '마켓플레이스'. ⇒ 사용자 정의 도구, MCP·LSP 설정 … 마켓플레이스 카탈로그는
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:1547 (caption) and code 1552-1553` 「위험한 셸 호출을 막고 상태 명령을 추가하는 확장 … 'Project policy blocks recursive deletion.'」 — `includes('rm -rf')` is trivially bypassed (`rm -fr`, `rm -r -f`), so calling it a deletion policy overstates what it does. ⇒ Caption: '`bash` 호출을 가로채는 최소 예'. Add one line: '문자열 검사는 우회하기 쉬우므로 실제 정책은 8장의 승인 규칙과 함께 씁니다.'
- I tool-sequence 1582-1593 (확장 파일이 도구 호출을 가로채기까지) — (a) The `tool_call` arrow starts at '도구 실행기', but snapshot docs/extensions.md says that for model-issued calls `tool_call` fires at arg-prep time in the agent loop, so a revision is seen by the approval gate; at HEAD an approval denial can short-circuit before `tool_call`. (b) The sequence never plays out the code example: no model, no `rm -rf` call, no block reason reaching the model. Steps 1–3 duplicate paragraph 1596. ⇒ Actors 모델 / 에이전트 루프 / ExtensionRunner / bash. Steps: the model generates `bash rm -rf build`; the loop passes it to the runner's `tool_call`; the handler returns `{ block: true, reason }`; the loop returns a blocked tool result with the reason to the model, and bash never runs. Leave the load phase to the prose.
- I exchange 1643-1650 (code-review 플러그인) — A legitimate fan-out: one package consumed by three subsystems. ⇒ Keep.
- R [tool-sequence ✓] TS ~L1582 확장 파일이 도구 호출을 가로채기까지 (5 steps; 765px / 652px) — Load time (steps 1–3) and call time (steps 4–5) are mixed with no visible break. Import uses `read`; registration uses `result`. ⇒ Split into two labelled phases (or a 2-tab EP 로드/호출) and fix the kinds.
- R [exchange ✗] EX ~L1643 code-review 플러그인 → 설치기 / 탐색기 / 확장 로더 (3 outputs; 452px / 660px) — Sequential (install → discover → load) drawn as parallel. ⇒ Use a numbered flow.

## 32 MCP 연동

- **Verdict:** keep. The protocol bridge (handshake, schema/name mapping, deferred tools, reconnect and ownership) is a large, distinct mechanism.
- **Overall:** Lifecycle coverage is strong and both sequences show genuine protocol order. But the chapter opens with config file locations before saying what MCP is or what role OMP plays. One claim is inaccurate (connection and `tools/list` 'in parallel'). The retry statement lacks the non-idempotence condition. Resources and prompts are named without saying where they surface, and server instructions injected into the system prompt are missing. Several details are stale at HEAD.
- **Organization:** The question grouping (define → connect → call → lifecycle) is good. Add a short setup: OMP is the MCP client; a server exposes tools, resources and prompts over JSON-RPC; OMP turns tools into session tools, resources into `mcp://` reads, and prompts into slash commands.
- **Ordering:** §1's discovery sources (1680) come before MCP is defined; fix with the setup above. The `i` field at 1766 relies on ch07 (tool-definitions-and-registry.ts:214); add '(7장)'. Discovery priority should reference ch29 rather than restate it.
- **Missing/stale:** Server `instructions` injected into the system prompt (snapshot sdk.ts:3109-3146 `getServerInstructions()`; HEAD adds per-server `instructions: false`). This links to ch05.
- **Missing/stale:** Where resources and prompts surface: resources through `mcp://` (internal-urls/mcp-protocol.ts), prompts as slash commands ('MCP prompt commands', lifecycle doc), and `resources/updated` subscriptions. 1738 only names them.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: the startup window is configurable (`mcp.startupTimeoutMs`, `OMP_MCP_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS`), and print mode waits for all servers (up to 30 s). Quiet reconnect probes for lost HTTP/SSE servers run from 15 s up to 5 min. Errors are structured (`MCPTransportError`, `MCP failure`). Name sanitization now keeps digits and caps names at 64 characters with a hash suffix. Tools may be mounted under `xd://` instead of top-level definitions. Browser MCP servers are dropped when the built-in browser is callable. Streamable HTTP resumes streams via `Last-Event-ID`.

- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1703` 「설정 검증은 이 구조를 확인할 뿐 실제 서버 도달 가능성은 연결 단계에서 판정합니다.」 — A negative qualification stating the obvious. ⇒ (삭제)
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1734` 「느린 서버 하나가 전체 세션 시작을 막지 않습니다.」 — Restates the previous two sentences. ⇒ (삭제)
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1800` 「수명 세대 값을 바꾸고」 — A literal rendering of 'lifecycle epoch'. ⇒ 세대 번호(epoch)를 올려
- P `chapters-20-32.ts:1756` 「서버의 `inputSchema`를 `normalizeSchemaForMCP()`로 정리해 OMP의 사용자 정의 도구 스키마로 등록합니다」 — Doesn't say what the normalization changes or why it is needed. ⇒ [INFERENCE — normalizeSchemaForMCP 구현으로 확인 필요] 제공자 API가 받지 않는 JSON Schema 키워드를 정리해, 원격 스키마를 그대로 모델 요청의 도구 정의로 쓸 수 있게 합니다.
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:1734` 「서버 연결과 `tools/list`는 병렬로 시작합니다.」 — Inaccurate: within one server the pipeline is sequential (connect/initialize → `listTools`; snapshot docs/mcp-runtime-lifecycle.md per-server steps 6–8). The parallelism is across servers. ⇒ Rewrite: '서버마다 연결과 `tools/list`를 차례로 진행하되, 여러 서버는 동시에 연결합니다.'
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:1774` 「브리지는 서버를 다시 연결하고 같은 호출을 한 번 더 시도합니다. … `MCP error: …` 도구 결과로」 — Missing the condition that matters: the source says retry happens only for 'retriable connection failures', and HEAD adds that failures after an accepted request POST are not replayed, so a state-changing tool does not run twice. `MCP error: …` is stale; HEAD returns a structured `MCP failure` diagnostic. ⇒ Rewrite: '요청이 서버에 닿기 전에 연결이 끊긴 경우에만 다시 연결해 한 번 재시도합니다. 서버가 이미 받은 호출은 반복하지 않으므로 상태를 바꾸는 도구가 두 번 실행되지 않습니다.' Update the error text for HEAD.
- B `chapters-20-32.ts:1696` 「"headers": { "Authorization": "TEAM_MCP_AUTH" }」 — Readers will assume the literal string is sent; omp resolves environment-variable names and `!command` values (docs/mcp-config.md). ⇒ Caption or sentence: '`TEAM_MCP_AUTH`는 같은 이름의 환경 변수 값으로 바뀝니다.'
- I tool-sequence 1720-1731 (MCP 서버가 세션 도구로 들어오기까지) — Real protocol order with a visible name mapping. Minor: `notifications/initialized` is a notification with no response, yet it is drawn like the calls. ⇒ Keep; mark the notification step as one-way.
- I tool-sequence 1759-1771 (모델 호출이 원격 tools/call이 되기까지) — Good: it shows the local name mapping back to `search_docs`. The two self-loops (조회·검증, 인자 정리) could merge. The final result is described, not shown. ⇒ Merge the self-loops and show the actual text block the model receives.
- R [tool-sequence ✓] TS ~L1720 MCP 서버가 세션 도구로 들어오기까지 (5 steps; 764px / 676px) — Inconsistent kinds: `request` for initialize and tools/list, `call` for the notification. '브리지 등록' is a `result` sent to the registry. ⇒ Unify the kinds. Consider merging with the next TS.
- R [tool-sequence ✓] TS ~L1759 모델 호출이 원격 tools/call이 되기까지 (6 steps; 972px / 830px) — Good: the name translation `mcp__…` → `search_docs` is visible. ⇒ Keep.

### Range patterns (ch 26–32)

- Trailing 'the harness should…' sentences with no omp evidence and no decision attached (5): 1078, 1319, 1371, 1375, 1517. At least one (1078) describes behavior omp already implements at HEAD. Delete them, or replace with what omp does.
- Negative qualifications and sentences restating a heading or diagram (7): 900 '모든 문장을 그대로 복사하는 기능이 아닙니다', 1217 '…것만으로 … 판단해서도 안 됩니다', 1336, 1367, 1622 '모든 기능이 같은 순간에 … 것은 아닙니다', 1703, 1734.
- Hedged '~수 있습니다' where the source gives a definite rule: 18 occurrences in 846-1814. Examples: 964 '빠질 수 있습니다' (exact age/idle limits exist), 1622 '재시작이 필요할 수 있습니다' (docs say restart is required), 942 '들어갈 수 있고 … 제공할 수 있습니다'.
- Literal technical transliterations and abstract nouns: '내구성' ×3 (866, 900, 942/949), '백잉 파일' ×2 and '물질화' (1024), '정체성' (1056), '은행' for bank (922), '표면' ×3 (1538, 1542, 1622), '수명 세대 값' (1800), and abstract '경계' (942 '처리 경계', 1517 '실행 경계가 흐려집니다').
- English or code spans misused in prose: `frontmatter` as inline code ×3 (1402, 1446, 1475), 'custom tool' and 'marketplace' (1640), 'capability' in a link label beside '기능' (1355).
- Exchange blocks used as tables or pipelines (3): 1042 (ch27 schemes), 1339 (ch29 sequential stages), 1507 (ch30 one-to-one mapping). Execution-path tabs used for outcomes that are not alternatives (2): 1246 (ch29 cumulative fold), 1173 (ch28 simultaneous outcomes).
- Tool-sequences padded with the generic ch06 tool loop: ch28 1141-1144 (4 of 8 steps) and ch30 1448-1452 (5 of 8 steps).
- Examples missing the observable result (given → action → result): template expansion (1478-1490), the artifact preview text (993-1000), the injected memory block and model consequence (886-897), and the env-var header in the MCP config (1696).
- Cross-chapter: Discovery and precedence are described four times: ch05 (project-instructions-and-prompt-assembly.ts:16-17), ch29 (1336-1349), ch30 (1402, 1437) and ch32 (1680). Make ch29 the single owner of the capability-provider/priority/dedup model and have the others reference it. ch05 currently uses that model before ch29 introduces it.
- Cross-chapter: Internal URLs are scattered and never introduced: memory:// in ch26 (910, 938), artifact:// and agent:// in ch27, skill:// in ch30, and mcp:// nowhere. ch27 should introduce the router and scheme list and come before ch26, or ch26 should forward-reference it.
- Cross-chapter: blob: is covered in both ch24 (673) and ch27 (1047-1056). Keep it in ch24 (session persistence) with one contrasting sentence in ch27.
- Cross-chapter: `branch_summary` is listed in ch24 (589, 622) but produced only in ch28. Neither chapter covers `/tree` navigation with summarize (docs/tree.md). Merging ch28 into ch24 closes the gap, and at HEAD it also disambiguates user `/rewind` (alias of `/branch`) from the model's `rewind` tool.
- Cross-chapter: 'Hooks' are used in ch25 (831), ch29 (1349 dedup key), ch30 (1517 '정책 훅') and ch31 (1618 '코드로 작성된 훅') but defined nowhere. ch31 should define hooks vs extensions.
- Cross-chapter: ch26 (960: local backend generates skills) and ch30 (1437: '자동 학습이 만든 관리 스킬') describe the same artifact without cross-linking.
- Cross-chapter: Skill-collision behavior changed at HEAD (namespacing instead of first-wins drop; foreign user-level sources opt-in). ch29's exchange and ch30:1402 must be updated together.
- Cross-chapter: '감사' (audit) is ambiguous in both ch25 (767 '감사 가능한 기록') and ch28 (1152). Use '다시 살펴볼 수 있는' as ch24:604 does.
- Cross-chapter: On ch28 vs 05/24: ch28's mechanism is a subset of ch24 (demote to a ch24 section). ch30 does not overlap ch05 mechanically (keep, but add a one-line pointer in ch05 for the skill-list block and cut the ch29 duplication from ch30).

## 33 계획 모드

- **Verdict:** keep. It has enough distinct mechanism for a chapter: the write/edit path guard (`plan-mode-guard.ts`), the `xd://propose` approval device, the approval overlay with five exits and a model-tier slider, re-injection of the approved plan after history rewrites (`#buildPlanReferenceMessage`), plan-mode subagent tool restriction (`structured-subagent.ts` PLAN_MODE_TOOLS) and the settle-time decision enforcement. None of these overlap goals or todo.
- **Overall:** This is the strongest chapter in the range. It is organized around two questions, what the model may do while planning and how approval hands off to execution, and its claims match the snapshot. Four problems weaken it: (a) it overstates enforcement, since the guard covers write and edit only; (b) it uses `xd://propose` before explaining it; (c) it leaves out the most consequential approval choice, compacting before execution; (d) it leaves out the plan-mode convergence reminder. The first exchange is a category list, not an observable guard result. I found no behavioral staleness at HEAD: only a settings-registry refactor and hashline fix e2a1b54f0b touched plan mode.
- **Organization:** Grouping by question is sound: section 1 covers allowed and blocked actions, section 2 covers approval and handoff. Two things are missing. Section 1 should say how plan mode is entered and that it excludes goal mode (`#enterPlanMode`: "Exit goal mode first."). Section 2 should cover the convergence rule that stops a planning turn from ending in prose. The read-only subagent restriction appears only as "읽기 전용 하위 에이전트". Name the four tools once here and have ch36 point back to this chapter.
- **Ordering:** L42 uses `xd://propose` before section 2 explains approval, and L60 then describes approval without naming the device. Move the `xd://propose` explanation to the start of section 2: write the plan file, then write the slug to `xd://propose`. In L42, keep only the fact that `write` stays active. The term "`plan` 역할의 모델" relies on ch21 model roles, which is fine because ch21 comes earlier.
- **Missing/stale:** Convergence enforcement: when a plan-mode turn ends without a tool call, `AgentSession.#enforcePlanModeDecisionAtSettle` injects `plan-mode-tool-decision-reminder.md` and forces `toolChoice: required` (capped by PLAN_MODE_REMINDER_MAX). The model must call `ask` or write to `xd://propose`, and prose approval requests are forbidden.
- **Missing/stale:** Plan-mode subagents are limited to `read`, `grep`, `glob`, `web_search` (+`ast_grep`). Isolation, apply/merge and eval-defined tools are rejected (`task/structured-subagent.ts`, `task/index.ts`).
- **Missing/stale:** Entry, exit and persistence: `/plan`, the `plan.defaultOnStartup` setting, `plan`/`plan_paused` mode entries restored on resume (`interactive-mode.ts` ~L3330), and mutual exclusion with goal mode.
- **Missing/stale:** Approval overlay details that matter to a harness: the model-tier slider ("continue with", default `default`), in-overlay section deletes and annotations that become refine feedback, and the keep-context option.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD 3f000c52: no behavioral change found (commits only touch the settings registry and the hashline '#' path fix e2a1b54f0b).

- P `chapters-33-46.ts:9` 「코드를 바꾸기 전에 조사, 계획 작성, 사람의 승인을 별도 단계로 묶는 실행 정책.」 — The noun list contradicts itself ("별도 단계로 묶는") and ends on the vague category noun "실행 정책". ⇒ 작업 트리를 읽기만 하며 계획을 쓰고, 사람이 승인한 뒤에야 코드를 바꾸게 하는 세션 모드.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:17` 「계획 모드는 계획용 프롬프트와 도구 정책을 함께 바꾸는 세션 상태입니다. OMP는 세션에 `enabled`, `planFilePath`, 작업 방식 같은 계획 모드 상태를 두고, 이 상태를 도구 실행에도 적용합니다.」 — "상태" appears three times, the field names add nothing, and the sentence never says who acts. ⇒ 계획 모드에 들어가면 OMP는 계획용 지침을 대화에 넣고 쓰기 도구에 가드를 겁니다. 모델은 파일을 읽고 검색하거나 읽기 전용 하위 에이전트에게 조사를 맡길 수 있지만, `write`나 `edit`로 작업 트리를 바꾸려 하면 실행 전에 거부됩니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:32` 「이 공간은 작업 트리와 분리된 계획용 저장소입니다.」 — `local://` is the session artifact directory, not a store reserved for plans (plan-mode-guard.ts: "scratch/plan space"). ⇒ `local://`은 세션 아티팩트 디렉터리라 작업 트리 밖에 있습니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:60` 「실행, 문맥 압축 후 실행, 수정 요청, 저장 후 종료 같은 다음 행동을 사람이 고르게 합니다.」 — The "같은" noun list omits the keep-context option and never says how the model requests approval. ⇒ 조사를 마친 모델은 계획을 `local://<slug>-plan.md`에 쓰고, 같은 `<slug>`를 `xd://propose`에 써서 승인을 요청합니다. 화면은 계획 본문을 보여 주고, 사람은 바로 실행할지, 문맥을 압축하거나 유지한 채 실행할지, 수정을 요청할지, 저장하고 끝낼지 고릅니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:93` 「승인된 계획은 실행 참조로 남습니다.」 — "실행 참조" is a calque of the code's "plan reference" and is unclear to readers. ⇒ 세션은 승인된 계획 파일의 경로를 기억합니다. 새 세션을 열거나 대화를 압축해 기록을 다시 만들면 계획 본문을 한 번 다시 넣으므로, 앞선 조사 대화가 사라져도 실행하는 모델은 계획을 볼 수 있습니다.

At 3b3a6dc9 `enforcePlanModeWrite` is called only by write and edit; `bash` stays active and is unguarded. The ban on state-changing commands exists only in `plan-mode-active.md`. ⇒ Replace with: 가드는 `write`·`edit`의 대상 경로만 검사합니다. 상태를 바꾸는 셸 명령을 금지하는 규칙은 지침에만 있으므로, `bash`로 실행한 `git commit`은 가드가 막지 않습니다.

- B `chapters-33-46.ts:36` 「코드 변경은 아직 시작되지 않습니다.」 — Repeats the label "차단되는 실행". ⇒ delete
- I exchange L19-39 (주어진 상태 → 허용되는 조사/기록/차단되는 실행) — Prose dressed as a diagram. The outputs are three policy categories, not results produced from the input, and the reader never sees the guard act. ⇒ Show concrete calls and their results instead: `read src/auth.ts` → 내용 반환; `write local://auth-plan.md` → 저장; `edit src/auth.ts` → `Plan mode: the working tree is read-only. Write your plan to a local://<slug>-plan.md file instead.` Optionally add `bash git commit` → 가드 없음(지침만), which makes the real limit of the guard visible. Rename the input label to “요청”.

Compacting before execution is why the plan must be decision-complete: `plan-mode-active.md` says "Approval may clear/compact the conversation; another engineer/fresh agent implements solely from the file". The stage "모드 종료: … 원래 실행 구성으로 되돌립니다" is also inaccurate, because the execution model comes from the overlay slider, which defaults to the `default` role. ⇒ Use three tabs: 수정 요청 / 승인·문맥 유지 / 승인·압축 후 실행. In the compaction tab, show that the investigation conversation is summarized, the plan body is re-injected and execution starts. Change the stage to “선택한 역할 모델(기본 `default`)로 실행”.

## 34 목표

- **Verdict:** keep. Its mechanism is distinct from plan mode and todo: an autonomous continuation loop (`#scheduleGoalContinuation`, `goal.continuationModes`), per-turn token deltas that exclude `cacheRead`, a `budget-limited` transition with a one-time hidden steer, and interrupt→`paused`. If ch35 is merged in (see ch35), this chapter becomes the home for “when may the agent stop?”.
- **Overall:** The facts about the goal record, its ops and token accounting are accurate, and excluding cache reads from the budget is a good harness detail that links back to ch01. The chapter's central mechanism, the continuation driver, is misattributed and never actually explained. The tool sequence credits the "목표 런타임" with deciding to continue "because failures remain". In source, the interactive mode schedules a hidden continuation prompt 800 ms after `agent_end` whenever the status is `active`, and it never inspects test results. Both interactive blocks are weak. The chapter also misses the completion-audit rules in the continuation prompt and the HEAD RPC goal support.
- **Organization:** Section 1 mixes the record, the tool ops and the paused/resume lifecycle. Section 2 mixes accounting, continuation and interruption. Regroup by question: (1) creating a goal and what is recorded; (2) how the next turn starts without the user (the continuation driver and its conditions); (3) when the loop stops (complete, budget-limited, paused by interrupt, drop). The closing comparison with todo belongs in a merged chapter, or should be cut.
- **Ordering:** L146 mentions paused goals and `resume` before L183 explains that only a user interrupt pauses a goal. `pause` is not a tool op. The continuation, which is the reason goals exist, is implied in the L136 exchange but only shown in the section-2 diagram. Introduce the continuation before the budget details.
- **Missing/stale:** Continuation driver conditions: `goal.continuationModes` (default `["interactive"]`). Continuation is suppressed in plan mode, in loop mode, when the editor holds text, or when an input is pending (`interactive-mode.ts` ~L1747).
- **Missing/stale:** The completion audit in `prompts/goals/goal-continuation.md`: map the objective to deliverables, check each against current evidence, treat uncertainty as not achieved, and never complete merely because the budget is exhausted. This is the harness's answer to premature completion.
- **Missing/stale:** The budget-limit steer is sent once per goal as a hidden steer (`#sendBudgetLimitSteer`). Raising or clearing the budget reactivates the goal (`onBudgetMutated`).
- **Missing/stale:** Entry and exclusivity: `/goal`, `--goal`. The `goal` tool works only in goal mode ("Goal mode is not active."). Goal mode and plan mode are mutually exclusive.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD a82a6882ac (2026-10-02): RPC `goal` command (get/create/resume/pause/drop), the goal included in `get_state`, and opt-in RPC continuation via `goal.continuationModes: ["rpc"]`. The RPC continuation re-checks abort, plan mode, goal status, idle and open todos. HEAD 7d634150c3 adds a per-op approval tier for the `goal` tool.

- P `chapters-33-46.ts:113` 「여러 턴에 걸친 목적과 사용량을 세션에 붙여 자율 실행의 종료 조건을 관리하는 장치.」 — Noun-heavy ("사용량을 세션에 붙여") and ends on the vague "장치". ⇒ 목적을 이룰 때까지 사람의 입력 없이 턴을 이어 가고, 토큰 예산으로 멈출 때를 정하는 세션 모드.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:117` 「목적과 실행 상태를 한 기록으로 묶기」 — Abstract nominal title built on "실행 상태". ⇒ 목표에 기록되는 것
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:121` 「목표는 한 턴의 요청보다 오래 유지되는 세션 제어 기록입니다.」 — "세션 제어 기록" is translationese. ⇒ 목표는 여러 턴에 걸쳐 유지되는 작업 목적입니다. OMP는 목표마다 목적(`objective`), 상태, 선택적인 토큰 예산, 지금까지 쓴 토큰과 시간을 기록합니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:136` 「턴이 끝나도 목표가 활성 상태면 후속 실행을 이어 갈 수 있습니다.」 — No actor, and "이어 갈 수 있습니다" leaves open who continues and when. ⇒ 모델이 목표를 닫지 않고 턴을 끝내면, 대화형 화면이 숨은 계속 지시를 보내 다음 턴을 시작합니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:146` 「`drop`은 목적을 포기하고 기록을 제거하는 선택이며」 — "선택" is an odd collocation, and the goal is set to `dropped`, not erased. ⇒ `drop`은 목표를 완료하지 않은 채 버리고, `complete`는 목표를 닫으며 최종 사용량 보고를 남깁니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:183` 「이렇게 해야 다음 세션 재개가 사용자 개입을 무시하고 자동 실행을 시작하지 않습니다.」 — Clumsy noun-subject construction ("세션 재개가 … 무시하고"). ⇒ 그래서 세션을 다시 열어도 사용자가 멈춘 작업이 저절로 다시 시작되지 않습니다.
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:175` 「목표가 아직 `active`이고 실패가 남아 있으므로 다음 턴에 목적을 다시 제공」 — This claims more than the source supports. `interactive-mode.ts #scheduleGoalContinuation` checks only goal status, plan mode, editor emptiness and `goal.continuationModes`. It knows nothing about failing tests. ⇒ Replace with: 모델이 `goal complete` 없이 턴을 끝냄 → 목표가 `active`이므로 숨은 계속 지시(남은 예산 포함) 전송
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:140` 「세 테스트의 실행 결과를 확인한 뒤 모델이 `goal complete`를 호출해야 목표가 `complete`로 닫힙니다.」 — Implies the harness verifies results. Only the prompt (`goal-continuation.md`) asks the model to audit evidence; the runtime accepts `complete` regardless. ⇒ State the actual boundary: 런타임은 테스트 결과를 확인하지 않습니다. 계속 지시가 완료 전에 근거를 점검하라고 요구할 뿐, 목표를 닫는 것은 모델의 `goal complete` 호출입니다.
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:183` 「하네스는 두 상태를 섞지 않아야 목표 완료와 개별 작업 완료를 각각 판단할 수 있습니다.」 — A generic moral, and it misleads: the continuation prompt tells the model to record deliverables in todo, so the two interact. ⇒ delete, or replace with the interplay: 계속 지시는 목적을 결과물로 나눠 할 일 목록에 적고, 각 결과물의 근거를 확인한 뒤에만 완료하라고 요구합니다.
- I exchange L123-143 (`goal create` → 목표 기록/세션 제어/완료 경계) — The three outputs are facets of one record plus two predictions, not results produced from the input. ⇒ Make it static: show the `goal` call JSON and the real result text from `goal-tool.ts` (`Goal: …\nStatus: active\nTokens: 0 used / 20000 budget\nRemaining tokens: 20000`).
- I tool-sequence L166-180 (목표가 다음 턴을 이어 가는 기준) — The decisive transition, a turn ending without `goal complete` followed by an automatic hidden prompt, is never shown. The actor "목표 런타임" also merges two parts: accounting (runtime) and continuation (interactive mode). ⇒ Use actors 모델 / 세션(대화형 모드·목표 런타임) / 도구. Events: 요청 1(목표 문맥) → 수정·테스트 → 결과(2/3 통과) → 턴 종료(`goal complete` 없음), 사용량 누적 → 숨은 `goal-continuation` 지시 → 수정·재실행 → 3/3 통과 → `goal complete` → 완료 보고. Optionally add a second scenario in which the budget runs out and the goal becomes `budget-limited`.
- R [exchange ✗] EX L124 goal create → 목표 기록 / 세션 제어 / 완료 경계 (3 outputs; 503px / 737px) — Only the first card is an output. The other two describe later behaviour. '완료 경계' is an abstract noun. ⇒ Show the created goal record as code and delete the EX.
- R [tool-sequence ✓] TS L167 목표가 다음 턴을 이어 가는 기준 (7 steps; 932px / 820px) — Good: the runtime re-prompts while the goal is active. `goal complete` is a tool call but is drawn as `answer`. ⇒ Keep; use kind `call` for step 7.

## 35 작업 추적

- **Verdict:** merge → 34 (목표) — combined chapter on when the agent may stop: 할 일 목록과 목표. As written, the only distinct mechanism is one tool's state transitions and normalization, which fits a section. The mechanisms that would justify more are absent: `todo.reminders` (default on, `todo.remindersMax` 3, "Remind the agent to complete todos before stopping"), `eager-todo.md` and `todo-error-reminder`. Once added, they answer the same question as goal continuation: the harness injects hidden prompts at settle to decide whether the agent may stop. The goal continuation prompt even tells the model to record deliverables in todo. Keep it as a separate chapter only if the reminder material is added and the chapter is reframed around it.
- **Overall:** This is an accurate reference for the `todo` state machine: nine ops, five statuses, single-active normalization and atomic failure. It leaves out the harness behavior that makes a todo list matter: stop-time reminders that keep the agent from quitting with open tasks, the eager `init` prompt, the hidden error reminder, and parent-only ownership. Its closing claims about resume and display are stale at HEAD. The exchange shows a sequence of states as if they were parallel outputs.
- **Organization:** Section 1 (data model and ops) and section 2 (blocking plus persistence and display) are reasonable, but section 2's last paragraph mixes a third question, what persists versus what is shown, under a blocking title. Add a section on how the harness uses the list at stop time, and give canonical state versus HUD its own short subsection.
- **Ordering:** L210 lists every status and op name before any example. Show the `init` example first, then introduce ops by purpose (create, change status, add/remove). Otherwise the chapter is self-contained.
- **Missing/stale:** Stop-time todo reminders: `todo.reminders` (default true) and `todo.remindersMax` (default 3) in settings-schema.ts. The `todo_reminder` session event is also forwarded to extensions, ACP and RPC.
- **Missing/stale:** `prompts/system/eager-todo.md` (forced or suggested `init` before substantive work) and `mid-run-todo-nudge.md`.
- **Missing/stale:** A failed `todo` call queues a hidden next-turn `todo-error-reminder`, and the UI warns that the panel may be stale.
- **Missing/stale:** `/todo` edits by the user append `user_todo_edit` entries and inject a system-reminder to the model.
- **Missing/stale:** Subagents do not inherit `todo`, because it is a parent-owned tool (except prewalk-armed children). RPC has `set_todos`.
- **Missing/stale:** STALE at HEAD: L272 says resume restores only open tasks. HEAD `TodoTracker.syncFromBranch()` restores the latest snapshot, including completed and abandoned tasks. The display rule also changed: the HUD hides only when every task is closed, and the dismissal persists as a `todo_hud_state` entry (docs/tools/todo.md diff).

- P `chapters-33-46.ts:206` 「단계, 작업, 한 번의 변경」 — The title is a noun list. ⇒ 단계별 목록을 만들고 한 번에 하나씩 바꾸기
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:210` 「모델은 한 번의 도구 호출에서 `init`, `start`, `done`, `drop`, `block`, `unblock`, `append`, `rm`, `view` 가운데 하나를 적용합니다.」 — Nine identifiers with no meaning attached (`rm` vs `drop` is unexplained). ⇒ 모델은 `todo`를 호출할 때마다 연산 하나를 적용합니다. `init`은 목록을 새로 만들고, `start`·`done`·`drop`·`block`·`unblock`은 작업의 상태를 바꾸며, `append`와 `rm`은 작업을 더하거나 지웁니다. `drop`은 작업을 포기한 것으로 남기고, `rm`은 목록에서 없앱니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:230` 「하네스가 부분 적용을 남기지 않기 때문에 모델은 오류를 본 다음 올바른 대상을 다시 조회해 한 번에 고칠 수 있습니다.」 — "한 번에 고칠 수 있습니다" is vague, and each call already carries a single op, so the atomicity claim is weaker than it sounds. ⇒ 실패한 호출은 목록을 전혀 바꾸지 않으므로, 모델은 이전 목록을 기준으로 다시 호출하면 됩니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:247` 「모든 열린 작업이 막혔을 때만 진행 중인 작업이 없는 목록이 됩니다.」 — Convoluted negative phrasing. ⇒ 열린 작업이 모두 막혀 있으면 진행 중인 작업이 하나도 없게 됩니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:272` 「세션은 이 결과를 캐시에 반영하고」 — "캐시" collides with prompt caching from ch01. ⇒ 세션은 이 결과로 현재 목록을 바꾸고
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:272` 「저장할 상태와 화면에 보일 상태를 따로 다뤄야 합니다.」 — A generic moral, and the premise is stale at HEAD (see missingOrStale). ⇒ delete
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:202` 「다음 행동과 막힌 이유를 모델과 사용자에게 함께 보여 주는 목록.」 — The description promises shared visibility but never says how the harness uses the list. ⇒ Rewrite: 단계별 할 일과 진행 상태를 세션에 기록하고, 열린 일이 남으면 멈추기 전에 모델에게 다시 알리는 목록.
- I code L212-223 (`todo init`) — Good: a concrete payload whose result is explained in the next paragraph. ⇒ Keep it, but render the resulting list (first task `in_progress`, others `pending`) next to the call so the normalization can be seen.
- I exchange L250-269 (변경 전 → `block` 적용 / 정규화된 다음 작업 / 입력 도착 뒤) — Misused block type. The three "outputs" are successive states, and the third comes from a later, separate `unblock` call, but the exchange fan-out presents them as alternatives. ⇒ Use a two-call tool-sequence (`block` with reason → list showing `blocked` + auto-promoted `무효화 조건 수정`; later `unblock` → `pending`), or a static before/after list pair.
