Task names in code spans render as spaced monospace Hangul ('캐 시 키 추 적'). ⇒ Use a 3-column state table (변경 전 / block 후 / unblock 후) with both tasks' status in each column. Write task names in quotes, not code spans.

## 36 하위 에이전트

- **Verdict:** keep. It has substantial distinct mechanism: agent discovery and precedence, per-role model and tool selection, a context-free child session, batch `context`, async jobs with `yield` finalization, a recursion, concurrency and budget gate, isolation backends with patch or branch merge, and idle/parked lifecycle.
- **Overall:** The chapter covers the right mechanisms: discovery, context inheritance, async delivery through `yield`/`agent://`/`history://`, limits, isolation and lifecycle. Its setup is weak. It never shows what an agent definition is, and it leaves out the `yield` reminders and budgets that force children to finish. Its diagram ends with follow-up messaging that duplicates ch37 and uses the removed `hub` tool. The isolation execution-path compares unlike scenarios. At HEAD, its claim that isolated children cannot be revived is stale.
- **Organization:** Section 2 answers two questions, how child changes return to the parent and how long a child lives. Split it, or move lifecycle to ch37 or a merged messaging section. Suggested sections: (1) agent definitions and what a child receives; (2) parallel runs and collecting results (async return, `yield`, limits); (3) isolated workspaces; (4) lifecycle and follow-up, which absorbs ch37 if merged.
- **Ordering:** L298 uses "에이전트 정의" and its precedence before saying what a definition contains (frontmatter `tools`, `model`, `spawns`, `output`, `blocking`). Add a short definition code block first. The L320 async paragraph explains the diagram's premise ("비동기 실행이 켜져 있다") only after the diagram.
- **Missing/stale:** Setup: an example agent definition (Markdown frontmatter: `name`, `description`, `tools`, `model`, `spawns`, `output`, `blocking`, `thinking-level`).
- **Missing/stale:** Forced completion: a child that omits `yield` gets up to 3 reminders, the last forcing `toolChoice = yield` (`MAX_YIELD_RETRIES`). Then comes `SYSTEM WARNING: Subagent exited without calling yield tool…`.
- **Missing/stale:** Idle TTL `task.agentIdleTtlMs` is 7 minutes; the soft request budget is 200 with a hard stop at 1.5×; `task.maxRuntimeMs` is off by default.
- **Missing/stale:** Plan-mode restriction on children (cross-reference ch33). `todo` is not inherited.
- **Missing/stale:** STALE at HEAD: L368 "격리 자식은 작업 공간을 정리한 뒤 되살릴 수 없으므로". HEAD docs/tools/task.md: "Kept-alive task agents retain their workspace through idle/parked transitions and can be revived; release captures final changes."
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: `solutionSpace` is now a required task-item field, plus a per-spawn `model` (highest precedence) and eval-defined `tools`. Follow-up guidance changed from `hub` to `write agent://<id>`, `read proc://<id>` and `write proc://<id>/kill`. Running subagents now get periodic completion estimates (`task.completionProbeMs`).

- P `chapters-33-46.ts:290` 「독립된 자식 세션에 역할과 문맥을 주고 병렬 작업의 결과를 부모에게 회수하는 실행 장치.」 — Ends on the vague "실행 장치". ⇒ 부모 에이전트가 역할과 배경을 정해 자식 세션을 띄우고, 그 결과를 돌려받는 방식.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:302` 「승인된 계획처럼 필요한 경계만 이어받습니다.」 — "경계를 이어받다" is an unnatural collocation. ⇒ 자식이 이어받는 것은 작업 디렉터리와 컨텍스트 파일, 스킬, 공유 `local://` 디렉터리, 부모가 넘긴 배경, 승인된 계획뿐입니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:334` 「작업 공간 분리와 자식의 수명」 — Two topics share one title, which is a sign the section should be split. ⇒ Split into “자식의 변경을 부모에 반영하기” and “끝난 자식에게 다시 묻기”.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:338` 「백엔드가 만든 복제·오버레이·작업 트리에서 변경분을 모은 뒤」 — Compressed noun chain, and "작업 트리" is wrong: the backends are APFS/Btrfs/ZFS/reflink clones, overlayfs, ProjFS, block-clone and rcopy (docs/tools/task.md). ⇒ 격리 백엔드는 저장소를 복제하거나 오버레이로 덮어 자식 전용 디렉터리를 만듭니다. 자식이 끝나면 바뀐 내용을 패치로 만들어 적용하거나, `omp/task/<id>` 브랜치에 커밋한 뒤 부모 쪽으로 가져옵니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:320` 「동시 실행 수, 재귀 깊이, 요청 예산, 벽시계 제한은 부모 세션의 런타임이 강제합니다.」 — A list of limits with no values or consequences. ⇒ 부모 세션은 동시에 실행할 자식 수(`task.maxConcurrency`)와 재귀 깊이를 제한합니다. 자식이 요청 200번(`task.softRequestBudget`)을 넘기면 정리하라는 알림을 받고, 1.5배에 이르면 그때까지의 결과를 내고 멈춥니다.
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:338` 「이 기능은 같은 파일을 고치는 병렬 작업끼리 충돌을 줄입니다.」 — Overstated. Isolation stops children from overwriting each other during execution, but conflicts still appear at merge, which is exactly what the execution-path shows. ⇒ Replace with: 자식들은 실행 중에 서로의 파일 변경을 덮어쓰지 않습니다. 같은 줄을 고쳤다면 그 충돌은 부모에 반영할 때 드러납니다.
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:368` 「하네스는 “완료 결과가 보존됨”과 “같은 실행 인스턴스를 다시 깨울 수 있음”을 구분해야 합니다.」 — A generic closing moral. It is also stale, because at HEAD isolated kept-alive agents can be revived. ⇒ delete
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:298` 「프로젝트의 `.omp/agents`, 사용자 에이전트, 플러그인, 번들 에이전트 순으로 같은 이름의 정의를 찾습니다.」 — A precedence list with no decision attached. The only consequence that matters is override. ⇒ Shorten to: 같은 이름이면 프로젝트 정의가 사용자·번들 정의보다 우선하므로, 저장소마다 기본 에이전트를 바꿔 쓸 수 있습니다.
- I tool-sequence L305-317 (두 조사를 병렬로 맡기고 결과를 회수하기) — The relationship async mode creates, `task` returning IDs at once so the parent keeps working, is not shown. Events 5-6 (follow-up through `hub`) duplicate ch37's sequence and use the removed `hub` tool. The actor "`task`·`hub` 조정 계층" is stale. ⇒ Insert “즉시 반환: 에이전트 ID 두 개 · 부모는 다른 작업 계속” after event 1. Cut events 5-6 and leave follow-up to the messaging section. Rename the actor to “`task` 런타임”.
- I execution-path L341-365 (패치 방식 / 브랜치 방식) — The scenarios differ: the patch tab ends in a conflict and the branch tab in success, so the reader cannot compare the methods. The merge method is a setting (`task.isolation.merge`), and automatic application is governed by `task.isolation.apply`, which is not mentioned. ⇒ Use the same conflicting input in both tabs. Patch: apply check fails → `<id>.patch` kept, nothing applied. Branch: parent WIP stashed → cherry-pick stops at the conflict → stash restored, `omp/task/<id>` kept for manual merge (`task/worktree.ts mergeTaskBranches`).
- R [tool-sequence ✗] TS L305 두 조사를 병렬로 맡기고 결과를 회수하기 (6 steps; 933px / 831px) — Both children share one actor ('자식 세션 A·B'), so the parallelism in the title can't be seen. Steps 5–6 (follow-up to an existing agent) duplicate ch37. ⇒ Cut steps 5–6. Show A and B as separate events (2a/2b), or use an exchange: batch → child A / child B.
- R [execution-path ✓] EP L341 자식 작업을 원래 작업 공간에 반영하는 두 방식 (2 tabs × 3; 604px / 719px) — Unfair comparison: the patch tab shows a conflict failure, while the branch tab shows success. ⇒ Use the same scenario (with a conflict) in both tabs.

## 37 에이전트 간 통신

- **Verdict:** merge → 36 (하위 에이전트), as a section “자식과 메시지 주고받기”. Peer messaging exists only among task-spawned agents (`isIrcEnabled`: true for subagents or sessions that can spawn). The lifecycle it depends on (idle/parked/revive) and follow-up messaging are already in ch36 L314-315 and L368, and job waiting is in ch12. What remains distinct, the mailbox bus (cap 100), aside versus wake delivery, broadcast to visible peers and race-safe `wait`, fits one section. Agent Hub can be a short subsection of that merged section or an example in ch38. Alternative: keep two chapters, but re-split them as ch36 “생성과 격리” and ch37 “수명·결과 전달·통신”, moving ch36's async delivery and idle/parked material here.
- **Overall:** The chapter is accurate for the snapshot but built around the `hub` tool, which HEAD removed on 2026-09-23 (f89a6db15e). Messaging is now `write agent://<id>` / `agent://all`, the roster is `read history://`, and an argument-free `wait` blocks only when the agent has nothing else to do. Even at the snapshot, its content overlaps ch36 (revival, follow-up) and ch12 (`wait` on owned jobs). The Agent Hub half is a TUI walkthrough whose exchange misstates behavior.
- **Organization:** Section 1 (messaging) and section 2 (human UI) answer different questions for different actors (model vs person). If kept, frame section 2 as the human-side view of the same registry, and cut UI trivia. The `wait` paragraph belongs with ch12's job waiting or should explicitly reference it.
- **Ordering:** It depends on ch36 terms (`parked`, agent ID), which is acceptable because ch36 comes first. It introduces `wait` with no reference to ch12, where readers first met waiting on jobs.
- **Missing/stale:** STALE at HEAD (f89a6db15e, 2026-09-23): `hub` removed. Messaging is `write agent://<id>` / `agent://all`, the roster is `read history://`, and job and service control moved to `proc://`. `wait` takes no arguments, returns on the first owned-job result, peer message or steering interrupt, uses a 5/10/30/60/300 s message-only ladder, and results auto-deliver ("Continue useful work instead of polling"). The reference `docs/tools/hub.md` no longer exists.
- **Missing/stale:** Delivery semantics: a running recipient receives a non-interrupting aside (`irc:incoming`), an idle one gets a wake turn, and a parked one is revived. A broadcast reaches visible live peers without reviving parked ones. The mailbox cap is 100 messages.
- **Missing/stale:** Messaging availability is derived, not configured (`isIrcEnabled`). Advisor rows are excluded from peer workflows.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD Agent Hub additions: an activity log (`2`, with filters and scope), the `/` filter, a pinned Subagents jump list above the editor, and click-to-focus with `tui.mouse`.

- P `chapters-33-46.ts:386` 「실행 중이거나 보관된 에이전트를 주소로 삼아 메시지, 깨우기, 완료 신호를 전달하는 조정 계층.」 — Noun list ending in the vague "조정 계층". ⇒ 에이전트끼리 ID로 메시지를 보내고, 쉬고 있는 에이전트를 깨워 답을 받는 방식.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:394` 「직접 메시지를 받은 보관 상태의 에이전트는 세션 기록에서 되살아납니다.」 — "보관 상태" clashes with ch36's `parked`, and the sentence is passive-ish. ⇒ `parked` 상태의 에이전트에게 메시지를 보내면, OMP가 저장된 세션 기록을 다시 열어 그 에이전트를 깨웁니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:410` 「완료 알림과 사람의 새 지시가 경쟁할 때 하나의 대기 지점에서 순서를 정하므로, 폴링 사이에 메시지를 잃지 않습니다.」 — Wrong actor (`wait` races job results and peer-agent messages, not human instructions) and the abstract "대기 지점". ⇒ 작업 완료와 다른 에이전트의 메시지 중 먼저 도착한 쪽이 `wait`를 끝내고, 나중에 온 쪽도 버려지지 않고 이어서 전달됩니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:441` 「실행 제어와 화면 초점을 분리해야 관찰하려고 연 작업을 실수로 취소하지 않습니다.」 — Abstract moral phrasing. ⇒ 그래서 살펴보려고 연 자식을 실수로 멈추지 않습니다.
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:428` 「값이 없는 사용량은 추정하지 않고 비어 있다고 표시합니다.」 — UI trivia unrelated to the chapter's question. ⇒ delete
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:394` 「`hub`의 메시지 기능은 프로세스 전체의 에이전트 레지스트리와 우편함 버스를 사용합니다. `list`로 … `send`로 …」 — Stale at HEAD: the `hub` tool, `list` and `send` were removed (f89a6db15e); `irc/messaging.ts executeSend` now takes only `{to, message}`. ⇒ Rewrite around `write agent://<id>` (or `agent://all`), `read history://` for the roster, and `wait`.
- I tool-sequence L397-407 (부모의 질문이 보관된 자식의 답으로 돌아오기까지) — Useful: it shows revival plus the two delivery modes (aside while running, release of `wait`). The `replyTo` detail is stale at HEAD, and the child's answer is itself a message to the parent, which the actor labels hide. ⇒ Change event 1 to `write agent://CacheScout`, drop `replyTo`, and label event 3 “`CacheScout`가 부모에게 메시지 전송”. Keep the delivery-mode detail.
- I exchange L431-438 (선택한 에이전트 → 대화 읽기/다시 실행/종료) — These are UI actions, not outputs. The distinction between “대화 읽기” and “다시 실행” is also false: `Enter` on a parked agent focuses it and revives it (docs/agent-hub.md), and `history://` is a model-facing URL, not a Hub control. ⇒ Replace with a small key table (`Enter` 포커스·parked면 되살림, `r` 되살리기, `x` 중단 후 제거, `Esc` 메인으로 복귀), or delete it and keep the L441 paragraph.
- R [tool-sequence ✓] TS L397 부모의 질문이 보관된 자식의 답으로 돌아오기까지 (4 steps; 645px / 599px) — The child's reply uses `answer` and the wake uses `request`. The label '우편함 전달' is a literal calque. ⇒ Keep; fix the kinds; relabel '부모에게 답장 전달'.
- R [exchange ✗] EX L431 선택한 에이전트 → 대화 읽기 / 다시 실행 / 종료 (3 outputs; 478px / 710px) — A menu of Hub actions. ⇒ Use a table: 키·동작 / 결과.

## 38 터미널 인터페이스

- **Verdict:** keep. Distinct mechanism: the frame-provider contract, history batches with ID acknowledgement, a viewport diff, resize replay modes, ANSI-aware width, and focus and overlay input routing.
- **Overall:** The differential-rendering section is excellent and faithful to `docs/tui-core-renderer.md`. Its key insight, that finality is an application decision and not inferred from scrolling, is well chosen. Section 1 is weaker: an architecture split that is stale at HEAD, an event-mapping exchange whose third output does not follow from its input, and an extension-author component contract that belongs in ch31. HEAD also adds a native rendering path that bypasses the history/viewport machinery.
- **Organization:** Section 1 covers three things: the package split, how events map to components, and the component and extension contract. Keep the first two and move the `render`/`handleInput`/`dispose`/`done`/`hasUI` contract to ch31 (extensions). Section 2 is well grouped.
- **Ordering:** Mostly sound. The tool-sequence in section 2 places its trigger (the next text chunk) last; put it first.
- **Missing/stale:** STALE at HEAD (0d6dbd32fc, 2026-09-17): UI and tool renderers moved to `@oh-my-pi/pi-tui` (`packages/tui/src/chat/tool-execution.ts`, `packages/tui/src/chrome/transcript-container.ts`, `packages/tui/src/tools/*`). L466's description of `packages/coding-agent` wiring tool renderers, and the reference to `packages/coding-agent/src/modes/components/tool-execution.ts`, are outdated.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: native rendering through the Tern Surface Protocol (`native/backend.ts`). Components are described and reconciled into document ops, and "none of this document's history, viewport, resize-replay or CPR machinery runs on that path". Also new: in-place resize (Warp, `PI_TUI_RESIZE_IN_PLACE`) and tmux client terminal detection.
- **Missing/stale:** Snapshot detail worth a sentence: assistant text is an append-only head that emits only stable complete rows while open Markdown stays mutable, and `replay` versus `append` batch kinds.

- P `chapters-33-46.ts:458` 「에이전트 이벤트를 대화 기록, 편집기, 도구 카드로 바꾸고 달라진 화면만 다시 그리는 터미널 계층.」 — Events do not "become" an editor, and the description ends on the vague "계층". ⇒ 세션 이벤트를 대화 기록과 도구 카드로 그리고, 바뀐 행만 다시 쓰는 터미널 화면.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:462` 「실행 상태를 터미널 구성 요소로 보여 주기」 — "실행 상태" is abstract. ⇒ 세션 이벤트를 화면 구성 요소로 바꾸기
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:466` 「에이전트 실행 상태를 터미널 제어 코드와 분리하므로 같은 세션을 SDK나 RPC에서도 실행할 수 있습니다.」 — Noun-heavy, with no actor. ⇒ 세션은 터미널을 모른 채 이벤트만 내보내므로, 같은 세션을 SDK나 RPC에서도 실행할 수 있습니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:529` 「하네스의 이벤트 수명과 렌더러의 행 수명이 같은 경계를 사용해야 깜박임과 중복 기록을 피할 수 있습니다.」 — Abstract "수명/경계" moral that restates the previous sentence. ⇒ 그래서 블록이 완료되었다는 이벤트가 곧 그 행을 기록으로 보낼 시점입니다.
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:491` 「헤드리스 모드에서는 이런 UI가 없으므로 확장은 `hasUI`를 확인해야 합니다.」 — A tangent for extension authors, and imprecise at HEAD: RPC may report `hasUI === true` while still not supporting `custom()` (docs/tui.md diff). ⇒ Move the whole L491 paragraph to ch31, or cut it to one sentence: 각 구성 요소는 `render(width)`로 화면 행을 돌려줍니다.
- I exchange L469-488 (세션 이벤트 → 대화/도구/입력과 초점) — The third output ("입력과 초점") does not follow from the listed session events, since key input is not a session event. Prose is dressed as a mapping. ⇒ Cut the third output. Keep the two event→component mappings, and if useful show the tool card's partial and complete renders (`renderCall` then `renderResult`).
- I tool-sequence L511-522 (스트리밍 답변 한 줄을 안전하게 갱신하기) — Good choice of relationship (history append, ID acknowledgement, viewport diff). The ordering is off, though: the trigger "다음 텍스트 조각" comes last and makes the run look like a loop with five steps. ⇒ Start with “텍스트 조각 도착”, then 프레임 계획 → 기록 추가 → 기록 확인(ID) → `viewport` 차이 갱신. Drop the final event.
- R [exchange ✓] EX L469 세션 이벤트 → 대화 / 도구 / 입력과 초점 구성 요소 (3 outputs; 477px / 711px) — The third fan-out of the same event set (see ch03 and ch04). ⇒ Keep only the TUI-specific part (the focus/cursor card). Reference ch03 for the rest.
- R [tool-sequence ✗] TS L511 스트리밍 답변 한 줄을 안전하게 갱신하기 (5 steps; 719px / 656px) — Internal calls don't show what changes on screen, and the trace ends mid-loop. '프레임 계획' uses `request` (the model colour). ⇒ Use a static terminal mock in two regions (history rows written once / viewport rows rewritten), before and after the next chunk.

## 39 SDK, RPC, ACP 인터페이스

- **Verdict:** keep. Three host interfaces with distinct transports, lifecycles and capability negotiation (SDK object lifecycle, RPC JSONL framing and correlation, ACP capability-gated routing of fs and terminal to the editor). That is enough for a chapter.
- **Overall:** The chapter has a good comparative frame, and the SDK lifecycle code block is useful. It falls short on two counts. First, it states the axis "who provides files, terminal and approval UI" but never says that RPC hosts can also provide tools, URI schemes and UI through sub-protocols. Second, its RPC completion rule is incomplete at HEAD, which now separates ack, yield (`prompt_result`) and `session_settled`. The RPC paragraph is overloaded. The RPC diagram skips the process boundary it is meant to teach, and the ACP diagram pads a skipped stage.
- **Organization:** Sections are grouped well (comparison, SDK/RPC lifecycle, ACP). The RPC half of section 2 needs its own subsection on correlation and completion, kept separate from the streaming steer/followUp detail, which should reference ch06.
- **Ordering:** "호스트" is used from L555 onward without being defined. L614 explains in prose what the following diagram shows, before the diagram appears. Put the diagram first, then explain the local-command and ordering cases.
- **Missing/stale:** Snapshot: `--mode rpc-ui` (`extension_ui_request` frames), the Extension UI sub-protocol, the Host Tool sub-protocol (`set_host_tools`) and the Host URI sub-protocol (`set_host_uri_schemes`) are all in snapshot docs/rpc.md and are the RPC counterpart of ACP's editor-provided fs and terminal.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: the completion model has three levels. `prompt_result` reports the yield, and `session_settled` / `prompt_result.sessionSettled` / `get_state.isSettled` report that the session is done after background work (async `task`, auto-backgrounded `bash`, `eval`) can no longer wake it. This connects directly to ch12 and ch36.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD c4d3204151 (2026-10-03): a machine-readable RPC wire schema (`modes/rpc/wire`, `bun run gen:rpc`) and generated clients in `sdk/python`, `sdk/rust` and `sdk/go`. The cited `python/omp-rpc/README.md` was removed.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: RPC `goal` command (a82a6882ac). SDK host options `hasUI` and `interactivePrompts` (docs/sdk.md diff).
- **Missing/stale:** ACP exposes plan mode as an ACP session mode and handles `xd://propose` writes (`acp-agent.ts` ~L1845-1918). This is a natural cross-reference to ch33.

- P `chapters-33-46.ts:547` 「같은 세션 런타임을 프로세스 안, 표준 입출력 너머, 편집기 안에서 제어하는 세 가지 경계.」 — Ends on an abstract "경계", and ACP also runs over stdio, so the three items are not parallel. ⇒ 다른 프로그램이 OMP 세션을 직접 호출하거나, 자식 프로세스로 띄워 JSON으로 조종하거나, 편집기에 연결하는 세 가지 방법.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:551` 「호스트가 런타임과 만나는 위치」 — Abstract spatial metaphor. ⇒ 세 인터페이스는 무엇이 다른가
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:555` 「차이는 누가 프로세스를 소유하고, 명령과 이벤트가 어느 경계를 건너며, 파일·터미널·승인 UI를 누가 제공하는가에 있습니다.」 — "호스트" is undefined and "경계를 건너며" is abstract. ⇒ 여기서 호스트는 OMP 세션을 띄우고 조종하는 프로그램입니다. 세 방식은 세션을 같은 프로세스에서 부르는지 자식 프로세스로 띄우는지, 그리고 파일·터미널·승인 화면을 누가 제공하는지가 다릅니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:577` 「직접 함수 호출과 프로토콜 호출은 장애 경계도 다릅니다.」 — "장애 경계" is jargon. ⇒ 프로세스를 나누면 실패를 처리하는 방법도 달라집니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:614` 「RPC에서는 각 명령의 `id`가 비동기 응답을 연결합니다. 모델 턴을 시작한 `prompt`는 … 스트리밍 중 새 프롬프트는 `steer`인지 `followUp`인지도 명시해야 합니다.」 — Five separate rules in one paragraph (correlation, completion, local commands, ordering, streaming behavior). ⇒ Split it. First: 응답은 도착 순서가 아니라 요청 `id`로 짝지어야 합니다. 긴 `bash`나 다른 명령의 응답이 먼저 올 수 있기 때문입니다. Then the diagram. Then: 슬래시 명령처럼 모델을 부르지 않는 프롬프트는 `data.agentInvoked: false`나 뒤이은 `prompt_result`로 끝나며 `agent_end`를 내보내지 않습니다. Move steer/followUp to a ch06 reference.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:645` 「권한 요청은 클라이언트가 광고하는 기능이 아니라 에이전트 정책에 따라 편집기 UI로 보내는 별도 경계입니다.」 — A confusing "X가 아니라 Y" with an abstract "경계". ⇒ 파일·터미널 기능은 편집기가 광고해야 쓰지만, 권한 요청은 그와 상관없이 OMP가 승인이 필요한 도구(`edit`, `bash` 등)를 실행하기 전에 편집기에 보냅니다.
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:577` 「기능 목록만 맞추는 대신 이 수명 경계를 먼저 선택해야 합니다.」 — Vague advice that offers no criterion. ⇒ delete
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:675` 「ACP는 세션 업데이트, 도구 호출, 권한 요청 같은 의미 이벤트를 주고받고 편집기가 자기 UI로 표현하는 규약입니다.」 — Restates the section and the diagram, with the jargon "의미 이벤트". ⇒ Delete, or keep one sentence naming the two files: `acp-agent.ts`가 세션 이벤트를 ACP 메시지로 바꾸고, `acp-client-bridge.ts`가 파일·터미널·권한 요청을 편집기로 보냅니다.
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:614` 「모델 턴을 시작한 `prompt`는 `agent_end`의 `isTerminal !== false`까지 기다려야 끝나지만」 — Presented as the complete rule. At HEAD `agent_end` carries no prompt identity, so prompts must be correlated through `prompt_result`, and the session is done only at `session_settled`, because background jobs can wake the agent after it yields (docs/rpc.md “Yield vs settled”). ⇒ Add the three levels: 명령 수락(`response`) → 모델 턴 종료(`prompt_result`) → 세션 종료(`session_settled`).
- I exchange L558-574 (공통 런타임 → SDK/RPC/ACP) — An acceptable comparison, but the RPC row omits that hosts can provide tools, URI schemes and UI (`set_host_tools`, `set_host_uri_schemes`, `--mode rpc-ui`). That makes ACP look unique on the very axis L555 defines. ⇒ Add to the RPC output: “`set_host_tools`로 호스트 도구를 제공하고, `rpc-ui`에서는 선택·확인 대화 상자를 호스트가 그립니다.” Or convert to a static table with columns 프로세스 / 전송 / 도구·UI 제공자.
- I tool-sequence L617-628 (모델 턴을 시작한 RPC 요청의 승인과 완료를 구분하기) — The ack-versus-completion contrast is the right thing to show. The separate `AgentSession` actor, though, sends frames straight to the host, skipping the stdout process boundary that RPC is about. The local-command case explained in L614 is not shown. ⇒ Use two actors (RPC 호스트, `omp --mode rpc` 프로세스). Turn it into an execution-path with two tabs. 모델 턴: `response success` → 이벤트 프레임 → `prompt_result` → `agent_end isTerminal` (→ `session_settled` at HEAD). 로컬 명령: `command_output` → `data.agentInvoked: false`, no `agent_end`.
- I execution-path L648-672 (편집기 파일 기능 있음 / 필요한 기능 없음) — The second tab pads a skipped "편집기 동기화" stage, and both results are abstract ("같은 문서 상태", "실행 경계가 유지됩니다"). The consequence that can actually be observed, unsaved buffer versus disk, is stated only in prose. ⇒ Input: “편집기에 저장하지 않은 수정이 있는 `src/cache.ts`”. Tab A: `fs/read_text_file` returns the buffer including the unsaved line. Tab B: local disk read returns the old content without it. Drop the skipped stage.
- R [exchange ✗] EX L558 공통 런타임 → SDK / RPC / ACP (3 outputs; 529px / 736px) — A taxonomy with the arrow backwards: hosts reach the runtime, not the other way round. ⇒ Reverse it (3 hosts → AgentSession) or use a table: 호스트 / 전송 / 프로세스.
- R [tool-sequence ✓] TS L617 모델 턴을 시작한 RPC 요청의 승인과 완료를 구분하기 (5 steps; 700px / 662px) — Good: the ack (step 2) comes before completion (step 5). The `agent_end` event uses `answer`, and the title is long. ⇒ Keep. Retitle 'RPC `prompt`의 즉시 응답과 실행 완료'.
- R [execution-path ✓] EP L648 같은 파일 수정이 ACP 기능에 따라 실행되는 방식 (2 tabs × 3; 605px / 719px) — A real capability alternative. ⇒ Keep.

### Range patterns (ch 33–39)

- Section-final moral sentences of the form “하네스는 …해야 합니다” / “…해야 …할 수 있습니다” appear 8 times across all 7 chapters, adding no condition or consequence. Examples: L93 “하네스는 계획의 “승인 여부”, … 어긋나지 않게 유지해야 합니다.”, L183 “하네스는 두 상태를 섞지 않아야 …”, L368 “하네스는 “완료 결과가 보존됨”과 … 구분해야 합니다.” Others: L42, L272, L441, L529, L577. Delete them, or replace each with the exact failure condition (as in the ch33 bash-guard rewrite).
- Non-literal “경계” appears about 10 times in prose: L42 “같은 경계를 가리켜야”, L302 “필요한 경계만 이어받습니다”, L577 “장애 경계도… 수명 경계를”, plus L529, L547, L555, L645, L669. Abstract “상태” also appears where nothing literal is meant: L17 ×3 “세션 상태/계획 모드 상태/이 상태를”, L462/L466 “실행 상태”. Replace each with the actor and the action.
- The exchange block is misused as a bulleted list in 5 of 7 chapters. ch33 fans out policy categories, ch34 facets of one record, ch35 sequential states, ch37 UI actions, and in ch38 the third output does not follow from the input. Only ch39's comparison and ch01/ch02-style input→output uses fit the block's meaning.
- Chapter descriptions end on vague category nouns in 6 of 7: “실행 정책” (33), “장치” (34), “실행 장치” (36), “조정 계층” (37), “터미널 계층” (38), “세 가지 경계” (39). Ch01/ch02 descriptions instead state what the subject does.
- Identifier dumps without meaning appear at least 5 times: ch33 L17 field names, ch34 L146 ops, ch35 L210 nine ops plus five statuses, ch36 L298 precedence chain, ch37 L428 roster columns.
- Model-side behavior is attributed to the runtime, or enforcement is overstated, 3 times: ch34 L175 continuation “실패가 남아 있으므로”, ch34 L140 completion gated on test results, ch33 L42 prompt/guard parity (bash is unguarded).
- Terms are used before they are introduced 4 times: `xd://propose` (33 L42), paused/`resume` (34 L146), 에이전트 정의 (36 L298), 호스트 (39 L555).
- Interactive steps are padded or out of order 3 times: ch38 sequence trigger placed last, ch39 ACP skipped “편집기 동기화” stage, ch36 sequence tail duplicating ch37.
- Snapshot staleness at HEAD 3f000c52 affects 5 of 7 chapters: `hub` removal (36, 37), the TUI package split and native rendering (38), RPC settle semantics and generated clients (39), todo resume/HUD (35), isolated-agent revival (36), RPC goal command (34). Pinned `3b3a6dc9` links still resolve, but three cited files no longer exist at HEAD: `docs/tools/hub.md`, `packages/coding-agent/src/modes/components/tool-execution.ts`, `python/omp-rpc/README.md`.
- Cross-chapter: `hub` removal (f89a6db15e, 2026-09-23) affects ch12 (tools-and-execution.ts L483, “`hub`의 작업 조회와 대기…”), ch36 (L308, L315) and ch37 (whole section 1). Rewrite these consistently to `wait`, `read proc://` / `write proc://<id>/kill` and `write agent://<id>`. Peer ReviewCh09to13 should check ch12/13 for the same issue.
- Cross-chapter: Duplication: ch36 L314-315 (follow-up through `hub` to an existing child) repeats ch37's tool-sequence. ch37 L410 (`wait` racing jobs and messages) repeats ch12's job waiting. ch39 L614 steer/followUp repeats ch06 (agent-loop.ts L105); reference ch06 instead.
- Cross-chapter: Misplacement: the ch38 L491 component and extension-UI contract (`render`/`handleInput`/`dispose`/`done`/`hasUI`) belongs in ch31 (extensions). Agent Hub (ch37 section 2) is a TUI surface and could become a ch38 example if ch37 merges into ch36.
- Cross-chapter: Plan mode (33) and goals (34) share the same mode infrastructure: `appendModeChange`, mutual exclusion, mode prompts and tool activation, and plan-mode suppression of goal continuation. Explain it once in ch33 and reference it from ch34. The plan-mode subagent restriction should be stated in ch33 and referenced from ch36 (L302 mentions only inheritance of the approved plan).
- Cross-chapter: Merging ch35 into ch34 gives one chapter on stop/continue control: todo stop-time reminders, goal continuation and the completion audit. Ch33's plan-mode settle enforcement uses the same pattern (hidden reminder plus `toolChoice: required`), so cross-reference it.
- Cross-chapter: ch34's token accounting excludes `cacheRead` (L164). Linking explicitly to ch01's prompt-caching section would make that design choice meaningful.
- Cross-chapter: ch39's `session_settled` (HEAD) depends on background work from ch12 and async subagents from ch36. Cross-reference both. Ch33's approved-plan re-injection after compaction should reference ch25 (compaction).
- Cross-chapter: ch44 (chapters-33-46.ts L1101) relies on Agent Hub control by collab guests. If ch37 is merged or re-scoped, keep a stable target for that reference.
- Cross-chapter: Terminology drift: `parked` is “보관 상태” in ch37 L394 but `parked` in ch36 L368. `local://` is “아티팩트 공간/계획용 저장소” in ch33 L32 but “공유 `local://` 아티팩트” in ch36 L302. Align both with ch27 (artifacts and internal URLs).

## 40 사용량 통계

- **Verdict:** merge → 41 벤치마크 (one chapter such as '사용량 통계와 벤치마크' or '실행 측정과 평가'). The distinct mechanism is one incremental JSONL parser feeding SQLite plus GROUP BY queries by model, folder, and hour. The chapter's own conclusion (L766: correlation, not cause → go look at the actual requests) hands off to controlled comparison, which is ch41's subject. Together they answer one harness question, 'did my harness change help, and at what cost?': observational data from real sessions vs controlled experiments. Separately, each chapter is two short sections.
- **Overall:** Accurate on the metric formulas (cache rate and tokens/s match `packages/stats/README.md`) and the cost caveat, but thin. It describes the `omp-stats` package's per-request metrics and skips the parts that matter most to harness engineering: tool-call statistics and the split between main agents and subagents. Its one interactive block is a hypothetical procedure with no data. It ends with two defensive/generic sentences. Teaches harness engineering only weakly, through the cache-diagnosis example; otherwise it is a package description.
- **Organization:** The two sections ('which records become metrics' / 'how to slice them') are grouped sensibly. The cost-estimate paragraph (L718) belongs with the metric definitions, where it already sits. Missing a third question the package answers: which tools consume context and fail (tool_calls: callsInTurn, argsChars, resultChars, isError, agentType). The execution-path asks the reader to pick model-vs-project as alternatives, but in practice both slices are applied; the grouping implies a choice that doesn't exist.
- **Ordering:** Fine internally: metrics are defined before the cache-drop example uses them. It relies on ch01/ch02 for prompt caching and ch24 for session JSONL; both come earlier, but neither is cross-referenced. The execution-path's 'project' branch suggests unstable instruction/tool-definition order as a cause without pointing back to ch02 L331, where that rule is taught.
- **Missing/stale:** Tool-call statistics already exist in the snapshot parser/server (`packages/stats/src/parser.ts` extractToolCalls/extractToolResultLink; `/api/stats/tools`): calls per turn, argument chars, result chars fed back into context, tool error flag. This is the most harness-relevant data in the package and is absent.
- **Missing/stale:** Agent-type split: transcripts are classified as main / subagent / advisor by path (`classifyAgentType`), so subagent cost is separable. Absent.
- **Missing/stale:** Cache Savings metric (README table) and per-request drill-down (`/api/request/:id`, `/api/session/trace`) are absent. The drill-down is the natural answer to L766.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale framing at HEAD: '완료된 세션' — HEAD's README describes a live dashboard that ingests newest-first in the background, a watcher that re-syncs transcripts as they're written, hourly rollup tables, and SSE `/api/events`. `GET /api/sync` became `POST /api/sync`.

- P `chapters-33-46.ts:694 (description)` 「완료된 세션 기록을 요청·비용·속도·오류 지표로 바꿔 하네스의 실제 운용을 비교하는 관측 계층.」 — Noun-stacked definition ending in the abstraction '관측 계층'. '하네스의 실제 운용을 비교' is vague. '완료된' is inaccurate: the parser ingests crash-truncated and in-progress transcripts (missing stopReason → 'aborted'), and at HEAD a watcher re-syncs transcripts as they are written. ⇒ 세션 기록에서 모델 요청과 도구 호출의 토큰·비용·지연·오류를 모아 모델·프로젝트·기간별로 비교하는 로컬 통계 도구.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:718` 「비용 값은 기록된 토큰에 일치하는 공개 API 단가를 적용한 API 환산 추정치입니다.」 — '토큰에 일치하는 단가' attaches 'matching' to the wrong noun (the price matches the model, not the tokens). 'API 환산 추정치' is a literal calque. ⇒ 비용은 기록된 토큰 수에 같은 모델의 공개 API 단가를 곱해 계산한 추정치입니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:718` 「통계 화면이 계산의 의미와 누락 조건을 함께 보여 줘야 서로 다른 결제 방식을 잘못 비교하지 않습니다.」 — Generic should-statement. '누락 조건' is unnatural. Replace it with the concrete behaviour from the README. ⇒ 공개 가격이 없는 구독 전용 모델은 `N/A`로 표시되고 금액 합계에서 빠집니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:736` 「같은 원시 요청을 여러 화면에서 다시 계산하지 않고 SQLite 쿼리 결과를 공유하므로 자동 보고와 사람이 보는 차트가 같은 기준을 사용합니다.」 — '원시 요청을 다시 계산' is awkward. The design rationale is not stated in the source [INFERENCE]. ⇒ 콘솔 요약, `--json` 출력, 웹 대시보드는 모두 같은 SQLite 집계를 읽습니다.
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:702` 「대화 화면을 다시 긁는 대신 런타임이 남긴 구조화 기록을 관측 자료로 사용합니다.」 — Contrasts with an alternative (screen scraping) that no one proposed. The previous sentence already says it reads session JSONL. ⇒ delete
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:766` 「이 분석은 완료된 실행의 상관관계를 보여 주지만 원인을 자동으로 증명하지는 않습니다. … 통계 계층의 역할은 한 번의 성공적인 대화 대신 반복 실행의 비용·속도·오류 패턴을 드러내는 것입니다.」 — A negative qualification without a decision, followed by a generic role statement that restates the description. ⇒ Replace with the concrete next step the package supports: '범위를 좁힌 뒤에는 요청 상세(`/api/request/:id`)나 세션 추적 화면에서 해당 요청의 실제 입력과 설정을 확인합니다.'
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:740` 「캐시율 하락을 원인까지 좁히기」 — The title promises 'down to the cause', and L766 contradicts it. ⇒ '캐시율이 떨어진 범위 좁히기'
- I exchange L705-715 (완료한 요청 기록 → 캐시율/출력 속도/오류·지연) — The first two outputs correctly derive values from the input. The third ignores the input's values (`2,000 ms`, `350 ms`, `stopReason: stop`) and states a general aggregation rule, which breaks the one-input→outputs contract. Input label '입력 6,000' doesn't say it excludes cached tokens, which the 40% formula depends on. Tokens/s silently includes the 350 ms TTFT. ⇒ Label the input '캐시되지 않은 입력 `6,000`'. Make output 3 concrete: '`stopReason: stop` → 오류 아님 · 평균 지연에 `2,000 ms`, 평균 TTFT에 `350 ms` 반영'. Optionally note in output 2 that the 2 s includes the 350 ms wait for the first token.
- I execution-path L738-763 (캐시율 하락: 모델별 / 프로젝트별) — Prose dressed as a diagram. The stages are generic advice with no numbers, so nothing is observed. The tabs present model vs project as alternative paths when an analyst would do both. The model path's third stage is labelled '다른 프로젝트', marked `skipped`, with text about excluding healthy models, so label, state, and text disagree. ⇒ Replace with one static breakdown showing illustrative numbers on a fixed scale (overall 62%→41%; by model: A 64%→63%, B 60%→22%) and one conclusion line ('B 모델 요청에서만 떨어졌다 → B의 요청 형식·설정 변경 확인'). Or keep tabs only if each tab shows the data that distinguishes the outcomes.
- R [exchange ✓] EX L705 완료한 요청 기록 → 캐시율 / 출력 속도 / 오류·지연 (3 outputs; 376px / 609px) — A real computed transform with arithmetic, but the third card has no numbers. ⇒ Give the error-rate/TTFT figures or drop the third card.
- R [execution-path ✗] EP L739 캐시율 하락을 원인까지 좁히기 (2 tabs × 3; 603px / 693px) — Hypothetical ('한 모델에서만 낮다면') with no data. `skipped` is misused ('다른 프로젝트'). ⇒ Use a small table of per-model and per-project cache rates where the drop is visible.

## 41 벤치마크

- **Verdict:** keep → absorb ch40 as its observational half. Distinct mechanism: experiment/arm/trace model, fixture contract, formatter-normalized verifier, resumable runs, credential gateway. It directly evaluates the harness components taught in ch10 (editing). It teaches harness engineering, not just a package.
- **Overall:** The strongest harness-engineering content in this range: hold the sample, model, and attempts fixed, vary one harness setting, verify outputs independently of the model's claim, and keep traces for diagnosis. But the worked example misrepresents the edit benchmark. Its agent has no check tool and never sees verifier results (default tools `read`/`edit`/`write` in `packages/typescript-edit-benchmark/src/in-process-client.ts`), yet the chapter traces '검사 도구 호출' and 'retry after verification'. The benchmark's stated purpose (edit precision, not bug-finding) is never given. `trial` and Harbor are used without introduction.
- **Organization:** Section 1 (experiment structure) and section 2 (one fixture) are the right two questions. The closing paragraph of section 2 (L846) restates section 1's exchange and L842. The resumability sentence (L808) is useful but mixed in with the 'what to record' rule. It would read better as one sentence on what the manager preserves.
- **Ordering:** Section 1 uses `trial` (L801, L808) without defining it and names Harbor (L792) without saying what it is. Section 2 introduces the fixture by its parts but not its purpose; the reader learns what is verified before learning what is being measured. Consider opening with the question ('편집 도구 설정을 바꾸면 정확도가 나아지는가') and the fixture, then generalising to experiment → run → trace.
- **Missing/stale:** Benchmark purpose and fixture generation: `generate.ts` states 'testing edit precision, not bug-finding'. Prompts fully determine the byte-exact fix, and difficulty tiers (easy/medium/hard/nightmare) vary how much locating the model must do (repeated lines, long files, similar blocks).
- **Missing/stale:** Credential isolation: 'Auth never enters containers' — a generated `models.yml` routes providers through a host auth gateway (metaharness README). Ties to ch23.
- **Missing/stale:** `--install source` runs omp from the repo's TS source, so uncommitted harness changes are measured on the next trial with no rebuild. This is the harness-engineering iteration loop, and it is absent.
- **Missing/stale:** `attempts` (pass@k), calibrated projections per experiment, and `scripts/trace-report.ts` (narrative failure analysis per trace).
- **Missing/stale:** Native Terminal-Bench runner (`bench:tb`) with a lean terminal tool allowlist and `edit.mode: replace`. This shows that benchmark arms also fix the tool surface.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: `packages/metaharness/agent/pi_upstream.py` adds an upstream-pi baseline agent ('same task containers, same host auth gateway, same output/usage accounting'). That is a cross-harness comparison and the clearest harness-vs-harness example available.

## 42 네이티브 모듈

- **Verdict:** demote-to-section → ch09 (a short subsection tracing `grep` across the TS/Rust boundary) plus one line in ch03's package map. Alternatively, ch11 could gain a note that the embedded shell runs in-process Rust builtins.. Amount of distinct, harness-relevant mechanism: one boundary rule (policy in TS, primitive in Rust; cancellation and timeouts cross the boundary). Loader candidate ordering, CPU variants, and stale-addon detection are packaging concerns any Node addon has; they don't change how an agent harness is designed. This describes an omp package rather than teaching harness engineering.
- **Overall:** Mostly a description of how a Node addon is packaged and loaded (platform tags, AVX2 variants, version sentinel, lazy entrypoints). That is distribution engineering, not agent-harness engineering. The one harness lesson, keeping model-facing policy in the TS tool and the primitive in Rust, takes one sentence, and the grep trace duplicates ch09's territory. Several facts are stale at HEAD (sentinel, lazy entrypoints, internal-URL materialization). The crate paragraph is name-dropping and mis-describes `pi-builtins`.
- **Organization:** Section 1 mixes loading (one-time, at import) with a per-call grep exchange. Section 2 then traces the same grep call again, so the grep example is split across two sections and duplicated. L925's crate list is unrelated to either section's question.
- **Ordering:** The section 1 exchange uses `grep` before section 2 introduces `GrepTool`. It also places the package loader inside a per-call flow, although loading happens once at module evaluation (`native/index.js` calls `loadNative()` at import). Merge into one section that starts from the model's `grep` call.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD: 'sentinel' validation (L886) became a release stamp `__piNativesBuildVersion()` (docs/natives-addon-loader-runtime.md diff).
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD: '데스크톱과 클립보드 진입점' (L873). The lazy entrypoints are now desktop, clipboard, path, and vcs.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD: '아카이브나 내부 URL을 검색 가능한 파일로 준비' (L904). Commit 7dc2ef905e ('unified filesystem access for native tools and internal URLs') removed temp-file materialization; native grep/glob/ast resolve `scheme://` paths in place through `InternalUrlFilesystem`/`pi-vfs`. This moves the boundary the chapter is about.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD adds `pi-edit` (edit-mode parsing, matching, staging, application, wrapped by native `EditSession`), so the edit tool's core now crosses this boundary too. It is a better example than the crate list.
- **Missing/stale:** Embedded brush shell with in-process builtins (cross-platform command behaviour) and the Rust task-cancellation contract (`docs/natives-rust-task-cancellation.md`). These are the native pieces with real harness consequences.

It is also inaccurate: `pi-builtins` is the set of in-process command utilities (`cat`, `grep`/`rg`, `sed`, `find`, `jq` …) for the embedded shell, not 'processes'. ⇒ Delete, or keep the one harness-relevant fact: '`bash` 도구의 셸은 Rust에 내장된 brush 셸이고, `cat`·`grep`·`sed` 같은 명령도 별도 프로세스가 아니라 `pi-builtins`의 내장 구현으로 실행됩니다.'

## 43 비트맵 컨텍스트 압축

- **Verdict:** keep → must be anchored as a strategy of ch25 컨텍스트 압축 (consider moving it right after ch25 or into the same category). A substantial distinct mechanism: capped serialization, text edges with an imaged middle, provider-aware shapes priced by actual transport, glyph fallback, frame/payload budgets with foveation, and eval-selected defaults. It changes harness decisions (when to image, how many frames, which model can read it). It is the only peripheral chapter here besides ch46 that teaches harness engineering rather than describing a package, but it currently reads as package documentation detached from ch25.
- **Overall:** Interesting, largely accurate, and genuinely about a harness trade-off (representation vs billed tokens vs legibility). But it never says that SnapCompact is the second method in the default `compaction.methodOrder` and runs only when the current model accepts images. Without those two facts the reader can't tell when it applies. The tool-sequence has the wrong order and actor for edge/middle splitting. Its endings are generic disclaimers instead of the concrete budget mechanisms omp implements.
- **Organization:** Section 1 (how frames are produced and persisted) and section 2 (how shapes are chosen) are the right questions. Missing a 'when does omp choose this method' question (method order, vision gate, overflow safety). L1009's harness advice belongs with concrete budget mechanics, which are absent.
- **Ordering:** The tool-sequence prompt uses `messagesToSummarize` with no definition in this chapter; it relies on ch25 without a pointer. Default position and vision requirement should come first, before the trace.
- **Missing/stale:** Default placement: `compaction.methodOrder` = `["remote", "snapcompact", "handoff", "shake", "soft"]` in both snapshot and HEAD docs/compaction.md. For most non-OpenAI vision models, SnapCompact is the default local compaction.
- **Missing/stale:** Vision gate: requires `model.input` to include `"image"`; otherwise automatic maintenance skips to the next method. Manual `/compact` with custom instructions forces an LLM summary.
- **Missing/stale:** Serialization caps and tool-output dimming (tool results head+tail 2,000 chars, argument caps 500/2,000, tool output in dim ink). This shows how the archive favours conversation over tool noise.
- **Missing/stale:** Frame budgets: `MAX_FRAMES_DEFAULT` 80 as an upper bound, provider image budget, `FRAME_DATA_BYTES_BUDGET` 3,000,000 bytes with one re-render, and HQ/LQ/HQ foveation (`HQ_EDGE_FRAMES` = 3).
- **Missing/stale:** Later compactions re-render from the bounded source text (`Archive.text`) rather than carrying old PNGs forward.
- **Missing/stale:** `snapcompact.systemPrompt` (`agents-md`/`all`) and `snapcompact.toolResults` options for transient imaging of the system prompt and large tool results.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: maintenance progress guard can rescue by re-rendering the archive with a smaller frame budget. Billing helpers `frameBilling`/`frameTokens` were added.

- B `chapters-33-46.ts:1005` 「압축률만 높이고 실제 프로젝트의 언어를 읽지 못하면 컨텍스트 보존이라는 목적을 달성하지 못하기 때문입니다.」 — A generic justification. The useful fact is omitted: when CJK dominates the text, `resolveShapeForText` switches the whole frame to `silver16-bw`. ⇒ Replace with '한글이 대부분인 대화는 전체 프레임을 `silver16-bw` 격자로 그립니다.'
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:1009` 「평가가 좋은 기본 `shape`도 특정 대화의 정답을 보장하지는 않습니다. … 비트맵은 의미 요약의 대체물이 아니라 오래된 원문을 다른 매체로 운반하는 선택입니다.」 — A disclaimer plus grandiose closing. Advice to 'limit frames and budgets' is stated as a should, although omp already does it. ⇒ Replace with mechanisms: '프레임 수는 최대 80장이고 남은 문맥, 제공자별 이미지 수, 요청당 3 MB 데이터 한도로 다시 줄어듭니다. 가운데가 길면 양 끝은 선명한 프레임, 가운데는 조밀한 프레임으로 그리고 가장 오래된 조밀 구간부터 버립니다.'
- I tool-sequence L954-967 (오래된 메시지가 다음 요청의 이미지 문맥이 되기까지) — Wrong order and actor: in `snapcompact.ts`, `compact()` serializes first and then `planArchive` splits the text into edges and middle, both inside SnapCompact. The diagram has the pipeline split before serialization. There is no concrete content (sizes, frame count), and the last event ('비전 모델이 … 현재 작업을 계속함') shows no consequence. The relationship worth showing, what occupies the next request, is spatial, not sequential. ⇒ Replace with a static occupancy diagram of the rebuilt request on a fixed token scale, with illustrative labelled numbers: lead-in + FILES | oldest text edge (1 page) | N image frames (≈ tokens per frame per provider) | newest text edge (1 page) | kept recent messages. Optionally put the raw-text equivalent alongside. If a sequence is kept: serialize → split → render, all in SnapCompact.
- I exchange L994-1002 (`shape` 후보 → 읽기 평가 / 비용 평가 / 선택 결과) — Prose dressed as a diagram: the input lists trade-offs, and the outputs are evaluation steps, not results derived from the input. ⇒ Use a static table from the README: provider → default shape → reason (Anthropic `11on16-bw`, 1932px for high-res lines; Google `8on22-bw`@2048 because Gemini bills a fixed per-image budget; OpenAI `8on22-bw` at `detail: "original"`). Add one row for Claude routed via Vertex (keeps the Claude shape, priced for the gateway).
- R [tool-sequence ✗] TS ~L955 오래된 메시지가 다음 요청의 이미지 문맥이 되기까지 (6 steps; 922px / 865px) — The key artifact, a rendered frame, is never shown. Step 6 draws the vision model 'answering' the pipeline. Steps 1 and 3 are self `read`s. ⇒ Use a bespoke static figure: a source text excerpt next to the PNG frame it becomes. Cut the TS to 4 steps or drop it.
- R [exchange ✗] EX ~L995 shape 후보 → 읽기 평가 / 비용 평가 / 선택 결과 (3 outputs; 452px / 634px) — A sequential experiment drawn as a fan-out. ⇒ Use a table: shape × EM/F1 × cost (with real values if published), or a numbered list.

## 44 실시간 협업

- **Verdict:** demote-to-section → ch39 SDK, RPC, ACP 인터페이스 (as a fourth host boundary: a remote guest over a relay controlling the same AgentSession). The distinct harness mechanism is another host interface: a command channel (prompt/abort/agent-cmd/ui-response) and an event stream (entry/event/state/bus/agents) over the same session runtime, with a capability check. That is the same kind of concern as RPC and ACP in ch39, which already frames itself as 'boundaries that control the same session runtime'. Crypto parameters and link grammar are product detail.
- **Overall:** Clear and mostly accurate on host authority, encrypted relay, and the read-only vs full-control split. The harness lessons are real but small: replicate events, not pixels; durable entries are stored but only events are rendered; session control is separate from host-machine control. Link-format trivia pads the chapter, and the paragraph after the diagram re-lists its frame types. Mostly a description of an omp feature.
- **Organization:** Section 1 (what is replicated) and section 2 (who may control) are the right questions. L1071's link grammar (terminal `.<key>`, legacy `#<key>`) answers no question in the chapter. The Agent Hub/ui-request paragraph (L1101) belongs with section 2's execution-path, where it sits.
- **Ordering:** Fine. It relies on ch24 (session entries/JSONL), ch38 (event-driven TUI rendering), and ch37 (Agent Hub) without cross-references; a guest's replica uses the regular `/resume` machinery, which ties directly to ch24.
- **Missing/stale:** Mid-turn join: the guest synthesizes `message_start` from the first subsequent `message_update`. If the host emits no further update, the live component can be absent. This shows the limit of events-only rendering.
- **Missing/stale:** Guest replica session at `~/.omp/collab/<roomId>.jsonl`, loaded through the regular `/resume` machinery (ties to ch24). Guests keep a local command allowlist (`/dump`, `/export`, `/theme` …).
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: `collab.autoStart` (`view`/`control` hosts every interactive session at start), `/collab list`, view-only room replacement on `/collab`, advisor records excluded from guest-visible subagents, transcript fetches capped at 4 MiB per reply.

- P `chapters-33-46.ts:1028 (description)` 「호스트의 한 세션을 암호화된 이벤트로 복제하고 원격 사용자의 관찰·질문·중단을 되돌려 보내는 구조.」 — Illogical: observation is not sent back. Ends in the abstraction '구조'. ⇒ 호스트에서 실행 중인 세션을 암호화된 이벤트로 다른 사용자에게 복제하고, 게스트의 프롬프트와 중단 요청을 호스트로 전달하는 기능.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:1036` 「`/collab`에서 호스트 세션은 모델과 모든 도구 실행의 정본으로 남습니다. … 따라서 화면 크기와 테마가 달라도 실행 의미는 같습니다.」 — '모델과 모든 도구 실행의 정본' is an awkward literal of 'authoritative'. '실행 의미' is vague. ⇒ `/collab`으로 세션을 공유해도 모델 호출과 도구 실행은 모두 호스트 머신에서 일어납니다. … 그래서 게스트는 자기 터미널 크기와 테마로 같은 대화와 도구 카드를 봅니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:1053` 「정본 기록과 일시적인 화면 이벤트를 분리해야 재접속 뒤에도 대화는 복원되고, 실시간 카드가 중복 표시되지 않습니다.」 — An abstract should-statement. The actual mechanism (docs/collab.md: entries appended to a replica, rendering is events-only to prevent double-render) is clearer. ⇒ 게스트는 `entry`를 복제 세션 파일에 저장만 하고 화면은 `event`로만 그립니다. 그래서 같은 메시지가 두 번 그려지지 않고, 게스트 쪽에서도 `/dump`와 문맥 사용량 계산이 동작합니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:1032 vs 1067 (section titles)` 「호스트가 실행하고 게스트는 실행 기록을 재구성한다 / 읽기 권한과 제어 권한을 링크에서 나누기」 — Mixed title forms (declarative sentence vs ~하기) within one chapter. ⇒ 호스트의 실행을 게스트 화면에 재구성하기
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:1071` 「터미널용 링크는 `.<key>`가 붙은 직접 릴레이 형식과 레거시 `#<key>` 형식도 허용합니다.」 — Name-dropping link grammar that answers no question in the chapter. ⇒ Delete. Add the actual trust rule from docs/collab.md: '링크 자체가 자격 증명이므로 비밀처럼 공유해야 합니다.'
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:1053` 「이후 `entry`는 영속 대화, `event`는 스트리밍 변화, `state`는 모델·문맥·참가자, `bus`와 `agents`는 하위 에이전트 진행을 전달합니다.」 — Re-narrates frame labels already shown in the tool-sequence's event 4. ⇒ Delete and keep only the causal sentence (see the prose rewrite above).

Minor: in event 1, the 4-byte routing prefix in the source is described for frames that target a guest (host→guest). Say '방 키로 봉인한 프레임' only, or verify the guest→host direction.

One inaccuracy: the full-control path's first stage says the host verifies the write token per action. The source says the token is verified at join, and writes from peers without it are rejected. ⇒ Change stage 1 to '참가 확인: 참가할 때 검증된 쓰기 토큰이 있는 참가자'.
- R [tool-sequence ✓] TS ~L1039 원격 질문이 호스트의 새 턴이 되기까지 (5 steps; 762px / 698px) — Step 3 collapses the whole host execution into one self `read`. '게스트 재현' uses `answer`. '불투명 전달' is a literal calque. ⇒ Keep. Relabel '암호문 그대로 전달' and fix the kinds.
- R [execution-path ✓] EP ~L1074 게스트 행동을 권한에 따라 처리하기 (2 tabs × 3; 603px / 693px) — A real alternative. ⇒ Keep.

## 45 데스크톱 자동화

- **Verdict:** merge → ch19 브라우저 자동화 → one chapter on GUI automation (브라우저와 데스크톱). Both chapters answer the same question: how an agent acts on a live GUI it observes only through snapshots. Both use an Eval-hosted worker, a semantic tree (DOM/ARIA vs OS AX) preferred over screenshot pixels, grounding actions in the latest observation, and screen text treated as untrusted. ch19 already has an execution-path of the same shape (DOM·접근성 vs 스크린샷). The desktop-specific mechanisms (coordinate frames and scaling, background input, platform capabilities, approval classification by method) fit in one section.
- **Overall:** Good core example (AX element vs screenshot coordinates) and an accurate statement that `read_only` is not a sandbox. But it repeats ch19's structure almost exactly. It leaves the term '프리루드' undefined. It misses the most harness-specific desktop concern, sharing the desktop with a live user (background input delivery vs takeover). Two lifecycle claims are stale at HEAD.
- **Organization:** Section 1 (how to target) and section 2 (lifecycle, permissions, safety) are reasonable. The capability paragraph (L1187) and the untrusted-text paragraph (L1191) are generic; the latter duplicates ch02 L327 and ch19.
- **Ordering:** L1127 introduces `computer` as a '프리루드' without definition. ch14 (Python/JS execution) and ch19 call the browser API a '파사드'. It should reuse ch14/ch19's term and cross-reference them.
- **Missing/stale:** Shared desktop with a live user: input defaults to background delivery that avoids moving the user's focus, pointer, or window order. Unsupported routes throw `BackgroundUnavailable`, with explicit foreground delivery or takeover as the fallback. This is the core desktop-harness concern and it is absent.
- **Missing/stale:** Screenshot scaling to model-transport caps (effective 1280×896 for some transports and as a Claude-family fallback), with coordinates in the scaled frame. This is why the harness, not the model, owns coordinate mapping.
- **Missing/stale:** AX reference generations: only the current and previous snapshot refs remain valid; refresh after `StaleRef`.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD (L1174): '호출은 한 번에 하나씩 실행됩니다' — the worker now accepts one active run and overlapping calls fail with `Computer worker is busy` instead of queueing.
- **Missing/stale:** Stale at HEAD (L1174): '중단으로 워커가 종료되면' — cancellation now retires the run's native work without discarding the desktop session. Only an unresponsive worker (timeout + 750 ms grace) or a crash resets it.
- **Missing/stale:** HEAD: native mutations take an OS-backed cross-process input/focus lock (`InputBusy`). `computer.control.acquire({ reason })` requires live human confirmation for deliberate foreground control. `computer.display` default changed `all`→`active`. `delivery` option renamed to `takeover`.

- P `chapters-33-46.ts:1119 (description)` 「화면 캡처, 운영체제 접근성 트리, 포인터·키보드 입력을 실제 데스크톱 세션에 연결하는 도구.」 — '연결하는 도구' is vague. The sentence lists inputs rather than saying what the feature does. ⇒ 스크린샷과 운영체제 접근성 트리로 데스크톱 앱을 관찰하고, 접근성 동작이나 포인터·키보드 입력으로 조작하는 Eval 기능.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:1123 (section title)` 「대상을 찾고 의미 있는 조작을 선택하기」 — '의미 있는 조작' is a literal of 'semantic action' and reads as 'meaningful action'. ⇒ 접근성 요소로 조작하고, 없으면 좌표로 입력하기
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:1127` 「OMP의 `computer`는 독립된 모델 도구가 아니라 Python·JavaScript Eval에 노출되는 프리루드입니다.」 — '프리루드' is undefined jargon, inconsistent with ch19's '파사드'. ⇒ OMP의 `computer`는 별도 모델 도구가 아니라, Python·JavaScript Eval 셀에서 바로 쓸 수 있도록 미리 정의된 전역 객체입니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:1157` 「픽셀 입력은 반드시 같은 대상의 가장 최근 스크린샷 좌표를 사용해야 하며」 — Phrased as advice, but omp enforces it (docs/computer-use.md: coordinate input before that capture is rejected). The actor is missing. ⇒ OMP는 같은 대상의 가장 최근 스크린샷을 좌표 기준으로 삼고, 그보다 앞선 기준의 입력은 거부합니다. 창 크기나 디스플레이 배치가 바뀌면 그 캡처도 무효가 됩니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:1187` 「코드는 플랫폼 이름만 보고 기능을 가정하지 말고 `computer.capabilities()`에서 실제 지원 여부와 전달 방식을 확인해야 합니다.」 — Negative-first advice. ⇒ 실제 지원 여부와 입력 전달 방식은 `computer.capabilities()`가 알려 줍니다.
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:1191` 「화면과 접근성 트리의 문구는 외부 애플리케이션이 제공하는 신뢰할 수 없는 데이터입니다. 그 문구가 비밀 전송이나 승인 우회를 요구해도 권한으로 취급하지 않습니다.」 — Restates ch02's prompt-injection paragraph and ch19's equivalent. The concrete harness mechanism (approval classification, computer safety system prompt) is left implicit. ⇒ Cut to one sentence with a cross-reference. Keep the consequential-action confirmation rule, attributed to the system prompt.
- I execution-path L1129-1154 (설정 창의 저장 버튼을 누르는 두 방법) — Valid: given state, branching observation, code, and consequence. Path 2's result ('최신 캡처에 속한 좌표만 사용합니다') is a rule, not an outcome. Stage '좌표 확인' hides the actor (the model reads the image and picks coordinates). ⇒ Stage: '모델이 캡처 이미지에서 버튼 중심 좌표를 고릅니다.' Result: '배치가 바뀌면 다시 캡처해야 하지만, AX를 제공하지 않는 앱도 조작할 수 있습니다.' Consider a third outcome at HEAD: background input refused (`BackgroundUnavailable`) → `{ takeover: true }`.
- I exchange L1176-1184 (호출 종류 → 읽기 호출 / 변경 호출 / `computer.run`) — Acceptable fan-out (one call type classified three ways). The third output carries the chapter's most important caveat. ⇒ Keep. Optionally make it a two-column static table (method → approval class), since nothing is transformed.
- R [execution-path ✓] EP ~L1130 설정 창의 저장 버튼을 누르는 두 방법 (2 tabs × 3; 605px / 696px) — A real fallback path. ⇒ Keep.
- R [exchange ✗] EX ~L1177 호출 종류 → 읽기 호출 / 변경 호출 / computer.run (3 outputs; 479px / 660px) — A taxonomy. ⇒ Use a table: 호출 / 승인 등급 / 허용 범위.

## 46 GitHub 자동화 서비스

- **Verdict:** keep. Distinct mechanism, and plainly harness engineering: external event intake with deduplication, durable queueing, crash recovery, per-issue workspace and session persistence, capability-limited host tools with gates and audit, credential isolation, and completion enforcement. None of this is covered elsewhere.
- **Overall:** The best case study in this range of building a harness around omp for unattended operation: idempotent webhook intake, per-issue serialization, resumable sessions, narrow write tools with pre-publish gates, and a credential sidecar. Weaknesses: the tool-sequence omits both the agent's actual work and the `gh-proxy` actor, which contradicts section 2; it relies on RPC 'host tools' that ch39 never introduces; and it misses agent-environment scrubbing, the repro requirement, and terminal-tool completion reminders, which are the mechanisms that make the boundaries real.
- **Organization:** Section 1 (intake → session) and section 2 (credential and write boundary) are the right split. The final paragraph's framing (OMP = execution engine, RoboOMP = harness) is the chapter's best sentence and could open the chapter. The `gh_open_pr` parameter list appears twice (L1229 and L1265).
- **Ordering:** The sequence in section 1 shows RoboOMP pushing directly to GitHub before section 2 explains that RoboOMP has no PAT and `gh-proxy` does the push. `gh_open_pr`/`skip_checks` appear before the gates are explained. 'Host tool' is used as known vocabulary, but ch39 never introduces RPC host tools (`set_host_tools`, `host_tool_call`/`host_tool_result` in docs/rpc.md). Either add that to ch39 or set it up here in one sentence.
- **Missing/stale:** Agent subprocess environment scrubbing (`worker._SCRUBBED_ENV_KEYS`: `GITHUB_TOKEN`, `ROBOMP_GH_PROXY_HMAC_KEY` …). This makes the credential boundary hold even though the agent has a shell.
- **Missing/stale:** 'No PR without a recorded repro': `repro_record` is required. `mark_unable_to_reproduce` marks the row `needs_info` and resumes the same session on the next reply.
- **Missing/stale:** Completion enforcement: terminal tools per task type (`gh_open_pr`/`mark_unable_to_reproduce`/`abort_task`; `submit_pr_review`) and completion reminders when a turn ends before reaching one (`_needs_completion_reminder`, `task_completion_max_reminders`). At HEAD this is detected via `HostToolCompletedEvent` across all dispatch paths, including eval.
- **Missing/stale:** One PR per issue: follow-up events push amendments to the same `farm/<hex>/<slug>` branch. Bad signature → `401`, never `5xx`. Inspection endpoints are localhost-only.
- **Missing/stale:** Prerequisite in ch39: RPC host tools (`set_host_tools`, `host_tool_call`/`host_tool_cancel`/`host_tool_update`/`host_tool_result` in docs/rpc.md) are never introduced, but ch46 depends on them.
- **Missing/stale:** Release sentinel (default off) can reasonably stay omitted. HEAD changes to `python/robomp` are minor (worker completion detection).

- P `chapters-33-46.ts:1210 (description)` 「웹훅을 내구성 있는 작업으로 바꾸고 이슈별 OMP 세션과 제한된 GitHub 쓰기를 조정하는 서비스.」 — '내구성 있는 작업' is a literal of 'durable job'. '조정하는' is vague. ⇒ GitHub 이벤트를 큐에 저장하고 이슈마다 OMP RPC 세션을 이어 실행하며, GitHub 쓰기는 검사를 거친 몇 가지 연산으로만 허용하는 자동화 서비스.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:1218` 「HTTP 요청 안에서 에이전트를 끝까지 실행하지 않으므로 GitHub에는 빠르게 응답하고, 워커가 내구성 있는 큐에서 작업을 가져갑니다.」 — Negative framing plus the literal '내구성 있는 큐'. ⇒ 웹훅 요청에는 이벤트를 저장한 직후 `202`로 응답하고, 에이전트 실행은 워커가 큐에서 꺼내 따로 진행합니다. 서명이 틀리면 `401`을 돌려 GitHub가 재전송하지 않게 합니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:1238` 「질문·제안·중복 이슈는 분류에 맞는 댓글 흐름을 택합니다.」 — Abstract '흐름'. ⇒ 질문·제안·중복 이슈에는 PR 없이 댓글만 남깁니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:1242` 「…함께 있어야 네트워크 재전송이나 프로세스 장애가 중복 PR과 잃어버린 진행으로 이어지지 않습니다.」 — Noun-heavy. '잃어버린 진행' is unnatural. ⇒ 같은 웹훅이 다시 와도 전달 ID로 걸러지고, 같은 이슈의 이벤트는 한 번에 하나만 처리되며, 재시작 뒤에는 저장된 세션을 이어 가므로 PR이 중복되거나 작업이 처음부터 다시 시작되지 않습니다.
- P `chapters-33-46.ts:1274` 「게시 전 검사에 실패하면 오류가 RPC 세션으로 돌아가 모델이 수정하게 됩니다.」 — '~하게 됩니다' is 번역투. ⇒ 게시 전 검사가 실패하면 오류가 도구 결과로 돌아가고, 모델이 고친 뒤 다시 호출합니다.
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:1278` 「GitHub 자동화가 신뢰할 수 있으려면 모델의 작업 능력뿐 아니라 중복 전달, 장애 복구, 자격 증명 보관, 감사 기록, 게시 전 검사를 하나의 실행 계약으로 다뤄야 합니다.」 — A generic closing list restating the chapter. '하나의 실행 계약' is grandiose. ⇒ Delete. Keep the first sentence (OMP = 실행 엔진, RoboOMP = 하네스).
- B `chapters-33-46.ts:1274` 「모델에게 PAT나 범용 GitHub 셸을 주는 대신 좁은 연산과 검증 결과만 제공하는 구조입니다.」 — Overstated: the agent does have `bash` in the worktree. The real guarantee is that its process environment is scrubbed of `GITHUB_TOKEN`/HMAC keys and the PAT lives only in `gh-proxy`. ⇒ '에이전트는 작업 트리 안에서 `bash`를 쓸 수 있지만, 그 프로세스 환경에서는 `GITHUB_TOKEN`과 프록시 서명 키가 지워져 있어 GitHub 쓰기는 호스트 도구를 거쳐야만 가능합니다.'
- I tool-sequence L1220-1235 (버그 이슈 하나가 수정 PR이 되기까지) — (1) It skips the agent's actual work: no classify, repro, fix, or test steps between '이슈 작업 시작' and `gh_open_pr`. (2) It omits `gh-proxy` and shows RoboOMP pushing to GitHub directly, contradicting L1261. (3) The last event, a new webhook, is drawn as an `answer` from GitHub, which is the wrong kind and duplicates L1238. (4) The parameter list repeats the exchange input. ⇒ Use 7 events: webhook → queue (self) → start RPC session → `host_tool_call repro_record` → (agent edits/tests, self) → `host_tool_call gh_open_pr` → RoboOMP self: `bun run fix`·`check`·`test` → via `gh-proxy` push + PR → `host_tool_result` with PR URL. Either add `gh-proxy` as an actor or put 'gh-proxy를 거쳐' in the detail. Drop the follow-up event.
- I exchange L1263-1271 (에이전트의 GitHub 요청 → 호스트 도구 / 게시 전 게이트 / gh-proxy) — Layers of one path drawn as a fan-out. Output 2 conflates `gh_push_branch` gates (branch, author, clean tree) with `gh_open_pr` gates (fix → check → full test, 1 h). ⇒ Fold into the corrected tool-sequence, or make it a static three-row list and attribute the gates correctly. Add that a `skip_checks=true` bypass is recorded in `tool_calls`.
- R [tool-sequence ✓] TS ~L1221 버그 이슈 하나가 수정 PR이 되기까지 (8 steps; 1139px / 1034px) — Step 8 '후속 이벤트' is a webhook drawn as an `answer` from GitHub, and it starts a new cycle. ⇒ End at step 7 and mention the follow-up in prose. Use `call`/`request` for webhooks.
- R [exchange ✗] EX ~L1264 에이전트의 GitHub 요청 → 호스트 도구 / 게시 전 게이트 / gh-proxy (3 outputs; 555px / 788px) — Sequential layers drawn as parallel. Overlaps TS steps 4–5. ⇒ Use a numbered flow that marks where the PAT first appears.

### Range patterns (ch 40–46)

- Closing generic/defensive sentences that restate the chapter or hedge without a decision: 9 instances across the seven chapters. Examples: L766 '원인을 자동으로 증명하지는 않습니다… 드러내는 것입니다', L846 '둘 중 하나만으로는 충분하지 않습니다', L921 '섞지 않는 것이 핵심입니다', L1009 '정답을 보장하지는 않습니다… 운반하는 선택입니다', L1278 '하나의 실행 계약으로 다뤄야 합니다'. Replace each with the concrete mechanism omp implements, or delete.
- Contrast against an alternative no one proposed (X 대신 / X가 아니라 / X하지 않습니다): 6 instances. Examples: L702 '대화 화면을 다시 긁는 대신', L842 '“수정 완료” 문장을 채점하지 않습니다', L974 '화면에 이미지를 한 번 보여 주는 기능이 아니라', L1218 'HTTP 요청 안에서 … 끝까지 실행하지 않으므로'. State the positive rule.
- `exchange` blocks used for pipelines or sequential steps instead of one input fanned to parallel outputs: 5 of the 6 exchanges here (L705 output 3, L794, L875, L994, L1263). Convert to static tables or fold into tool-sequences. Only L1176 fits the block's contract.
- Paragraphs re-narrating diagram labels: L1053 (frame list repeated from the sequence), L842+L846 (repeat the verdict events), L1229/L1265 (same parameter list twice).
- Descriptions as noun-stacked definitions ending in abstract heads ('관측 계층', '실험 시스템', 'N-API 경계', '컨텍스트 보존 방식', '구조', '도구', '조정하는 서비스'): all 7 chapters. Lead with what the component does and for whom.
- Literal calques and abstract nouns where nothing literal is meant: '내구성 있는 작업/큐' (L1210, L1218), '정본' (L1036, L1053), '실행 의미' (L1036), '지점' (L842), '흐름' (L1238), '잃어버린 진행' (L1242), '의미 있는 조작' (L1123), '모양' for shape (L988), '~하게 됩니다' (L1274).
- Hidden actors in interactive steps: '좌표 확인' (L1148, who picks coordinates?), '구간 분리' attributed to the pipeline instead of SnapCompact (L960), '형식 정규화' drawn as a message rather than a verifier self-step (L836).
- Facts stale at HEAD 3f000c52: ch42 sentinel → release stamp, lazy entrypoints, internal-URL materialization removed; ch45 call serialization → busy rejection, abort no longer resets the worker; ch40 'completed sessions' vs live watcher; ch40 `GET /api/sync` → `POST`.
- Cross-chapter: ch45 duplicates ch19's structure (semantic tree vs screenshot execution-path, live GUI state, untrusted page/screen text) and uses an undefined term ('프리루드') for what ch19 calls the Eval `browser` '파사드'. Merge into one GUI-automation chapter with shared terminology.
- Cross-chapter: ch43 is a compaction strategy, but ch25 mentions it in one sentence (chapters-20-32.ts:827), and ch43 never says it is second in the default `compaction.methodOrder` or that it needs an image-capable model. Add the method-order/vision gate to ch43 and a forward link from ch25. Ideally place ch43 adjacent to ch25 rather than under 'Specialized components'.
- Cross-chapter: ch46 depends on RPC host tools, which ch39 omits. Add a short host-tool subsection to ch39 (`set_host_tools`, `host_tool_call`/`host_tool_result`) or a one-sentence setup in ch46.
- Cross-chapter: ch44 belongs with ch39's host interfaces (the same AgentSession controlled by another party over another transport). It also leans on ch24 (replica JSONL via `/resume` machinery), ch38 (events-only rendering), and ch37 (Agent Hub) without cross-references.
- Cross-chapter: ch40 and ch41 answer one question (did a harness change help, at what cost?) and should be one chapter. ch40's cache diagnosis should point back to ch01/ch02's caching rule (messages-and-tools.ts:331) instead of implying it.
- Cross-chapter: ch42's grep trace overlaps ch09 (file reading and search), and its crate paragraph overlaps ch03's package map (coding-agents-and-omp.ts:189-196). Move the boundary point into ch09 and drop the rest.
- Cross-chapter: Credential isolation recurs in ch23 (credentials), ch41 (benchmark auth gateway, missing), and ch46 (`gh-proxy`, env scrubbing). Cross-reference so ch46/ch41 show applications of ch23's principle.
- Cross-chapter: Prompt-injection / untrusted-content guidance is repeated in ch02 (messages-and-tools.ts:327), ch19, and ch45 (L1191). Keep the definition in ch02 and use one-line cross-references elsewhere.
- Cross-chapter: Category placement: ch40/41 sit under 'Interfaces and supporting infrastructure' next to TUI and SDK, although they are about evaluation. ch43 sits under 'Specialized components' although it is a default compaction path.
